# 中华人民共和国监察法实施条例

**分类**：监察法规  
**法律部门**：宪法相关法  
**公布日期**：2025-06-01  
**生效日期**：2025-06-01

> （2021年7月20日国家监察委员会全体会议决定　2021年9月20日国家监察委员会公告第1号公布　2025年4月27日国家监察委员会全体会议修订　2025年6月1日国家监察委员会公告第2号公布） 目　　录 第一章　总　　则 第二章　监察机关及其职责 第三章　监察范围和管辖 第四章　监察权限 第五章　监察程序 第六章　反腐败国际合作 第七章　对监察机关和监察人员的监督 第八章　法律责任 第九章　附　　则 第一章　总　　则 第一条　为了推动监察工作法治化、规范化，保障依法公正行使监察权，根据《中华人民共和国监察法》（以下简称监察法），结合工作实际，制定本条例。 第二条　坚持中国共产党对监察工作的全面领导，增强政治意识、大局意识、核心意识、看齐意识，坚定中国特色社会主义道路自信、理论自信、制度自信、文化自信，坚决维护习近平总书记党中央的核心、全党的核心地位，坚决维护以习近平同志为核心的党中央权威和集中统一领导，把党的领导贯彻到监察工作各方面和全过程。 第三条　监察机关与党的纪律检查机关合署办公，坚持法治思维和法治方式，促进执纪执法贯通、有效衔接司法，实现依纪监督和依法监察、适用纪律和适用法律有机融合。

---

### 第一章　总则

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　为了推动监察工作法治化、规范化，保障依法公正行使监察权，根据《中华人民共和国监察法》（以下简称监察法），结合工作实际，制定本条例。

**Article 1** These Regulations are formulated in accordance with the Supervision Law of the People's Republic of China (hereinafter referred to as the Supervision Law), in light of the actual work, for the purposes of promoting the law-based and standardized supervision work and ensuring the lawful and impartial exercise of supervisory power.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　坚持中国共产党对监察工作的全面领导，增强政治意识、大局意识、核心意识、看齐意识，坚定中国特色社会主义道路自信、理论自信、制度自信、文化自信，坚决维护习近平总书记党中央的核心、全党的核心地位，坚决维护以习近平同志为核心的党中央权威和集中统一领导，把党的领导贯彻到监察工作各方面和全过程。

**Article 2** The overall leadership of the Communist Party of China over supervision work shall be upheld, political awareness, awareness of the overall situation, core awareness, and alignment awareness shall be enhanced, confidence in the path, theory, system, and culture of socialism with Chinese characteristics shall be strengthened, and the position of General Secretary Xi Jinping as the core of the Central Committee and the whole Party shall be resolutely maintained, and the authority of the Central Committee with Comrade Xi Jinping as its core and its centralized and unified leadership shall be resolutely upheld, and the Party's leadership shall be implemented in all aspects and the entire process of supervision work.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　监察机关与党的纪律检查机关合署办公，坚持法治思维和法治方式，促进执纪执法贯通、有效衔接司法，实现依纪监督和依法监察、适用纪律和适用法律有机融合。

**Article 3** A supervisory organ shall work jointly with a Party disciplinary inspection organ in the same office, uphold law-based thinking and law-based methods, promote the integration of discipline enforcement and law enforcement and effective connection with the judiciary, and realize the organic integration of supervision in accordance with Party discipline and supervision in accordance with the law, and the application of discipline and law.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　监察机关应当依法履行监督、调查、处置职责，坚持实事求是，坚持惩前毖后、治病救人，坚持惩戒与教育相结合，实现政治效果、法律效果和社会效果相统一。

**Article 4** A supervisory organ shall, according to law, perform its duties of supervision, investigation, and disposition, adhere to seeking truth from facts, adhere to the principle of learning from past mistakes to avoid future ones and curing the sickness to save the patient, and adhere to combining punishment with education, so as to achieve the unity of political effects, legal effects, and social effects.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　监察机关应当坚定不移惩治腐败，推动深化改革、完善制度，规范权力运行，加强新时代廉洁文化建设，引导公职人员提高觉悟、担当作为、依法履职，一体推进不敢腐、不能腐、不想腐，着力铲除腐败滋生的土壤和条件。

**Article 5** A supervisory organ shall unswervingly punish corruption, promote deepening reforms and improving systems, standardize the exercise of power, strengthen the construction of a clean culture in the new era, guide public officials to raise their awareness, take on responsibilities, perform duties according to law, and jointly promote the mechanisms that ensure that they dare not, cannot, and do not want to be corrupt, and strive to eradicate the soil and conditions for corruption.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　监察机关坚持民主集中制，对于线索处置、立案调查、案件审理、处置执行、复审复核中的重要事项应当集体研究，严格按照权限履行请示报告程序。

**Article 6** A supervisory organ shall adhere to the principle of democratic centralism, and shall conduct collective study on important matters concerning clue handling, case filing and investigation, case review, disposition enforcement, and review and re-examination, and strictly follow the procedures for requesting instructions and reporting in accordance with the limits of authority.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　监察机关应当尊重和保障人权，在适用法律上一律平等，充分保障监察对象以及相关人员的人身权、知情权、财产权、申辩权、申诉权以及申请复审复核权等合法权益。

**Article 7** A supervisory organ shall respect and protect human rights, apply the law equally to all, and fully protect the lawful rights and interests of the persons subject to supervision and relevant persons, including the right to personal inviolability, the right to be informed, property rights, the right to defense, the right to petition, and the right to apply for review and re-examination.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　监察机关办理职务犯罪案件，应当与人民法院、人民检察院互相配合、互相制约，在案件管辖、证据审查、案件移送、涉案财物处置等方面加强沟通协调，对于人民法院、人民检察院提出的退回补充调查、排除非法证据、调取同步录音录像、要求调查人员出庭等意见依法办理。

**Article 8** When handling duty crime cases, a supervisory organ shall cooperate with and mutually check each other with the People's Court and the People's Procuratorate, strengthen communication and coordination in aspects such as case jurisdiction, evidence review, case referral, and disposal of property involved in the case, and handle in accordance with law the opinions raised by the People's Court and the People's Procuratorate on returning the case for supplementary investigation, excluding illegally obtained evidence, retrieving synchronized audio and video recordings, and requiring investigators to appear in court.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　监察机关开展监察工作，可以依法提请组织人事、公安、国家安全、移民管理、审计、统计、市场监管、金融监管、财政、税务、自然资源、银行、证券、保险等有关部门、单位予以协助配合。  
有关部门、单位应当根据监察机关的要求，依法协助采取有关措施、共享相关信息、提供相关资料和专业技术支持，配合开展监察工作。

**Article 9** When carrying out supervision work, a supervisory organ may, according to law, request assistance and cooperation from relevant departments and units such as organization and personnel, public security, state security, immigration administration, audit, statistics, market regulation, financial regulation, finance, taxation, natural resources, banking, securities, and insurance.  
The relevant departments and units shall, in accordance with the requirements of the supervisory organ, assist in taking relevant measures, sharing relevant information, providing relevant materials and professional technical support, and cooperate in carrying out supervision work according to law.

### 第二章　监察机关及其职责

#### 第一节　领导体制

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　国家监察委员会在党中央领导下开展工作。地方各级监察委员会在同级党委和上级监察委员会双重领导下工作，监督执法调查工作以上级监察委员会领导为主，线索处置和案件查办在向同级党委报告的同时应当一并向上一级监察委员会报告。  
上级监察委员会应当加强对下级监察委员会的领导。下级监察委员会对上级监察委员会的决定必须执行，认为决定不当的，应当在执行的同时向上级监察委员会反映。上级监察委员会对下级监察委员会作出的错误决定，应当按程序予以纠正，或者要求下级监察委员会予以纠正。

**Article 10** The National Commission of Supervision shall carry out work under the leadership of the Central Committee of the Communist Party of China. Local supervisory commissions at all levels shall work under the dual leadership of the Party committee at the same level and the supervisory commission at the next higher level, with supervision, law enforcement, and investigation work primarily under the leadership of the supervisory commission at the next higher level; clue handling and case investigation shall be reported to the supervisory commission at the next higher level at the same time as being reported to the Party committee at the same level.  
The supervisory commission at the higher level shall strengthen leadership over the supervisory commission at the lower level. The supervisory commission at the lower level must implement the decisions of the supervisory commission at the next higher level; if it considers a decision inappropriate, it shall, while implementing it, reflect its views to the supervisory commission at the next higher level. The supervisory commission at the higher level shall correct any erroneous decision made by the supervisory commission at the lower level in accordance with procedures, or require the supervisory commission at the lower level to make corrections.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　上级监察委员会可以依法统一调用所辖各级监察机关的监察人员办理监察事项。调用决定应当以书面形式作出。  
监察机关办理监察事项应当加强互相协作和配合，对于重要、复杂事项可以提请上级监察机关予以协调。

**Article 11** The supervisory commission at the higher level may, according to law, uniformly deploy supervisory personnel of the supervisory organs at various levels under its jurisdiction to handle supervisory matters. The deployment decision shall be made in writing.  
Supervisory organs shall strengthen mutual cooperation and coordination when handling supervisory matters, and may request the supervisory organ at the higher level to coordinate important or complex matters.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　各级监察委员会依法向本级中国共产党机关、国家机关、中国人民政治协商会议委员会机关、法律法规授权或者受委托管理公共事务的组织和单位以及所管辖的国有企业、事业单位等派驻或者派出监察机构、监察专员。  
省级和设区的市级监察委员会依法向地区、盟、开发区等不设置人民代表大会的区域派出监察机构或者监察专员。县级监察委员会和直辖市所辖区（县）监察委员会可以向街道、乡镇等区域派出监察机构或者监察专员。  
监察机构、监察专员开展监察工作，受派出机关领导。

**Article 12** Supervisory commissions at all levels shall, according to law, station or dispatch supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners to the organs of the Communist Party of China, state organs, organs of the Chinese People's Political Consultative Conference, organizations and units authorized by laws and regulations or entrusted to administer public affairs, as well as state-owned enterprises and public institutions under their jurisdiction.  
Provincial and city supervisory commissions divided into districts shall, according to law, dispatch supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners to prefectures, leagues, development zones, and other areas where people's congresses are not established. County supervisory commissions and supervisory commissions of districts (counties) under municipalities directly under the Central Government may dispatch supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners to subdistricts, townships, and other areas.  
Supervisory institutions and supervisory commissioners shall carry out supervisory work under the leadership of the dispatching organ.

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　各级监察委员会派驻或者派出的监察机构、监察专员根据派出机关授权，按照管理权限依法对派驻或者派出监督单位、区域等的公职人员开展监督，对职务违法和职务犯罪进行调查、处置。监察机构、监察专员可以按规定与地方监察委员会联合调查严重职务违法、职务犯罪，或者移交地方监察委员会调查。  
前款规定的监察机构、监察专员未被授予职务犯罪调查权的，其发现监察对象涉嫌职务犯罪线索，应当及时向派出机关报告，由派出机关调查或者依法移交有关地方监察委员会调查。

**Article 13** The supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners stationed or dispatched by supervisory commissions at all levels shall, according to the authorization of the dispatching organ and within the scope of their management authority, conduct supervision over public officials of the units or areas under their supervision according to law, and investigate and handle duty-related violations and duty-related crimes. Supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners may, as prescribed, jointly investigate serious duty-related violations and duty-related crimes with local supervisory commissions, or transfer them to local supervisory commissions for investigation.  
Where the supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners as prescribed in the preceding paragraph have not been granted the authority to investigate duty-related crimes, if they discover clues of suspected duty-related crimes concerning the subjects of supervision, they shall promptly report to the dispatching organ, which shall investigate or, according to law, transfer the matter to the relevant local supervisory commission for investigation.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　经国家监察委员会批准，国家监察委员会有关派驻监察机构、监察专员可以按照[监察法第十二条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-12)第二款、第三款规定再派出。  
再派出监察机构、监察专员开展监察工作，受派出它的监察机构、监察专员领导。  
再派出监察机构、监察专员根据授权，按照管理权限依法对再派出监督单位的公职人员开展监督，对职务违法进行调查、处置。职务犯罪的调查、处置，按照[本条例第五十二条](#art-52)第二款规定办理。

**Article 14** With the approval of the National Commission of Supervision, the relevant stationed supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners of the National Commission of Supervision may further dispatch personnel according to the provisions of Article 12, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Supervision Law.  
The further dispatched supervisory institutions and supervisory commissioners shall carry out supervisory work under the leadership of the supervisory institution or supervisory commissioner that dispatched them.  
The further dispatched supervisory institutions and supervisory commissioners shall, according to authorization and within the scope of their management authority, conduct supervision over public officials of the units under their further supervision according to law, and investigate and handle duty-related violations. The investigation and handling of duty-related crimes shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Article 52, paragraph 2 of these Regulations.

#### 第二节　监察监督

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　监察机关依法履行监察监督职责，对公职人员政治品行、行使公权力和道德操守情况进行监督检查，督促有关机关、单位加强对所属公职人员的教育、管理、监督。

**Article 15** Supervisory organs shall perform their supervisory duties according to law, conduct supervision and inspection of the political conduct, exercise of public power, and moral integrity of public officials, and urge relevant organs and units to strengthen the education, management, and supervision of the public officials under them.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　监察机关应当坚决维护宪法确立的国家指导思想，加强对公职人员特别是领导人员坚持党的领导、坚持中国特色社会主义制度，贯彻落实党和国家路线方针政策、重大决策部署，履行从严管理监督职责，依法行使公权力等情况的监督。

**Article 16** Supervisory organs shall firmly uphold the national guiding ideology established by the Constitution, and strengthen supervision over the circumstances of public officials, especially leading officials, in adhering to the Party's leadership, upholding the socialist system with Chinese characteristics, implementing the Party and the state's guidelines, policies, and major decisions and deployments, performing the duty of strict management and supervision, and exercising public power according to law.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　监察机关应当加强对公职人员理想信念教育、为人民服务教育、宪法法律法规教育和社会主义先进文化、革命文化、中华优秀传统文化教育，弘扬社会主义核心价值观，深入开展警示教育，教育引导公职人员树立正确的权力观、政绩观、事业观，保持为民务实清廉本色。

**Article 17** Supervisory organs shall strengthen education for public officials on ideals and beliefs, on serving the people, on the Constitution, laws, and regulations, and on advanced socialist culture, revolutionary culture, and fine traditional Chinese culture, promote the core socialist values, conduct in-depth warning education, and educate and guide public officials to foster a correct view of power, performance, and career, and to maintain the essential quality of being for the people, pragmatic, and incorruptible.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　监察机关应当结合公职人员的职责加强日常监督，通过收集群众反映、座谈走访、查阅资料、召集或者列席会议、听取工作汇报和述责述廉、开展监督检查等方式，促进公职人员依法用权、公正用权、为民用权、廉洁用权。

**Article 18** Supervisory organs shall strengthen routine supervision in light of the duties of public officials, and promote the lawful, fair, for-the-people, and clean exercise of power by public officials through methods such as collecting public feedback, holding discussions and conducting visits, reviewing materials, convening or attending meetings, listening to work reports and reports on accountability and integrity, and conducting supervision and inspection.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　监察机关可以与公职人员进行谈心谈话，发现政治品行、行使公权力和道德操守方面有苗头性、倾向性问题的，及时进行教育提醒。

**Article 19** Supervisory organs may conduct heart-to-heart talks with public officials; if they discover signs or tendencies of problems in political conduct, exercise of public power, or moral integrity, they shall promptly provide education and reminders.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　监察机关对于发现的行业性、系统性、区域性的突出问题，以及群众反映强烈的问题，可以通过专项监督进行深入了解，督促有关机关、单位强化治理，促进公职人员履职尽责。

**Article 20** For outstanding problems of an industrial, systematic, or regional nature that are discovered, as well as problems strongly reflected by the public, supervisory organs may conduct in-depth understanding through special supervision, and urge relevant organs and units to strengthen governance, so as to promote public officials to perform their duties and fulfill their responsibilities.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　监察机关应当加强基层监督工作，促进基层监督资源和力量整合，有效衔接村（居）务监督等各类基层监督，畅通群众监督渠道，及时发现、处理侵害群众利益的不正之风和腐败问题。

**Article 21** Supervisory organs shall strengthen grassroots supervision work, promote the integration of grassroots supervision resources and forces, effectively link with various types of grassroots supervision such as village (neighborhood) affairs supervision, unblock channels for public supervision, and promptly discover and address misconduct and corruption that infringe upon the interests of the people.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　监察机关应当以办案促进整改、以监督促进治理，在查清问题、依法处置的同时，剖析问题发生的原因，发现制度建设、权力配置、监督机制等方面存在的问题，向有关机关、单位提出改进工作的意见或者监察建议，促进完善制度，提高治理效能。  
对同一行业、系统、区域相关职务违法或者职务犯罪案件，监察机关应当加强类案分析，深入挖掘存在的共性问题，提出综合性改进工作的意见或者监察建议。

**Article 22** Supervisory organs shall promote rectification through case handling and governance through supervision; while ascertaining the facts of problems and handling them according to law, they shall analyze the causes of the problems, identify issues in system construction, power allocation, supervision mechanisms, and other aspects, and submit opinions on improving work or supervisory suggestions to relevant organs or units, so as to promote the improvement of systems and enhance governance efficiency.  
For cases of duty-related violations or duty-related crimes in the same industry, system, or region, supervisory organs shall strengthen the analysis of similar cases, deeply explore the common problems that exist, and submit comprehensive opinions on improving work or supervisory suggestions.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　监察机关应当依法运用大数据、人工智能等信息化手段，整合各类监督信息资源，强化数据综合分析研判，促进及时预警风险、精准发现问题。

**Article 23** Supervisory organs shall, according to law, utilize information-based methods such as big data and artificial intelligence, integrate various types of supervision information resources, strengthen comprehensive data analysis and assessment, and promote timely risk early warning and precise problem detection.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　监察机关开展监察监督，应当与纪律监督、派驻监督、巡视监督统筹衔接，与人大监督、民主监督、行政监督、司法监督、审计监督、财会监督、统计监督、群众监督和舆论监督等贯通协调，健全信息、资源、成果共享等机制，形成监督合力。

**Article 24** When carrying out supervisory supervision, supervisory organs shall coordinate and link it with discipline supervision, stationed supervision, and inspection supervision, and shall connect and coordinate with people's congress supervision, democratic supervision, administrative supervision, judicial supervision, audit supervision, financial supervision, statistical supervision, public supervision, and media supervision, improve mechanisms for sharing information, resources, and results, and form a synergy of supervision.

#### 第三节　监察调查

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　监察机关依法履行监察调查职责，依据监察法、《中华人民共和国公职人员政务处分法》（以下简称政务处分法）和《中华人民共和国刑法》（以下简称刑法）等规定对职务违法和职务犯罪进行调查。

**Article 25** Supervisory organs shall perform their duty of supervisory investigation according to law, and investigate duty-related violations and duty-related crimes in accordance with the Supervision Law, the Law of the People's Republic of China on Administrative Sanctions for Public Officials (hereinafter referred to as the Administrative Sanctions Law), the Criminal Law of the People's Republic of China (hereinafter referred to as the Criminal Law), and other provisions.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　监察机关负责调查的职务违法是指公职人员实施的与其职务相关联，虽不构成犯罪但依法应当承担法律责任的下列违法行为：  
（一）利用职权实施的违法行为；  
（二）利用职务上的影响实施的违法行为；  
（三）履行职责不力、失职失责的违法行为；  
（四）其他违反与公职人员职务相关的特定义务的违法行为。

**Article 26** Duty-related violations subject to investigation by supervisory organs refer to the following illegal acts committed by public officials that are related to their duties, which do not constitute crimes but for which they shall bear legal liability according to law:  
(1) Illegal acts committed by abusing authority;  
(2) Illegal acts committed by taking advantage of the influence of their positions;  
(3) Illegal acts of ineffective performance of duties, dereliction of duty, or failure to fulfill responsibilities;  
(4) Other illegal acts that violate specific obligations related to the duties of public officials.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　监察机关发现公职人员存在其他违法行为，具有下列情形之一的，可以依法进行调查、处置：  
（一）超过行政违法追究时效，或者超过犯罪追诉时效、未追究刑事责任，但需要依法给予政务处分的；  
（二）被追究行政法律责任，需要依法给予政务处分的；  
（三）监察机关调查职务违法或者职务犯罪时，对被调查人实施的事实简单、清楚，需要依法给予政务处分的其他违法行为一并查核的。  
监察机关发现公职人员成为监察对象前有前款规定的违法行为的，依照前款规定办理。

**Article 27** Where a supervisory organ discovers that a public official has committed other illegal acts, and any of the following circumstances exists, it may conduct investigation and handling according to law:  
(1) The statute of limitations for administrative violation has expired, or the statute of limitations for criminal prosecution has expired and criminal liability has not been pursued, but an administrative sanction needs to be imposed according to law;  
(2) Administrative legal liability has been pursued, and an administrative sanction needs to be imposed according to law;  
(3) When investigating a duty-related violation or duty-related crime, the supervisory organ simultaneously verifies other illegal acts committed by the investigated person that are simple and clear in facts and for which an administrative sanction needs to be imposed according to law.  
Where a supervisory organ discovers that a public official committed illegal acts as prescribed in the preceding paragraph before becoming a subject of supervision, it shall handle the matter in accordance with the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　监察机关依法对[监察法第十一条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-11)第二项规定的职务犯罪进行调查。

**Article 28** Supervisory organs shall investigate the duty-related crimes as specified in Article 11, item (2) of the Supervision Law according to law.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　监察机关依法调查涉嫌贪污贿赂犯罪，包括贪污罪，挪用公款罪，受贿罪，单位受贿罪，利用影响力受贿罪，行贿罪，对有影响力的人行贿罪，对单位行贿罪，介绍贿赂罪，单位行贿罪，巨额财产来源不明罪，隐瞒境外存款罪，私分国有资产罪，私分罚没财物罪，以及公职人员在行使公权力过程中实施的职务侵占罪，挪用资金罪，对外国公职人员、国际公共组织官员行贿罪，非国家工作人员受贿罪和相关联的对非国家工作人员行贿罪。

**Article 29** Supervisory organs shall investigate suspected embezzlement and bribery crimes according to law, including the crime of embezzlement, the crime of misappropriation of public funds, the crime of acceptance of bribes, the crime of acceptance of bribes by a unit, the crime of influence-peddling to accept bribes, the crime of offering bribes, the crime of offering bribes to persons with influence, the crime of offering bribes to a unit, the crime of introduction of bribes, the crime of offering bribes by a unit, the crime of holding a huge amount of property with unidentified sources, the crime of concealing overseas deposits, the crime of unlawfully distributing state-owned assets, the crime of unlawfully distributing confiscated property, as well as the crime of job occupation committed by a public official in the course of exercising public power, the crime of misappropriation of funds, the crime of offering bribes to foreign public officials or officials of international public organizations, the crime of acceptance of bribes by a non-state functionary, and the related crime of offering bribes to a non-state functionary.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　监察机关依法调查公职人员涉嫌滥用职权犯罪，包括滥用职权罪，国有公司、企业、事业单位人员滥用职权罪，滥用管理公司、证券职权罪，食品、药品监管渎职罪，故意泄露国家秘密罪，报复陷害罪，阻碍解救被拐卖、绑架妇女、儿童罪，帮助犯罪分子逃避处罚罪，违法发放林木采伐许可证罪，办理偷越国（边）境人员出入境证件罪，放行偷越国（边）境人员罪，挪用特定款物罪，非法剥夺公民宗教信仰自由罪，侵犯少数民族风俗习惯罪，打击报复会计、统计人员罪，以及司法工作人员以外的公职人员利用职权实施的非法拘禁罪、虐待被监管人罪、非法搜查罪。

**Article 30** A supervision organ shall, according to law, investigate crimes of abuse of power suspected to have been committed by public officials, including the crime of abuse of power, the crime of abuse of power by personnel of state-owned companies, enterprises, and institutions, the crime of abuse of authority in managing companies and securities, the crime of dereliction of duty in food and drug supervision, the crime of intentionally disclosing state secrets, the crime of retaliation and frame-up, the crime of obstructing the rescue of abducted or trafficked women and children, the crime of assisting criminals to evade punishment, the crime of illegally issuing forest tree felling permits, the crime of processing entry and exit documents for persons illegally crossing the national (border) frontier, the crime of allowing persons illegally crossing the national (border) frontier to pass, the crime of misappropriating specific funds and materials, the crime of illegally depriving citizens of their freedom of religious belief, the crime of infringing upon the customs and habits of minority nationalities, the crime of retaliating against accountants and statisticians, and the crime of illegal detention, the crime of maltreating persons under custody, and the crime of illegal search committed by public officials other than judicial personnel through the use of their powers.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　监察机关依法调查公职人员涉嫌玩忽职守犯罪，包括玩忽职守罪，国有公司、企业、事业单位人员失职罪，签订、履行合同失职被骗罪，国家机关工作人员签订、履行合同失职被骗罪，环境监管失职罪，传染病防治失职罪，商检失职罪，动植物检疫失职罪，不解救被拐卖、绑架妇女、儿童罪，失职造成珍贵文物损毁、流失罪，过失泄露国家秘密罪。

**Article 31** A supervision organ shall, according to law, investigate crimes of dereliction of duty suspected to have been committed by public officials, including the crime of dereliction of duty, the crime of negligence of duty by personnel of state-owned companies, enterprises, and institutions, the crime of being defrauded due to negligence in concluding and performing contracts, the crime of being defrauded due to negligence in concluding and performing contracts by state organ personnel, the crime of negligence in environmental supervision, the crime of negligence in prevention and control of infectious diseases, the crime of negligence in commodity inspection, the crime of negligence in animal and plant quarantine, the crime of failing to rescue abducted or trafficked women and children, the crime of causing damage or loss of valuable cultural relics through negligence, and the crime of negligently disclosing state secrets.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　监察机关依法调查公职人员涉嫌徇私舞弊犯罪，包括徇私舞弊低价折股、出售公司、企业资产罪，非法批准征收、征用、占用土地罪，非法低价出让国有土地使用权罪，非法经营同类营业罪，为亲友非法牟利罪，枉法仲裁罪，徇私舞弊发售发票、抵扣税款、出口退税罪，商检徇私舞弊罪，动植物检疫徇私舞弊罪，放纵走私罪，放纵制售伪劣商品犯罪行为罪，招收公务员、学生徇私舞弊罪，徇私舞弊不移交刑事案件罪，违法提供出口退税凭证罪，徇私舞弊不征、少征税款罪。

**Article 32** A supervision organ shall, according to law, investigate crimes of engaging in malpractices for personal gain suspected to have been committed by public officials, including the crime of converting shares or selling assets of companies or enterprises at low prices through malpractices for personal gain, the crime of illegally approving expropriation, requisition, or occupation of land, the crime of illegally granting state-owned land use rights at low prices, the crime of illegally operating similar businesses, the crime of illegally seeking profits for relatives or friends, the crime of perverting the law in arbitration, the crime of issuing invoices, deducting taxes, or granting tax rebates for exports through malpractices for personal gain, the crime of engaging in malpractices for personal gain in commodity inspection, the crime of engaging in malpractices for personal gain in animal and plant quarantine, the crime of conniving at smuggling, the crime of conniving at criminal acts of manufacturing and selling counterfeit or shoddy goods, the crime of engaging in malpractices for personal gain in recruiting public servants or students, the crime of failing to transfer criminal cases through malpractices for personal gain, the crime of illegally providing export tax rebate vouchers, and the crime of failing to collect or under-collecting taxes through malpractices for personal gain.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　监察机关依法调查公职人员在行使公权力过程中涉及的重大责任事故犯罪，包括重大责任事故罪，教育设施重大安全事故罪，消防责任事故罪，重大劳动安全事故罪，强令、组织他人违章冒险作业罪，危险作业罪，不报、谎报安全事故罪，铁路运营安全事故罪，重大飞行事故罪，大型群众性活动重大安全事故罪，危险物品肇事罪，工程重大安全事故罪。

**Article 33** A supervision organ shall, according to law, investigate major liability accident crimes involved in the exercise of public power by public officials, including the crime of major liability accident, the crime of major safety accident involving educational facilities, the crime of liability accident involving fire protection, the crime of major labor safety accident, the crime of ordering or organizing others to operate in violation of regulations and taking risks, the crime of dangerous operations, the crime of failing to report or falsely reporting safety accidents, the crime of safety accident in railway operation, the crime of major flight accident, the crime of major safety accident in large-scale mass activities, the crime of causing an accident involving dangerous articles, and the crime of major safety accident in engineering.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　监察机关依法调查公职人员在行使公权力过程中涉及的其他犯罪，包括破坏选举罪，背信损害上市公司利益罪，金融工作人员购买假币、以假币换取货币罪，利用未公开信息交易罪，诱骗投资者买卖证券、期货合约罪，背信运用受托财产罪，违法运用资金罪，违法发放贷款罪，吸收客户资金不入账罪，违规出具金融票证罪，对违法票据承兑、付款、保证罪，非法转让、倒卖土地使用权罪，私自开拆、隐匿、毁弃邮件、电报罪，故意延误投递邮件罪，泄露不应公开的案件信息罪，披露、报道不应公开的案件信息罪，接送不合格兵员罪。

**Article 34** A supervision organ shall, according to law, investigate other crimes involved in the exercise of public power by public officials, including the crime of undermining elections, the crime of breach of trust damaging interests of listed companies, the crime of financial staff purchasing counterfeit currency or exchanging counterfeit currency for genuine currency, the crime of trading using undisclosed information, the crime of inducing investors to buy or sell securities or futures contracts, the crime of breach of trust in using entrusted property, the crime of illegally using funds, the crime of illegally granting loans, the crime of failing to record client funds, the crime of issuing financial instruments in violation of regulations, the crime of accepting, paying, or guaranteeing illegal instruments, the crime of illegally transferring or reselling land use rights, the crime of privately opening, concealing, or destroying mail or telegrams, the crime of intentionally delaying delivery of mail, the crime of disclosing case information that should not be made public, the crime of disclosing or reporting case information that should not be made public, and the crime of accepting or sending unqualified recruits.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　监察机关发现依法由其他机关管辖的违法犯罪线索，应当及时移送有管辖权的机关。  
监察机关调查结束后，对于应当给予被调查人或者涉案人员行政处罚等其他处理的，依法移送有关机关。

**Article 35** Where a supervision organ discovers clues of illegal conduct or crimes that shall be under the jurisdiction of another organ according to law, it shall promptly transfer them to the organ with jurisdiction.  
After the investigation by the supervision organ is concluded, where administrative penalties or other dispositions shall be imposed on the person under investigation or the persons involved, they shall be transferred to the relevant organ according to law.

#### 第四节　监察处置

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　监察机关对违法的公职人员，依据监察法、政务处分法等规定作出政务处分决定。

**Article 36** A supervision organ shall, based on the provisions of the Supervision Law, the Law on Administrative Discipline for Public Officials, and other regulations, make decisions on administrative discipline for public officials who have committed illegal acts.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　监察机关在追究违法的公职人员直接责任的同时，依法对履行职责不力、失职失责，造成严重后果或者恶劣影响的领导人员予以问责。  
监察机关应当组成调查组依法开展问责调查。调查结束后经集体讨论形成调查报告，需要进行问责的按照管理权限作出问责决定，或者向有权作出问责决定的机关、单位书面提出问责建议。

**Article 37** While investigating the direct liability of public officials who have committed illegal acts, a supervision organ shall, according to law, hold accountable the leading personnel who have failed to perform their duties effectively, been derelict in their duties, or caused serious consequences or adverse impacts.  
The supervision organ shall form an investigation team to carry out an accountability investigation according to law. After the investigation is concluded, an investigation report shall be formed through collective discussion. Where accountability is required, a decision on accountability shall be made according to the management authority, or a written proposal on accountability shall be submitted to the organ or unit that has the authority to make such a decision.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　监察机关对涉嫌职务犯罪的人员，经调查认为犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分，需要追究刑事责任的，依法移送人民检察院审查起诉。

**Article 38** Where a supervision organ, after investigating a person suspected of duty-related crimes, deems that the criminal facts are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and criminal liability shall be investigated, it shall transfer the case to the People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution according to law.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　监察机关根据监督、调查结果，发现监察对象所在单位具有下列情形之一，需要整改纠正的，依法提出监察建议，推动以案促改工作：  
（一）廉政建设方面存在突出问题的；  
（二）权力运行制约监督方面存在较大风险的；  
（三）监察对象教育管理监督方面存在突出问题的；  
（四）执行法律法规制度不到位的；  
（五）不履行或者不正确履行法定职责的；  
（六）其他需要提出监察建议的情形。  
监察机关应当跟踪了解监察建议的采纳情况，指导、督促有关单位限期整改，对未达到整改要求的提出进一步整改意见，推动监察建议落实到位。

**Article 39** Where a supervision organ, based on the results of supervision and investigation, discovers that the unit to which the supervisee belongs has any of the following circumstances requiring rectification and correction, it shall, according to law, make a supervision proposal to promote the work of learning from cases and promoting reform:  
(1) There are prominent problems in the construction of clean government;  
(2) There are significant risks in the restriction and supervision of the exercise of power;  
(3) There are prominent problems in the education, management, and supervision of supervisees;  
(4) The implementation of laws, regulations, and systems is not up to standard;  
(5) There is failure to perform or incorrect performance of statutory duties;  
(6) Other circumstances where a supervision proposal is necessary.  
The supervision organ shall follow up and understand the adoption of the supervision proposal, guide and urge the relevant unit to make rectification within a time limit, and propose further rectification opinions for those that have not met the rectification requirements, so as to ensure the implementation of the supervision proposal.

### 第三章　监察范围和管辖

#### 第一节　监察对象

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　监察机关依法对所有行使公权力的公职人员进行监察，实现国家监察全面覆盖。

**Article 40** A supervision organ shall, according to law, supervise all public officials who exercise public power, so as to achieve full coverage of state supervision.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第一项所称公务员范围，依据《中华人民共和国公务员法》（以下简称公务员法）确定。  
[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第一项所称参照公务员法管理的人员，是指有关单位中经批准参照公务员法进行管理的工作人员。

**Article 41** The scope of public servants as referred to in Item 1 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law shall be determined in accordance with the Public Servants Law of the People's Republic of China (hereinafter referred to as the Public Servants Law).  
The personnel managed with reference to the Public Servants Law as referred to in Item 1 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law refer to the staff members in relevant units who are approved to be managed with reference to the Public Servants Law.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第二项所称法律、法规授权或者受国家机关依法委托管理公共事务的组织中从事公务的人员，是指在上述组织中，除参照公务员法管理的人员外，对公共事务履行组织、领导、管理、监督等职责的人员，包括具有公共事务管理职能的行业协会等组织中从事公务的人员，以及法定检验检测、检疫等机构中从事公务的人员。

**Article 42** The personnel engaging in public affairs in organizations authorized by laws and regulations or entrusted by state organs according to law to manage public affairs as referred to in Item 2 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law refer to, in the aforementioned organizations, the personnel who perform duties such as organization, leadership, management, and supervision over public affairs, excluding those managed with reference to the Public Servants Law, including personnel engaging in public affairs in industry associations and other organizations with public affairs management functions, as well as personnel engaging in public affairs in statutory inspection, testing, quarantine and other institutions.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第三项所称国有企业管理人员，是指国家出资企业中的下列人员：  
（一）在国有独资、全资公司、企业中履行组织、领导、管理、监督等职责的人员；  
（二）经党组织或者国家机关，国有独资、全资公司、企业，事业单位提名、推荐、任命、批准等，在国有控股、参股公司及其分支机构中履行组织、领导、管理、监督等职责的人员；  
（三）经国家出资企业中负有管理、监督国有资产职责的组织批准或者研究决定，代表其在国有控股、参股公司及其分支机构中从事组织、领导、管理、监督等工作的人员。

**Article 43** The management personnel of state-owned enterprises as referred to in Item 3 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law refer to the following personnel in state-invested enterprises:  
(1) Personnel who perform duties such as organization, leadership, management, and supervision in wholly state-owned or wholly state-funded companies or enterprises;  
(2) Personnel who, through nomination, recommendation, appointment, or approval by Party organizations or state organs, wholly state-owned or wholly state-funded companies or enterprises, or public institutions, perform duties such as organization, leadership, management, and supervision in state-controlled or state-invested companies and their branches;  
(3) Personnel who, with the approval or decision after study by the organization responsible for the management and supervision of state-owned assets in state-invested enterprises, engage in organization, leadership, management, supervision and other work on its behalf in state-controlled or state-invested companies and their branches.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第四项所称公办的教育、科研、文化、医疗卫生、体育等单位中从事管理的人员，是指国家为了社会公益目的，由国家机关举办或者其他组织利用国有资产举办的教育、科研、文化、医疗卫生、体育等事业单位中，从事组织、领导、管理、监督等工作的人员。

**Article 44** The management personnel in publicly-run units such as education, scientific research, culture, medical and health care, and sports as referred to in Item 4 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law refer to the personnel who engage in organization, leadership, management, supervision and other work in public institutions such as education, scientific research, culture, medical and health care, and sports that are established by state organs or established by other organizations using state-owned assets for the purpose of public welfare.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第五项所称基层群众性自治组织中从事管理的人员，是指该组织中的下列人员：  
（一）从事集体事务和公益事业管理的人员；  
（二）从事集体资金、资产、资源管理的人员；  
（三）协助人民政府从事行政管理工作的人员，包括从事救灾、防疫、抢险、防汛、优抚、帮扶、移民、救济款物的管理，社会捐助公益事业款物的管理，国有土地的经营和管理，土地征收、征用补偿费用的管理，代征、代缴税款，有关计划生育、户籍、征兵工作，协助人民政府等国家机关在基层群众性自治组织中从事的其他管理工作。

**Article 45** The management personnel in primary-level self-governing mass organizations as referred to in Item 5 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law refer to the following personnel in such organizations:  
(1) Personnel engaged in the management of collective affairs and public welfare undertakings;  
(2) Personnel engaged in the management of collective funds, assets, and resources;  
(3) Personnel who assist the people's government in performing administrative management work, including the management of relief funds and materials for disaster relief, epidemic prevention, emergency rescue, flood control, special care, assistance, resettlement, and relief; the management of funds and materials for social donation to public welfare undertakings; the operation and management of state-owned land; the management of compensation fees for land expropriation and requisition; the collection and payment of taxes on behalf of the government; work related to family planning, household registration, and conscription; and other management work in primary-level self-governing mass organizations assisting the people's government and other state organs.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　下列人员属于[监察法第十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-15)第六项所称其他依法履行公职的人员：  
（一）履行人民代表大会职责的各级人民代表大会代表，履行公职的中国人民政治协商会议各级委员会委员、人民陪审员、人民监督员；  
（二）虽未列入党政机关人员编制，但在党政机关中从事公务的人员；  
（三）在集体经济组织等单位、组织中，由党组织或者国家机关，国有独资、全资公司、企业，国家出资企业中负有管理监督国有和集体资产职责的组织，事业单位提名、推荐、任命、批准等，从事组织、领导、管理、监督等工作的人员；  
（四）在依法组建的评标、谈判、询价等组织中代表国家机关，国有独资、全资公司、企业，事业单位，人民团体临时履行公共事务组织、领导、管理、监督等职责的人员；  
（五）其他依法行使公权力的人员。

**Article 46** The following persons are other personnel who perform public duties according to law as referred to in Item 6 of Article 15 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) Deputies to the people's congresses at all levels who perform the duties of the people's congresses, members of the committees of the Chinese People's Political Consultative Conference at all levels who perform public duties, people's assessors, and people's supervisors;  
(2) Personnel who, although not included in the staffing of Party and government organs, engage in public affairs in Party and government organs;  
(3) Personnel who, through nomination, recommendation, appointment, or approval by Party organizations or state organs, wholly state-owned or wholly state-funded companies or enterprises, organizations responsible for the management and supervision of state-owned and collective assets in state-invested enterprises, or public institutions, engage in organization, leadership, management, supervision and other work in collective economic organizations and other units or organizations;  
(4) Personnel who, in bidding evaluation, negotiation, inquiry and other organizations established according to law, temporarily perform duties such as organization, leadership, management, and supervision of public affairs on behalf of state organs, wholly state-owned or wholly state-funded companies or enterprises, public institutions, or people's organizations;  
(5) Other personnel who exercise public power according to law.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　有关机关、单位、组织集体作出的决定违法或者实施违法行为的，监察机关应当对负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员中的公职人员依法追究法律责任。

**Article 47** Where a decision made collectively by a relevant organ, unit, or organization is illegal, or an illegal act is committed collectively, the supervision organ shall, according to law, investigate the legal liability of the leading personnel who are responsible and the public officials among the persons directly responsible.

#### 第二节　管辖

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　监察机关开展监督、调查、处置，按照管理权限与属地管辖相结合的原则，实行分级负责制。

**Article 48** A supervision organ shall carry out supervision, investigation, and disposition in accordance with the principle of combining management authority with territorial jurisdiction, and implement a system of graded responsibility.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　设区的市级以上监察委员会按照管理权限，依法管辖同级党委管理的公职人员涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪案件。  
县级监察委员会和直辖市所辖区（县）监察委员会按照管理权限，依法管辖本辖区内公职人员涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪案件。  
地方各级监察委员会按照[本条例第十三条](#art-13)、第五十二条规定，可以依法管辖工作单位在本辖区内的有关公职人员涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪案件。  
监察机关调查公职人员涉嫌职务犯罪案件，可以依法对涉嫌行贿犯罪、介绍贿赂犯罪或者共同职务犯罪的涉案人员中的非公职人员一并管辖并进行调查处置。非公职人员涉嫌利用影响力受贿罪的，监察机关按照其所利用的公职人员的管理权限确定管辖并进行调查处置。

**Article 49** Supervision commissions at or above the level of a city divided into districts shall, according to law, have jurisdiction over cases of suspected duty-related violations and duty-related crimes involving public officials managed by the Party committee at the same level in accordance with the management authority.  
County-level supervision commissions and supervision commissions of districts (counties) under municipalities directly under the Central Government shall, according to law, have jurisdiction over cases of suspected duty-related violations and duty-related crimes involving public officials within their respective jurisdictions in accordance with the management authority.  
Local supervision commissions at all levels may, according to law, have jurisdiction over cases of suspected duty-related violations and duty-related crimes involving relevant public officials whose work units are located within their jurisdictions in accordance with the provisions of Articles 13 and 52 of these Regulations.  
Where a supervision organ investigates a case of a public official suspected of a duty-related crime, it may, according to law, have jurisdiction over and investigate and dispose of non-public officials among the persons involved who are suspected of crimes of offering bribes, introducing bribes, or jointly committing duty-related crimes. Where a non-public official is suspected of the crime of using influence to accept bribes, the supervision organ shall determine jurisdiction and carry out investigation and disposition in accordance with the management authority of the public official whose influence is used.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　上级监察机关对于下一级监察机关管辖范围内的职务违法和职务犯罪案件，具有下列情形之一的，可以依法提级管辖：  
（一）在本辖区有重大影响的；  
（二）涉及多个下级监察机关管辖的监察对象，调查难度大的；  
（三）其他需要提级管辖的重大、复杂案件。  
上级监察机关对于所辖各级监察机关管辖范围内有重大影响的案件，必要时可以依法直接调查或者组织、指挥、参与调查。  
地方各级监察机关所管辖的职务违法和职务犯罪案件，具有第一款规定情形的，可以依法报请上一级监察机关管辖。

**Article 50** A superior supervisory organ may, according to law, elevate the level of jurisdiction over duty-related illegal or criminal cases within the jurisdiction of a lower-level supervisory organ under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) Having a significant impact within its jurisdiction;  
(2) Involving supervisees under the jurisdiction of multiple lower-level supervisory organs, making investigation difficult;  
(3) Other major or complex cases requiring elevation of jurisdiction.  
A superior supervisory organ may, when necessary, directly investigate, or organize, direct, or participate in the investigation of, any case with a significant impact within the jurisdiction of any supervisory organ under its supervision, according to law.  
Where a duty-related illegal or criminal case under the jurisdiction of a local supervisory organ at any level meets the circumstances specified in paragraph 1, that organ may, according to law, request a supervisory organ at the next higher level to assume jurisdiction.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　上级监察机关可以依法将其所管辖的案件指定下级监察机关管辖。  
设区的市级监察委员会将同级党委管理的公职人员涉嫌职务违法或者职务犯罪案件指定下级监察委员会管辖的，应当报请省级监察委员会批准；省级监察委员会将同级党委管理的公职人员涉嫌职务违法或者职务犯罪案件指定下级监察委员会管辖的，应当报送国家监察委员会相关监督检查部门备案。  
上级监察机关对于下级监察机关管辖的职务违法和职务犯罪案件，具有下列情形之一，认为由其他下级监察机关管辖更为适宜的，可以依法指定给其他下级监察机关管辖：  
（一）管辖有争议的；  
（二）指定管辖有利于案件公正处理的；  
（三）下级监察机关报请指定管辖的；  
（四）其他有必要指定管辖的。  
被指定的下级监察机关未经指定管辖的监察机关批准，不得将案件再行指定管辖。发现新的职务违法或者职务犯罪线索，以及其他重要情况、重大问题，应当及时向指定管辖的监察机关请示报告。

**Article 51** A superior supervisory organ may, according to law, designate a case under its own jurisdiction to a lower-level supervisory organ for jurisdiction.  
Where a supervisory commission of a city divided into districts designates to a lower-level supervisory commission a case involving a public official under the management of the same-level Party committee suspected of duty-related illegality or crime, it shall report to the provincial-level supervisory commission for approval; where a provincial-level supervisory commission designates such a case to a lower-level supervisory commission, it shall submit the matter to the relevant supervision and inspection department of the National Commission of Supervision for filing.  
Where a superior supervisory organ, with respect to a duty-related illegal or criminal case under the jurisdiction of a lower-level supervisory organ, finds it more appropriate for another lower-level supervisory organ to assume jurisdiction under any of the following circumstances, it may, according to law, designate the case to that other lower-level supervisory organ for jurisdiction:  
(1) There is a dispute over jurisdiction;  
(2) Designation of jurisdiction is conducive to the fair handling of the case;  
(3) The lower-level supervisory organ requests designation of jurisdiction;  
(4) Other circumstances where designation of jurisdiction is necessary.  
The designated lower-level supervisory organ shall not redesignate the case for jurisdiction without the approval of the supervisory organ that made the designation. If it discovers new leads of duty-related illegality or crime, or other important circumstances or major issues, it shall promptly report to the supervisory organ that made the designation for instructions.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　工作单位在地方、管理权限在主管部门的公职人员涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪，一般由驻在主管部门、有管辖权的监察机构、监察专员管辖；经协商，监察机构、监察专员可以按规定移交公职人员工作单位所在地的地方监察委员会调查，或者与地方监察委员会联合调查。地方监察委员会在工作中发现上述公职人员有关问题线索，应当向驻在主管部门、有管辖权的监察机构、监察专员通报，并协商确定管辖。  
前款规定单位的其他公职人员涉嫌职务违法的，可以由公职人员工作单位所在地的地方监察委员会管辖。涉嫌职务犯罪的，一般由公职人员工作单位所在地的地方监察委员会管辖；因涉及主管部门管理的公职人员等特殊情形，驻在主管部门的监察机构、监察专员认为由自己管辖或者其他地方监察委员会管辖更为适宜的，经与公职人员工作单位所在地的地方监察委员会协商，可以自行调查或者依法办理指定管辖。  
地方监察委员会调查前两款规定案件，应当按程序将立案、留置、移送审查起诉、撤销案件等重要情况通报相关监察机构、监察专员。

**Article 52** Where a public official whose work unit is located in a locality but whose management authority belongs to the competent department is suspected of duty-related illegality or crime, the case shall generally be under the jurisdiction of the supervisory body or supervisory commissioner stationed in the competent department that has jurisdiction; after consultation, the supervisory body or supervisory commissioner may, according to regulations, transfer the case to the local supervisory commission of the place where the public official's work unit is located for investigation, or conduct a joint investigation with the local supervisory commission. Where the local supervisory commission discovers leads concerning such public official in its work, it shall notify the supervisory body or supervisory commissioner stationed in the competent department that has jurisdiction, and determine the jurisdiction through consultation.  
Where another public official of the work unit specified in the preceding paragraph is suspected of duty-related illegality, the case may be under the jurisdiction of the local supervisory commission of the place where the public official's work unit is located. Where the public official is suspected of duty-related crime, the case shall generally be under the jurisdiction of the local supervisory commission of the place where the public official's work unit is located; however, due to special circumstances such as involving a public official under the management of the competent department, if the supervisory body or supervisory commissioner stationed in the competent department considers it more appropriate for itself or another local supervisory commission to have jurisdiction, it may, after consultation with the local supervisory commission of the place where the public official's work unit is located, investigate the case on its own or handle the designation of jurisdiction according to law.  
Where a local supervisory commission investigates a case specified in the preceding two paragraphs, it shall, according to procedures, notify the relevant supervisory bodies and supervisory commissioners of important circumstances such as case filing, detention, referral for prosecution, and case dismissal.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　监察机关办理案件中涉及无隶属关系的其他监察机关的监察对象，认为需要立案调查的，应当商请有管理权限的监察机关依法立案调查。商请立案时，应当提供涉案人员基本情况、已经查明的涉嫌违法犯罪事实以及相关证据材料。  
承办案件的监察机关认为由其一并调查更为适宜的，可以报请有权决定的上级监察机关指定管辖。

**Article 53** Where a supervisory organ, in handling a case, involves a supervisee of another supervisory organ with which no subordination relationship exists, and considers it necessary to file a case for investigation, it shall consult with the supervisory organ that has management authority to file the case and investigate according to law. When consulting for case filing, it shall provide the basic information of the person involved, the facts of suspected illegality or crime that have been established, and relevant evidentiary materials.  
If the supervisory organ undertaking the case considers it more appropriate for itself to investigate the case together, it may report to a superior supervisory organ with the authority to decide for designation of jurisdiction.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　公职人员既涉嫌贪污贿赂、失职渎职等严重职务违法和职务犯罪，又涉嫌公安机关、人民检察院等机关管辖的犯罪，依法由监察机关为主调查的，应当由监察机关和其他机关分别依职权立案，监察机关承担组织协调职责，协调调查和侦查工作进度、重要调查和侦查措施使用等重要事项。

**Article 54** Where a public official is suspected of both serious duty-related illegality and crime such as embezzlement, bribery, dereliction of duty or malfeasance, and crimes under the jurisdiction of public security organs, People's Procuratorates or other organs, and the investigation is primarily undertaken by the supervisory organ according to law, the supervisory organ and the other organs shall each file cases within their respective functions and powers. The supervisory organ shall assume the responsibility of organization and coordination, coordinating the progress of investigation and criminal investigation work, and important matters such as the use of important investigative and criminal investigation measures.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　监察机关必要时可以依法调查司法工作人员利用职权实施的涉嫌非法拘禁、刑讯逼供、非法搜查等侵犯公民权利、损害司法公正的犯罪，并在立案后及时通报同级人民检察院。  
监察机关在调查司法工作人员涉嫌贪污贿赂等职务犯罪中，可以对其涉嫌的前款规定的犯罪一并调查，并及时通报同级人民检察院。人民检察院在办理直接受理侦查的案件中，发现犯罪嫌疑人同时涉嫌监察机关管辖的其他职务犯罪，经沟通全案移送监察机关管辖的，监察机关应当依法进行调查。

**Article 55** Where necessary, a supervisory organ may, according to law, investigate crimes committed by judicial personnel by abusing their powers, such as illegal detention, extortion of confessions by torture, illegal search, which infringe upon citizens' rights and impair judicial justice, and shall timely notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level after filing a case.  
Where a supervisory organ is investigating a judicial officer suspected of duty-related crimes such as embezzlement or bribery, it may simultaneously investigate the crimes specified in the preceding paragraph that the judicial officer is suspected of, and shall timely notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level. Where a People's Procuratorate, in handling a case directly accepted for investigation, discovers that the criminal suspect is also suspected of other duty-related crimes under the jurisdiction of a supervisory organ, and after communication, the entire case is transferred to the supervisory organ for jurisdiction, the supervisory organ shall conduct the investigation according to law.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　监察机关对于退休公职人员在退休前或者退休后，或者离职、死亡的公职人员在履职期间实施的涉嫌职务违法或者职务犯罪行为，可以依法进行调查。  
对前款规定人员，按照其原任职务的管辖规定确定管辖的监察机关；由其他监察机关管辖更为适宜的，可以依法指定或者交由其他监察机关管辖。

**Article 56** A supervisory organ may, according to law, investigate suspected duty-related illegal or criminal acts committed by a retired public official before or after retirement, or by a public official who has resigned or died during the period of performing official duties.  
For a person specified in the preceding paragraph, the supervisory organ with jurisdiction shall be determined in accordance with the jurisdiction rules based on their former position; if it is more appropriate for another supervisory organ to have jurisdiction, the case may, according to law, be designated or transferred to that other supervisory organ for jurisdiction.

### 第四章　监察权限

#### 第一节　一般要求

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　监察机关应当加强监督执法调查工作规范化建设，严格按规定对监察措施进行审批和监管，依照法定的范围、程序和期限采取相关措施，出具、送达法律文书。

**Article 57** A supervisory organ shall strengthen the standardized construction of supervision, law enforcement and investigation work, strictly examine and approve and supervise supervisory measures according to regulations, adopt relevant measures in accordance with the statutory scope, procedures and time limits, and issue and serve legal documents.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　监察机关应当根据开展监督执法调查工作的需要、涉嫌职务违法或者职务犯罪行为的严重程度、监察措施适用对象与案件的关联程度，以及采取监察措施的紧急程度等情况，合理确定采取监察措施的对象、种类和期限，不得超过必要限度。禁止违反规定滥用监察措施。

**Article 58** A supervisory organ shall reasonably determine the targets, types and duration of supervisory measures based on the needs of carrying out supervision, law enforcement and investigation work, the severity of the suspected duty-related illegal or criminal acts, the degree of connection between the object of the supervisory measure and the case, and the urgency of taking the supervisory measure, and shall not exceed the necessary limits. The abuse of supervisory measures in violation of regulations is prohibited.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　监察机关在初步核实中，可以依法采取谈话、询问、查询、调取、勘验检查、调查实验、鉴定措施；立案后可以采取讯问、强制到案、责令候查、管护、留置、禁闭、冻结、搜查、查封、扣押、通缉措施。发现存在逃跑、自杀等重大安全风险，在立案前依法对[监察法第二十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-25)第一款第一项、第二项规定的人员采取管护措施的，符合立案条件的应当及时立案。需要采取技术调查、限制出境措施的，应当按照规定交有关机关依法执行。设区的市级以下监察机关在初步核实中不得采取技术调查措施。监察机关采取谈话、函询方式处置问题线索的，适用监察法和本条例关于采取该两项措施的相关规定。  
开展问责调查，根据具体情况可以依法采取相关监察措施。

**Article 59** A supervisory organ may, in preliminary verification, take measures such as interviews, inquiries, inquiries (records searching), retrieval, inspection and examination, investigation experiments, and expert evaluations according to law; after filing a case, it may take measures such as interrogation, compulsory appearance, release on pending investigation, protective custody, detention, confinement, freezing, search, sealing, seizure, and wanted notice. If it discovers significant safety risks such as escape or suicide, and takes protective custody measures according to law against persons specified in items (1) and (2) of paragraph 1 of Article 25 of the Supervision Law before filing a case, it shall promptly file a case if the conditions for case filing are met. Where technical investigation measures or measures restricting exit from the country are necessary, they shall be submitted to the relevant organ for implementation according to law. A supervisory organ below the level of a city divided into districts shall not take technical investigation measures during preliminary verification. Where a supervisory organ uses interviews or correspondence to handle leads, the relevant provisions of the Supervision Law and these Regulations regarding these two measures shall apply.  
In conducting an accountability investigation, relevant supervisory measures may be taken according to law depending on the specific circumstances.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　开展讯问、搜查、查封、扣押以及重要的谈话、询问等调查取证工作，应当全程同步录音录像，并保持录音录像资料的完整性。  
对谈话、讯问、询问进行同步录音录像的，谈话笔录、讯问笔录、询问笔录记载的起止时间应当与录音录像资料反映的起止时间一致。谈话笔录、讯问笔录、询问笔录内容应当与录音录像资料内容相符。  
同步录音录像资料应当妥善保管、及时归档，留存备查。监察机关案件监督管理部门应当开展常态化检查。人民检察院、人民法院需要调取同步录音录像的，监察机关应当依法予以提供。

**Article 60** In carrying out evidence collection work such as interrogation, search, sealing, seizure, and important interviews or inquiries, the entire process shall be simultaneously recorded by audio and video, and the integrity of the audio and video recordings shall be maintained.  
Where simultaneous audio and video recordings are made during interviews, interrogations, or inquiries, the start and end times recorded in the interview transcripts, interrogation transcripts, and inquiry transcripts shall be consistent with the times reflected in the audio and video recordings. The contents of the interview transcripts, interrogation transcripts, and inquiry transcripts shall be consistent with the contents of the audio and video recordings.  
The simultaneous audio and video recordings shall be properly kept, promptly archived, and retained for future reference. The case supervision and management department of the supervisory organ shall conduct regular inspections. Where the People's Procuratorate or the People's Court needs to obtain simultaneous audio and video recordings, the supervisory organ shall provide them according to law.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　需要商请其他监察机关协助收集证据材料的，应当依法出具《委托调查函》；商请其他监察机关对采取措施提供一般性协助的，应当依法出具《商请协助采取措施函》。商请协助事项涉及协助地监察机关管辖的监察对象的，应当由协助地监察机关按照所涉人员的管理权限报批。协助地监察机关对于协助请求，应当依法予以协助配合。

**Article 61** Where it is necessary to request assistance from another supervisory organ in collecting evidentiary materials, a "Letter of Commissioned Investigation" shall be issued according to law; where it is necessary to request general assistance from another supervisory organ in taking measures, a "Letter Requesting Assistance in Taking Measures" shall be issued according to law. Where the matter for which assistance is requested involves a supervisee under the jurisdiction of the assisting supervisory organ, the assisting supervisory organ shall report and obtain approval according to the management authority of the person involved. The assisting supervisory organ shall provide assistance and cooperation according to law with respect to the request for assistance.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　采取、解除或者变更监察措施需要告知、通知相关人员的，应当依法办理。告知包括口头、书面两种方式，除本条例另有规定外，通知应当采取书面方式。采取口头方式告知、通知的，应当将相关情况制作工作记录；采取书面方式告知、通知的，可以通过直接送交、邮寄、转交等途径送达，将有关回执或者凭证附卷。  
无法告知、通知，或者相关人员拒绝接收的，调查人员应当在工作记录或者有关文书上记明。

**Article 62** Where a supervisory measure is taken, lifted or changed, and it is necessary to inform or notify the relevant person, this shall be handled according to law. Informing includes both oral and written methods; except as otherwise provided in these Regulations, notification shall be in written form. Where information or notice is given orally, a work record of the relevant circumstances shall be made; where information or notice is given in writing, it may be served by direct delivery, mail, transfer or other means, and the relevant receipt or voucher shall be attached to the file.  
Where it is impossible to give information or notice, or the relevant person refuses to accept it, the investigator shall record this in the work record or relevant document.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　监察机关采取监察措施，依法需要见证人在场的，应当邀请合适的见证人在场。下列人员不得担任见证人：  
（一）生理上、精神上有缺陷或者未成年，不具有相应辨别能力或者不能正确表达的人；  
（二）与案件有利害关系，可能影响案件公正处理的人；  
（三）监察机关的工作人员或者其聘用的人员；  
（四）依法协助监察机关采取监察措施的工作人员。

**Article 63** Where a supervisory organ takes a supervisory measure and, according to law, requires the presence of a witness, it shall invite a suitable witness to be present. The following persons shall not serve as witnesses:  
(1) Persons who have physical or mental defects or are minors and lack the corresponding ability to discern or correctly express themselves;  
(2) Persons who have an interest in the case and may affect the fair handling of the case;  
(3) Staff members of the supervisory organ or persons employed by it;  
(4) Staff members who assist the supervisory organ in taking supervisory measures according to law.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　监察机关依法变更强制到案、责令候查、管护、留置以及禁闭等监察强制措施的，原监察强制措施自监察机关采取新的监察强制措施之时自动解除。

**Article 64** Where a supervisory organ changes compulsory measures such as compulsory appearance, release on pending investigation, protective custody, detention, or confinement according to law, the original compulsory measure shall be automatically lifted at the time when the supervisory organ takes the new compulsory measure.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　县级以上监察机关需要提请公安机关协助采取强制到案、责令候查、管护、留置、搜查措施的，应当按规定报批，请同级公安机关依法予以协助。提请协助时，应当出具提请协助函，列明提请协助的具体事项和建议，协助采取措施的时间、地点等内容，附采取监察措施决定书复印件。  
因保密需要，不宜在采取监察措施前向公安机关告知采取措施对象姓名的，可以作出说明，进行保密处理。  
需要提请异地公安机关协助采取监察措施的，应当按规定报批，向协作地同级监察机关出具协作函件和相关文书，由协作地监察机关提请当地公安机关依法予以协助。

**Article 65** Where a supervisory organ at or above the county level needs to request a public security organ to assist in taking measures such as compulsory appearance, release on pending investigation, protective custody, detention, or search, it shall report for approval according to regulations and request the public security organ at the same level to provide assistance according to law. When requesting assistance, it shall issue a letter requesting assistance, specifying the specific matters and suggestions for which assistance is requested, the time and place of the assistance measures, and shall attach a copy of the decision to take the supervisory measure.  
If, due to confidentiality needs, it is inappropriate to inform the public security organ of the name of the person subject to the measure before taking the measure, an explanation may be made and confidentiality measures may be taken.  
Where it is necessary to request assistance from a public security organ in a different locality to take supervisory measures, it shall report for approval according to regulations, issue a letter of cooperation and relevant documents to the supervisory organ at the same level in the cooperating locality, and the supervisory organ in the cooperating locality shall request the local public security organ to provide assistance according to law.

#### 第二节　证据

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　可以用于证明案件事实的材料都是证据，包括：  
（一）物证；  
（二）书证；  
（三）证人证言；  
（四）被害人陈述；  
（五）被调查人陈述、供述和辩解；  
（六）鉴定意见；  
（七）勘验检查、辨认、调查实验等笔录；  
（八）视听资料、电子数据。  
监察机关向有关单位和个人收集、调取证据时，应当告知其必须依法如实提供证据。对于不按要求提供有关材料，泄露相关信息，伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据，提供虚假情况或者阻止他人提供证据的，依法追究法律责任。  
监察机关依照监察法和本条例规定收集的证据材料，经审查符合法定要求的，在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用。

**Article 66** All materials that can be used to prove the facts of a case are evidence, including:  
(1) Physical evidence;  
(2) Documentary evidence;  
(3) Witness testimony;  
(4) Victim statements;  
(5) Statements, confessions and exculpations of the person under investigation;  
(6) Expert evaluation opinions;  
(7) Records of inspection and examination, identification, investigation experiments, etc.;  
(8) Audio-visual materials and electronic data.  
When collecting or obtaining evidence from relevant units or individuals, a supervisory organ shall inform them that they must provide evidence truthfully according to law. For those who fail to provide relevant materials as required, disclose relevant information, forge, conceal or destroy evidence, provide false information, or prevent others from providing evidence, legal liability shall be pursued according to law.  
Evidentiary materials collected by a supervisory organ in accordance with the Supervision Law and these Regulations may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings if they meet the statutory requirements after review.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　监察机关认定案件事实应当以证据为根据，全面、客观地收集、固定被调查人有无违法犯罪以及情节轻重的各种证据，形成相互印证、完整稳定的证据链。  
只有被调查人陈述或者供述，没有其他证据的，不能认定案件事实；没有被调查人陈述或者供述，证据符合法定标准的，可以认定案件事实。

**Article 67** When determining the facts of a case, a supervisory organ shall base its findings on evidence, comprehensively and objectively collecting and fixing all kinds of evidence concerning whether the person under investigation has committed illegality or crime and the seriousness of the circumstances, so as to form a mutually corroborating, complete and stable chain of evidence.  
If there is only the statement or confession of the person under investigation and no other evidence, the facts of the case shall not be determined; if there is no statement or confession of the person under investigation but the evidence meets the statutory standards, the facts of the case may be determined.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　证据必须经过查证属实，才能作为定案的根据。审查认定证据，应当结合案件的具体情况，从证据与待证事实的关联程度、各证据之间的联系、是否依照法定程序收集等方面进行综合判断。

**Article 68** Evidence must be verified as truthful before it may serve as the basis for deciding a case. In reviewing and determining evidence, comprehensive judgment shall be made in light of the specific circumstances of the case, from the aspects of the degree of relevance between the evidence and the facts to be proved, the connection among the various pieces of evidence, and whether they were collected in accordance with statutory procedures.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　监察机关调查终结的职务违法案件，应当事实清楚、证据确凿。证据确凿，应当符合下列条件：  
（一）定性处置的事实都有证据证实；  
（二）定案证据真实、合法；  
（三）据以定案的证据之间不存在无法排除的矛盾；  
（四）综合全案证据，所认定事实清晰且令人信服。

**Article 69** For a duty-related illegal case that a supervisory organ has concluded its investigation, the facts shall be clear and the evidence shall be conclusive. Evidence is conclusive if it meets the following conditions:  
(1) All facts concerning the characterization and disposition are supported by evidence;  
(2) The evidence on which the case is decided is truthful and lawful;  
(3) There are no irresolvable contradictions among the evidence relied upon to decide the case;  
(4) Considering all the evidence in the case, the facts determined are clear and convincing.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　监察机关调查终结的职务犯罪案件，应当事实清楚，证据确实、充分。证据确实、充分，应当符合下列条件：  
（一）定罪量刑的事实都有证据证明；  
（二）据以定案的证据均经法定程序查证属实；  
（三）综合全案证据，对所认定事实已排除合理怀疑。  
证据不足的，不得移送人民检察院审查起诉。

**Article 70** : For a duty-related crime case that the supervisory organ has concluded investigation, the facts shall be clear and the evidence shall be reliable and sufficient. Evidence being reliable and sufficient shall meet the following conditions:  
(1) All facts for conviction and sentencing are proved by evidence;  
(2) The evidence on which the case is determined have all been verified as true through statutory procedures;  
(3) Comprehensive evaluation of all evidence in the case has eliminated reasonable doubt regarding the facts determined.  
If the evidence is insufficient, the case shall not be transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　调查人员应当依法文明规范开展调查工作。严禁以暴力、威胁、引诱、欺骗以及非法限制人身自由等非法方法收集证据，严禁侮辱、打骂、虐待、体罚或者变相体罚被调查人、涉案人员和证人。  
监察机关应当保障被强制到案人员、被管护人员、被留置人员以及被禁闭人员的合法权益，尊重其人格和民族习俗，保障饮食、休息和安全，提供医疗服务。

**Article 71** : Investigators shall conduct investigation according to law in a civilized and standardized manner. It is strictly prohibited to collect evidence by illegal methods such as violence, threats, enticement, deception, and illegal restriction of personal freedom, and it is strictly prohibited to insult, beat, abuse, physically punish or impose disguised corporal punishment on the person under investigation, persons involved in the case, and witnesses.  
The supervisory organ shall protect the lawful rights and interests of persons compulsorily brought to the scene, persons under protective custody, persons under detention, and persons under confinement, respect their personality and ethnic customs, ensure their diet, rest, and safety, and provide medical services.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　对于调查人员采用暴力、威胁以及非法限制人身自由等非法方法收集的被调查人供述、证人证言、被害人陈述，应当依法予以排除。  
前款所称暴力的方法，是指采用殴打、违法使用戒具等方法或者变相肉刑的恶劣手段，使人遭受难以忍受的痛苦而违背意愿作出供述、证言、陈述；威胁的方法，是指采用以暴力或者严重损害本人及其近亲属合法权益等进行威胁的方法，使人遭受难以忍受的痛苦而违背意愿作出供述、证言、陈述。  
收集物证、书证不符合法定程序，可能严重影响案件公正处理的，应当予以补正或者作出合理解释；不能补正或者作出合理解释的，对该证据应当予以排除。

**Article 72** : Confessions of the person under investigation, testimony of witnesses, and statements of victims collected by investigators through illegal methods such as violence, threats, and illegal restriction of personal freedom shall be excluded according to law.  
The method of violence as mentioned in the preceding paragraph refers to the use of heinous means such as beating, illegal use of restraints, or disguised corporal punishment, causing a person to suffer unbearable pain and to make confessions, testimony, or statements against his or her will; the method of threats refers to the use of threats such as violence or serious harm to the lawful rights and interests of the person or his or her close relatives, causing a person to suffer unbearable pain and to make confessions, testimony, or statements against his or her will.  
If the collection of physical evidence or documentary evidence does not comply with statutory procedures and may seriously affect the fair handling of the case, corrections shall be made or reasonable explanations shall be provided; if corrections cannot be made or reasonable explanations cannot be provided, such evidence shall be excluded.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　监察机关监督检查、调查、案件审理、案件监督管理等部门发现监察人员在办理案件中，可能存在以非法方法收集证据情形的，应当依据职责进行调查核实。对于被调查人控告、举报调查人员采用非法方法收集证据，并提供涉嫌非法取证的人员、时间、地点、方式和内容等材料或者线索的，应当受理并进行审核。根据现有材料无法证明证据收集合法性的，应当进行调查核实。  
经调查核实，确认或者不能排除以非法方法收集证据的，对有关证据依法予以排除，不得作为案件定性处置、移送审查起诉的依据。认定调查人员非法取证的，应当依法处理，另行指派调查人员重新调查取证。  
监察机关接到对下级监察机关调查人员采用非法方法收集证据的控告、举报，可以直接进行调查核实，也可以交由下级监察机关调查核实。交由下级监察机关调查核实的，下级监察机关应当及时将调查结果报告上级监察机关。

**Article 73** : The departments of supervision and inspection, investigation, case adjudication, and case supervision and management of the supervisory organ, upon discovering that there may be circumstances where investigators collect evidence by illegal methods in handling cases, shall conduct investigation and verification according to their duties. Where the person under investigation accuses or reports that investigators used illegal methods to collect evidence, and provides materials or clues such as the suspected illegal evidence collectors, time, place, method, and content, such accusation or report shall be accepted and reviewed. If the legality of evidence collection cannot be proved based on the existing materials, investigation and verification shall be conducted.  
After investigation and verification, if it is confirmed or cannot be ruled out that evidence was collected by illegal methods, the relevant evidence shall be excluded according to law and shall not be used as a basis for case characterization, disposition, or transfer for examination and prosecution. If investigators are determined to have collected evidence illegally, they shall be dealt with according to law, and other investigators shall be designated to conduct investigation and evidence collection anew.  
If a supervisory organ receives accusations or reports that investigators of a lower-level supervisory organ used illegal methods to collect evidence, it may directly conduct investigation and verification, or may refer the matter to the lower-level supervisory organ for investigation and verification. If referred to the lower-level supervisory organ for investigation and verification, the lower-level supervisory organ shall promptly report the investigation results to the higher-level supervisory organ.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　对收集的证据材料及扣押的财物应当妥善保管，严格履行交接、调用手续，定期对账核实，不得违规使用、调换、损毁或者自行处理。

**Article 74** : The collected evidentiary materials and seized property shall be properly kept, the procedures for handover and transfer shall be strictly followed, and regular account verification shall be conducted. They shall not be used, exchanged, damaged, or disposed of in violation of regulations.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　监察机关对行政机关在行政执法和查办案件中收集的物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据，勘验、检查等笔录，以及鉴定意见等证据材料，经审查符合法定要求的，可以作为证据使用。  
根据法律、行政法规规定行使国家行政管理职权的组织在行政执法和查办案件中收集的证据材料，视为行政机关收集的证据材料。

**Article 75** : For the physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, electronic data, records of inspection and examination, and expert evaluation opinions collected by administrative organs in administrative law enforcement and case investigation, if they meet the statutory requirements after review, the supervisory organ may use them as evidence.  
Evidence materials collected by organizations exercising state administrative powers according to laws and administrative regulations in administrative law enforcement and case investigation shall be regarded as evidence materials collected by administrative organs.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　监察机关对人民法院、人民检察院、公安机关、国家安全机关等在刑事诉讼中收集的物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据，勘验、检查、辨认、侦查实验等笔录，以及鉴定意见等证据材料，经审查符合法定要求的，可以作为证据使用。  
监察机关办理职务违法案件，对于人民法院生效刑事判决、裁定和人民检察院不起诉决定采信的证据材料，可以直接作为证据使用。

**Article 76** : For the physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, electronic data, records of inspection, examination, identification, and investigation experiments, and expert evaluation opinions collected by the People's Courts, People's Procuratorates, public security organs, and state security organs in criminal proceedings, if they meet the statutory requirements after review, the supervisory organ may use them as evidence.  
In handling duty-related illegal cases, the supervisory organ may directly use as evidence the evidentiary materials accepted by the effective criminal judgments and rulings of the People's Courts and the decisions of non-prosecution by the People's Procuratorates.

#### 第三节　谈话

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　监察机关对涉嫌职务违法的监察对象，可以依法进行谈话，要求其如实说明情况或者作出陈述。  
谈话应当个别进行。负责谈话的人员不得少于二人。

**Article 77** : The supervisory organ may conduct talks with supervision subjects suspected of duty-related illegal acts according to law, requiring them to truthfully explain the circumstances or make statements.  
Talks shall be conducted individually. The number of persons responsible for the talk shall not be less than two.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　对一般性问题线索的处置，可以采取谈话方式进行，对监察对象给予警示、批评、教育。谈话应当在监察机关谈话场所、具备安全保障条件的工作地点等场所进行，明确告知谈话事项，注重谈清问题、取得教育效果。

**Article 78** : For the handling of clues of general issues, the method of talk may be adopted to give warnings, criticism, and education to the supervision subject. The talk shall be conducted at the talk venue of the supervisory organ, workplaces with safety and security conditions, or other such places, clearly informing the matters of the talk, focusing on clarifying the issues and achieving educational effects.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　采取谈话方式处置问题线索的，经审批可以由监察人员或者委托被谈话人所在单位主要负责人等进行谈话。  
监察机关谈话应当形成谈话笔录或者记录。谈话结束后，可以根据需要要求被谈话人在十五个工作日以内作出书面说明。被谈话人应当在书面说明每页签名，修改的地方也应当签名。  
委托谈话的，受委托人应当在收到委托函后的十五个工作日以内进行谈话。谈话结束后及时形成谈话情况材料报送监察机关，必要时附被谈话人的书面说明。

**Article 79** : If the method of talk is adopted to handle clues of issues, after approval, the talk may be conducted by supervisory personnel or by commissioning the principal responsible person of the unit where the person being talked to belongs, etc.  
The talk conducted by the supervisory organ shall produce transcripts or records. After the talk concludes, the person being talked to may be required to provide a written explanation within 15 working days as needed. The person being talked to shall sign each page of the written explanation, and also sign any modifications.  
If the talk is commissioned, the commissioned person shall conduct the talk within 15 working days after receiving the commission letter. After the talk concludes, materials on the talk situation shall be promptly formed and submitted to the supervisory organ, with the written explanation of the person being talked to attached if necessary.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　监察机关开展初步核实工作，一般不与被核查人接触；确有需要与被核查人谈话的，应当按规定报批。

**Article 80** : When carrying out preliminary verification work, the supervisory organ shall generally not contact the person being verified; if it is truly necessary to have a talk with the person being verified, approval shall be sought in accordance with regulations.

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　监察机关对涉嫌职务违法的被调查人立案后，可以依法进行谈话。  
与被调查人首次谈话时，应当出示《被调查人权利义务告知书》，由其签名、捺指印。被调查人拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。对于被调查人未被限制人身自由的，应当在首次谈话时出具《谈话通知书》。  
与涉嫌严重职务违法的被调查人进行谈话的，应当全程同步录音录像，并告知被调查人。告知情况应当在录音录像中予以反映，并在笔录中记明。

**Article 81** : After filing a case against a person under investigation suspected of duty-related illegal acts, the supervisory organ may conduct talks according to law.  
During the first talk with the person under investigation, the 'Notice of Rights and Obligations of the Person under Investigation' shall be presented, and the person shall sign and affix a fingerprint. If the person under investigation refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigators shall note it on the document. For persons under investigation whose personal freedom is not restricted, the 'Notice of Talk' shall be issued during the first talk.  
If conducting a talk with a person under investigation suspected of serious duty-related illegal acts, simultaneous audio and video recording shall be made throughout the process, and the person under investigation shall be informed. The fact of such notification shall be reflected in the audio and video recording and noted in the transcript.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　立案后，与被责令候查人员或者未被限制人身自由的被调查人谈话的，应当在具备安全保障条件的场所进行。  
调查人员按规定通知被调查人所在单位派员或者被调查人家属陪同被调查人到指定场所的，应当与陪同人员办理交接手续，填写《陪送交接单》。

**Article 82** : After case filing, if conducting a talk with a person ordered to await investigation or a person under investigation whose personal freedom is not restricted, the talk shall be conducted at a place with safety and security conditions.  
If investigators, in accordance with regulations, notify the unit of the person under investigation to send a representative or the family members of the person under investigation to accompany him or her to the designated place, handover procedures shall be conducted with the accompanying personnel, and the 'Accompaniment Handover Form' shall be filled out.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　调查人员与被强制到案人员、被管护人员、被留置人员或者被禁闭人员谈话的，按照法定程序在执行相关监察强制措施的场所进行。  
与在押的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人谈话的，应当持以监察机关名义出具的介绍信、工作证件，商请有关案件主管机关依法协助办理。  
与在看守所、监狱服刑的人员谈话的，应当持以监察机关名义出具的介绍信、工作证件办理。

**Article 83** : If investigators conduct a talk with a person compulsorily brought to the scene, a person under protective custody, a person under detention, or a person under confinement, the talk shall be conducted at the place where the relevant supervisory compulsory measures are executed in accordance with statutory procedures.  
If conducting a talk with a criminal suspect or defendant in custody, the investigators shall hold a letter of introduction and work credentials issued in the name of the supervisory organ, and consult with the competent organ of the relevant case for assistance in handling according to law.
