If conducting a talk with a person serving a sentence in a detention center or prison, the investigators shall hold a letter of introduction and work credentials issued in the name of the supervisory organ to handle the matter.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　与被调查人进行谈话，应当合理安排时间、控制时长，保证其饮食和必要的休息时间。

**Article 84** : When conducting a talk with a person under investigation, the time shall be reasonably arranged, the duration controlled, and his or her meals and necessary rest time shall be ensured.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　谈话笔录应当在谈话现场制作。笔录应当详细具体，如实反映谈话情况。笔录制作完成后，应当交给被调查人核对。被调查人没有阅读能力的，应当向其宣读。  
笔录记载有遗漏或者差错的，应当补充或者更正，由被调查人在补充或者更正处捺指印。被调查人核对无误后，应当在笔录中逐页签名、捺指印。被调查人拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在笔录中记明。调查人员也应当在笔录中签名。

**Article 85** : The talk transcript shall be made at the talk site. The transcript shall be detailed and specific, truthfully reflecting the talk situation. After the transcript is made, it shall be handed to the person under investigation for verification. If the person under investigation lacks reading ability, it shall be read to him or her.  
If the transcript contains omissions or errors, supplements or corrections shall be made, and the person under investigation shall affix a fingerprint at the place of supplement or correction. After verification with no errors, the person under investigation shall sign and affix a fingerprint on each page of the transcript. If the person under investigation refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigators shall note it in the transcript. The investigators shall also sign the transcript.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　被调查人请求自行书写说明材料的，应当准许。必要时，调查人员可以要求被调查人自行书写说明材料。  
被调查人应当在说明材料上逐页签名、捺指印，在末页写明日期。对说明材料有修改的，在修改之处应当捺指印。说明材料应当由二名调查人员接收，在首页记明接收的日期并签名。

**Article 86** : If the person under investigation requests to write explanatory materials by himself or herself, permission shall be granted. When necessary, investigators may require the person under investigation to write explanatory materials on his or her own.  
The person under investigation shall sign and affix a fingerprint on each page of the explanatory materials, and write the date on the last page. If there are modifications to the explanatory materials, a fingerprint shall be affixed at the place of modification. The explanatory materials shall be received by two investigators, who shall record the date of receipt on the first page and sign.

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　[本条例第八十一条](#art-81)至第八十六条的规定，也适用于在初步核实中开展的谈话。

**Article 87** : The provisions of Articles 81 through 86 of these Regulations also apply to talks conducted in preliminary verification.

#### 第四节　讯问

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　监察机关对涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人，可以依法进行讯问，要求其如实供述涉嫌犯罪的情况。

**Article 88** : The supervisory organ may interrogate a person under investigation suspected of duty-related crimes according to law, requiring him or her to truthfully confess the suspected criminal circumstances.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　讯问被管护人员、被留置人员，应当在留置场所进行。

**Article 89** : Interrogation of a person under protective custody or a person under detention shall be conducted at the place of detention.

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　讯问应当个别进行，调查人员不得少于二人。  
首次讯问时，应当向被讯问人出示《被调查人权利义务告知书》，由其签名、捺指印。被讯问人拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。被讯问人未被限制人身自由的，应当在首次讯问时向其出具《讯问通知书》。  
讯问一般按照下列顺序进行：  
（一）核实被讯问人的基本情况，包括姓名、曾用名、出生年月日、户籍地、身份证件号码、民族、职业、政治面貌、文化程度、工作单位及职务、住所、家庭情况、社会经历，是否属于党代表大会代表、人大代表、政协委员，是否受到过党纪政务处分，是否受到过刑事处罚等；  
（二）告知被讯问人如实供述自己罪行可以依法从宽处理和认罪认罚的法律规定；  
（三）讯问被讯问人是否有犯罪行为，让其陈述有罪的事实或者无罪的辩解，应当允许其连贯陈述。  
调查人员的提问应当与调查的案件相关。被讯问人对调查人员的提问应当如实回答。调查人员对被讯问人的辩解，应当如实记录，认真查核。  
发现涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人自动投案、如实供述监察机关还未掌握的违法犯罪行为、揭发他人犯罪行为或者提供重要线索等，可能具有自首、立功等法定情节的，应当依法及时讯问。对某一具体涉嫌职务犯罪事实初步查清后，应当在全面梳理分析在案证据的基础上进行讯问。  
讯问时，应当告知被讯问人将进行全程同步录音录像。告知情况应当在录音录像中予以反映，并在笔录中记明。

**Article 90** : Interrogations shall be conducted individually, and there shall be no fewer than two investigators.  
At the first interrogation, the investigator shall present the "Notice of Rights and Obligations of the Investigated Person" to the interrogated person, who shall sign and affix a fingerprint on it. If the interrogated person refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigator shall record this in the document. If the interrogated person's personal freedom is not restricted, the "Notice of Interrogation" shall be issued to him or her at the first interrogation.  
Interrogations shall generally be conducted in the following order:  
(1) verifying the basic information of the interrogated person, including name, former name, date of birth, place of household registration, identity document number, ethnicity, occupation, political status, educational level, work unit and position, domicile, family situation, social experience, whether he or she is a representative of a Party congress, a deputy to a People's Congress, or a member of a Chinese People's Political Consultative Conference committee, whether he or she has received Party discipline or government disciplinary sanctions, and whether he or she has received criminal punishment, etc.;  
(2) informing the interrogated person of the legal provisions on lenient treatment according to law for truthful confession of his or her crimes and on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment;  
(3) interrogating the interrogated person as to whether he or she has committed a criminal act, and allowing him or her to make a coherent statement of the facts of guilt or a defense of innocence.  
The investigator's questions shall be relevant to the case under investigation. The interrogated person shall answer the investigator's questions truthfully. The investigator shall truthfully record and carefully verify the interrogated person's defense.  
Where it is discovered that the investigated person suspected of duty-related crimes voluntarily surrenders, truthfully confesses criminal or illegal conduct not yet known to the supervisory organ, exposes criminal conduct of others, or provides important leads, etc., and may have statutory circumstances such as voluntary surrender or meritorious service, the interrogation shall be conducted in a timely manner according to law. After preliminary verification of a specific fact of suspected duty-related crime, interrogation shall be conducted on the basis of a comprehensive analysis of the evidence on record.  
During interrogation, the interrogated person shall be informed that simultaneous audio and video recording will be conducted throughout the process. The notification shall be reflected in the recording and noted in the record.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　[本条例第八十二条](#art-82)至第八十六条的要求，也适用于讯问。

**Article 91** : The requirements of Articles 82 through 86 of these Regulations shall also apply to interrogations.

#### 第五节　询问

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　监察机关按规定报批后，可以依法对证人、被害人等人员进行询问，了解核实有关问题或者案件情况。

**Article 92** : After reporting for approval as prescribed, the supervisory organ may, according to law, conduct inquiries with witnesses, victims, and other persons to learn about and verify relevant issues or case circumstances.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　证人未被限制人身自由的，可以在其工作地点、住所或者其提出的地点进行询问，也可以通知其到指定地点接受询问。到证人提出的地点或者调查人员指定的地点进行询问的，应当在笔录中记明。  
调查人员认为有必要或者证人提出需要由所在单位派员或者其家属陪同到询问地点的，应当办理交接手续并填写《陪送交接单》。

**Article 93** : If a witness's personal freedom is not restricted, the inquiry may be conducted at his or her workplace, residence, or a location proposed by him or her, or he or she may be notified to go to a designated location to accept the inquiry. If the inquiry is conducted at a location proposed by the witness or designated by the investigator, this shall be noted in the record.  
If the investigator deems it necessary or the witness proposes that he or she needs to be accompanied to the inquiry location by a person from his or her work unit or a family member, handover procedures shall be completed and the "Escort Handover Form" shall be filled out.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　询问应当个别进行。负责询问的调查人员不得少于二人。  
首次询问时，应当向证人出示《证人权利义务告知书》，由其签名、捺指印。证人拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。证人未被限制人身自由的，应当在首次询问时向其出具《询问通知书》。  
询问时，应当核实证人身份，问明证人的基本情况，告知证人应当如实提供证据、证言，以及作伪证或者隐匿证据应当承担的法律责任。不得向证人泄露案情，不得采用非法方法获取证言。  
询问重大或者有社会影响案件的重要证人，应当对询问过程全程同步录音录像，并告知证人。告知情况应当在录音录像中予以反映，并在笔录中记明。

**Article 94** : Inquiries shall be conducted individually. There shall be no fewer than two investigators responsible for the inquiry.  
At the first inquiry, the investigator shall present the "Notice of Rights and Obligations of a Witness" to the witness, who shall sign and affix a fingerprint on it. If the witness refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigator shall record this in the document. If the witness's personal freedom is not restricted, the "Notice of Inquiry" shall be issued to him or her at the first inquiry.  
During the inquiry, the identity of the witness shall be verified, the basic information of the witness shall be asked, and the witness shall be informed that he or she shall truthfully provide evidence and testimony and the legal liability for giving false testimony or concealing evidence. The details of the case shall not be disclosed to the witness, and testimony shall not be obtained by illegal means.  
When inquiring with an important witness in a major case or a case with social impact, simultaneous audio and video recording shall be conducted throughout the inquiry process, and the witness shall be informed. The notification shall be reflected in the recording and noted in the record.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　询问未成年人，应当通知其法定代理人到场。无法通知或者法定代理人不能到场的，应当通知未成年人的其他成年亲属或者所在学校、居住地基层组织的代表等有关人员到场。询问结束后，由法定代理人或者有关人员在笔录中签名。调查人员应当将到场情况记录在案。  
询问聋、哑人，应当有通晓聋、哑手势的人员参加。调查人员应当在笔录中记明证人的聋、哑情况，以及翻译人员的姓名、工作单位和职业。询问不通晓当地通用语言、文字的证人，应当有翻译人员。询问结束后，由翻译人员在笔录中签名。

**Article 95** : When inquiring with a minor, his or her legal representative shall be notified to be present. If it is impossible to notify or the legal representative cannot be present, other adult relatives of the minor or representatives from his or her school or the basic-level organization of the place of residence shall be notified to be present. After the inquiry is completed, the legal representative or the relevant person shall sign the record. The investigator shall record the presence in the file.  
When inquiring with a deaf or mute person, a person who understands sign language for the deaf and mute shall participate. The investigator shall note in the record the witness's deaf or mute condition, as well as the name, work unit, and occupation of the interpreter. When inquiring with a witness who does not understand the common language and characters used locally, an interpreter shall be provided. After the inquiry is completed, the interpreter shall sign the record.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　凡是知道案件情况的人，都有如实作证的义务。对故意提供虚假证言的证人，应当依法追究法律责任。  
证人或者其他任何人不得帮助被调查人伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据或者串供，不得实施其他干扰调查活动的行为。

**Article 96** : Every person who knows the circumstances of a case has the obligation to testify truthfully. A witness who intentionally provides false testimony shall be investigated for legal liability according to law.  
No witness or any other person may assist the investigated person in forging, concealing, or destroying evidence, or in colluding to make false statements, or engage in any other acts that interfere with the investigation activities.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　证人、鉴定人、被害人因作证，本人或者近亲属人身安全面临危险，向监察机关请求保护的，监察机关应当受理并及时进行审查；对于确实存在人身安全危险的，监察机关应当采取必要的保护措施。监察机关发现存在上述情形的，应当主动采取保护措施。  
监察机关可以采取下列一项或者多项保护措施：  
（一）不公开真实姓名、住址和工作单位等个人信息；  
（二）禁止特定的人员接触证人、鉴定人、被害人及其近亲属；  
（三）对人身和住宅采取专门性保护措施；  
（四）其他必要的保护措施。  
依法决定不公开证人、鉴定人、被害人的真实姓名、住址和工作单位等个人信息的，可以在询问笔录等法律文书、证据材料中使用化名。但是应当另行书面说明使用化名的情况并标明密级，单独成卷。  
监察机关采取保护措施需要协助的，可以提请公安机关等有关单位和要求有关个人依法予以协助。

**Article 97** : If a witness, expert evaluator, or victim, due to testifying, faces danger to the personal safety of himself, herself, or his or her close relatives, and requests protection from the supervisory organ, the supervisory organ shall accept the request and conduct a timely review; if there is indeed a danger to personal safety, the supervisory organ shall take necessary protection measures. If the supervisory organ discovers the existence of the above circumstances, it shall proactively take protection measures.  
The supervisory organ may take one or more of the following protection measures:  
(1) not disclosing personal information such as real name, address, and work unit;  
(2) prohibiting specific persons from contacting the witness, expert evaluator, victim, and their close relatives;  
(3) taking special protection measures for persons and residences;  
(4) other necessary protection measures.  
Where it is decided according to law not to disclose the personal information such as the real name, address, and work unit of a witness, expert evaluator, or victim, pseudonyms may be used in legal documents such as inquiry records and evidentiary materials. However, a separate written explanation of the use of pseudonyms shall be made, the classification level shall be indicated, and it shall be kept in a separate volume.  
If the supervisory organ needs assistance in taking protection measures, it may request assistance from public security organs and other relevant units and require relevant individuals to provide assistance according to law.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　[本条例第八十三条](#art-83)至第八十六条的要求，也适用于询问。询问重要涉案人员，根据情况适用[本条例第八十二条](#art-82)的规定。  
询问被害人，适用询问证人的规定。

**Article 98** : The requirements of Articles 83 through 86 of these Regulations shall also apply to inquiries. When inquiring with important persons involved in the case, the provisions of Article 82 of these Regulations shall apply according to the circumstances.  
Inquiries with victims shall be governed by the provisions on inquiries with witnesses.

#### 第六节　强制到案

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　监察机关调查严重职务违法或者职务犯罪，对于经通知无正当理由不到案的被调查人，经依法审批，可以强制其到监察机关谈话场所或者留置场所接受调查。  
首次通知到案一般应当以书面方式，确因情况紧急无法书面通知的，可以通过电话等方式通知，并将相关情况制作工作记录。  
采取强制到案措施时，调查人员不得少于二人，应当向被强制到案人员出具《强制到案决定书》。

**Article 99** : Where a supervisory organ investigates a serious duty-related violation of law or duty-related crime, and the investigated person fails to appear after being notified without justifiable reasons, the supervisory organ may, after approval according to law, compel such person to go to the supervisory organ's talk venue or detention venue to accept investigation.  
The first notice to appear shall generally be in writing. If it is truly impossible to notify in writing due to urgent circumstances, notification may be made by telephone or other means, and a work record shall be made of the relevant circumstances.  
When taking the compulsory appearance measure, there shall be no fewer than two investigators, and the "Decision on Compulsory Appearance" shall be issued to the person subject to compulsory appearance.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　监察机关应当立即将被强制到案人员送至监察机关谈话场所或者留置场所。强制到案的时间自被强制到案人员到达相关场所时起算。  
被强制到案人员到案后，应当要求其在《强制到案决定书》上填写到案时间，并签名、捺指印；强制到案结束后，应当要求被强制到案人员在《强制到案决定书》上填写结束时间，并签名、捺指印。被强制到案人员拒绝填写或者签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
一次强制到案持续的时间不得超过十二小时；依法需要采取管护或者留置措施的，按规定报批后，强制到案持续的时间不得超过二十四小时。两次强制到案间隔的时间不得少于二十四小时，不得以连续强制到案的方式变相拘禁被调查人。两次强制到案的间隔时间从第一次强制到案结束时起算。

**Article 100** : The supervisory organ shall immediately transport the person subject to compulsory appearance to its talk venue or detention venue. The time of compulsory appearance shall be calculated from the moment the person subject to compulsory appearance arrives at the relevant venue.  
After the person subject to compulsory appearance appears, he or she shall be required to fill in the time of appearance on the "Decision on Compulsory Appearance," and sign and affix a fingerprint; after the compulsory appearance ends, he or she shall be required to fill in the end time on the "Decision on Compulsory Appearance," and sign and affix a fingerprint. If the person subject to compulsory appearance refuses to fill in or sign and affix a fingerprint, the investigator shall record this in the document.  
The duration of a single compulsory appearance shall not exceed twelve hours; if it is necessary to take custody or detention measures according to law, after reporting and approval as prescribed, the duration of compulsory appearance shall not exceed twenty-four hours. The interval between two compulsory appearances shall be no less than twenty-four hours, and the investigated person shall not be detained in a disguised form by means of continuous compulsory appearances. The interval between two compulsory appearances shall be calculated from the end of the first compulsory appearance.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　监察机关强制被调查人到案后，应当对涉嫌职务违法的被调查人及时谈话，对涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人及时讯问。

**Article 101** : After the supervisory organ compels the investigated person to appear, it shall promptly conduct a talk with the investigated person suspected of duty-related violation of law, and promptly interrogate the investigated person suspected of duty-related crime.

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　监察机关在强制到案期限内未作出采取其他监察强制措施决定的，强制到案期满，应当立即结束强制到案。

**Article 102** : If the supervisory organ does not make a decision to take other supervisory compulsory measures within the period of compulsory appearance, upon expiration of the compulsory appearance period, the compulsory appearance shall be immediately terminated.

#### 第七节　责令候查

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　监察机关调查严重职务违法或者职务犯罪，对于符合[监察法第二十三条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-23)第一款规定的，经依法审批，可以对被调查人采取责令候查措施。

**Article 103** : Where a supervisory organ investigates a serious duty-related violation of law or duty-related crime, and the conditions provided for in Article 23, Paragraph 1 of the Supervision Law are met, the supervisory organ may, after approval according to law, take the measure of ordered awaiting investigation against the investigated person.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　采取责令候查措施时，调查人员不得少于二人，应当向被责令候查人员宣布《责令候查决定书》，出示《被责令候查人员权利义务告知书》，由被责令候查人员签名、捺指印，要求其遵守[监察法第二十三条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-23)第二款的规定，告知其违反规定应负的法律责任。被责令候查人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
监察机关将其他监察强制措施变更为责令候查措施的，应当按照前款规定履行权利义务告知程序。  
责令候查最长不得超过十二个月，自向被责令候查人员宣布之日起算。

**Article 104** : When taking the measure of ordered awaiting investigation, there shall be no fewer than two investigators, they shall announce the "Decision on Ordered Awaiting Investigation" to the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation, present the "Notice of Rights and Obligations of a Person Subject to Ordered Awaiting Investigation," and request the person to sign and affix a fingerprint, require him or her to comply with the provisions of Article 23, Paragraph 2 of the Supervision Law, and inform him or her of the legal liability for violating the provisions. If the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigator shall record this in the document.  
If the supervisory organ changes other supervisory compulsory measures to the measure of ordered awaiting investigation, the procedure for notification of rights and obligations as prescribed in the preceding paragraph shall be followed.  
The maximum duration of ordered awaiting investigation shall not exceed twelve months, calculated from the date of announcement to the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　除无法通知的以外，监察机关应当在采取责令候查措施后二十四小时以内，通知被责令候查人员所在单位和家属。当面通知的，由有关人员在《责令候查通知书》上签名。无法当面通知的，可以先以电话等方式通知，并通过邮寄、转交等方式送达《责令候查通知书》，要求有关人员在《责令候查通知书》上签名。有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 105** : Except where notification is impossible, the supervisory organ shall, within twenty-four hours after taking the measure of ordered awaiting investigation, notify the work unit and family members of the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation. If notification is made in person, the relevant person shall sign the "Notice of Ordered Awaiting Investigation." If notification cannot be made in person, the notice may first be made by telephone or other means, and the "Notice of Ordered Awaiting Investigation" shall be served by mail or hand delivery, etc., requesting the relevant person to sign on the "Notice of Ordered Awaiting Investigation." If the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigator shall record this in the document.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　责令候查应当由决定采取责令候查措施的监察机关执行。  
执行责令候查的监察机关应当履行下列职责：  
（一）监督、考察被责令候查人员遵守有关规定，及时掌握其活动、住址、工作单位、联系方式及变动情况；  
（二）审批被责令候查人员离开所居住的直辖市、设区的市的城市市区或者不设区的市、县的辖区（以下统称所居住的市、县）的申请；  
（三）被责令候查人员违反应当遵守的规定的，及时制止或者纠正；  
（四）会同被责令候查人员所在单位、家属等对被责令候查人员开展思想教育、心理疏导工作。

**Article 106** : Ordered awaiting investigation shall be executed by the supervisory organ that decided to take the measure.  
The supervisory organ executing the ordered awaiting investigation shall perform the following duties:  
(1) supervise and inspect the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation to comply with relevant provisions, and promptly keep abreast of his or her activities, address, work unit, contact information, and changes thereof;  
(2) examine and approve applications by the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation to leave the city urban area of the municipality directly under the Central Government or the city with districts where he or she resides, or the jurisdiction of the city without districts or county where he or she resides (hereinafter collectively referred to as the city or county of residence);  
(3) promptly stop or rectify violations by the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation of the provisions he or she shall comply with;  
(4) jointly with the work unit and family members of the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation, carry out ideological education and psychological counseling for the person.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　被责令候查人员未经批准不得离开所居住的市、县。确有正当理由需要离开的，应当经决定采取责令候查措施的监察机关批准。  
在同一直辖市、设区的市内跨区活动的，不属于离开所居住的市、县。  
本条第一款所称正当理由，是指就医、就学、参与诉讼、往返居住地与工作地、处理重要家庭事务或者参加重要公务、商务活动等。

**Article 107** : A person subject to ordered awaiting investigation may not leave the city or county of residence without approval. If there is indeed a justifiable reason to leave, approval shall be obtained from the supervisory organ that decided to take the measure of ordered awaiting investigation.  
Activities across districts within the same municipality directly under the Central Government or city with districts shall not be considered leaving the city or county of residence.  
The term "justifiable reason" as used in the first paragraph of this Article refers to seeking medical treatment, attending school, participating in litigation, commuting between residence and workplace, handling important family affairs, or participating in important official or business activities, etc.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　被责令候查人员需要离开所居住的市、县的，应当向监察机关提出书面申请，并注明事由、目的地、路线、交通方式、往返日期、联系方式等。监察机关应当自收到书面申请之日起三日以内作出决定。被责令候查人员有紧急事由，无法及时提出书面申请的，可以先行通过电话等方式提出申请，并及时补办书面申请手续。  
监察机关批准被责令候查人员离开所居住的市、县的申请后，应当告知其遵守下列要求：  
（一）保持联系方式畅通，并在接到通知后及时到案接受调查；  
（二）严格按照批准的地点、路线、往返日期出行；  
（三）不得从事妨碍调查的活动；  
（四）返回居住地后及时向执行机关报告。  
对于被责令候查人员因正常工作或者生活需要经常性离开所居住的市、县的，可以根据情况简化批准程序，一次性审批其在特定期间内按照批准的地点、路线出行。

**Article 108** : If a person subject to ordered awaiting investigation needs to leave the city or county of residence, he or she shall submit a written application to the supervisory organ, specifying the reason, destination, route, means of transportation, date of departure and return, contact information, etc. The supervisory organ shall make a decision within three days from the date of receipt of the written application. If the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation has an urgent reason and cannot submit a written application in a timely manner, he or she may first submit the application by telephone or other means, and promptly complete the written application formalities afterwards.  
After the supervisory organ approves the application for the person subject to ordered awaiting investigation to leave the city or county of residence, it shall inform him or her to comply with the following requirements:  
(1) keep contact information open, and promptly appear for investigation upon receiving notice;  
(2) travel strictly in accordance with the approved location, route, and dates of departure and return;  
(3) not engage in activities that hinder the investigation;  
(4) report to the executing organ promptly after returning to the place of residence.  
For a person subject to ordered awaiting investigation who needs to frequently leave the city or county of residence due to normal work or life needs, the approval procedures may be simplified according to the circumstances, and one-time approval may be granted for him or her to travel in accordance with the approved location and route within a specified period.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　被责令候查人员具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第二十三条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-23)第三款所规定的违反责令候查规定，情节严重：  
（一）企图逃跑、自杀的；  
（二）实施伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据或者串供、干扰证人作证行为，严重影响调查工作正常进行的；  
（三）对举报人、控告人、被害人、证人、鉴定人等相关人员实施打击报复的；  
（四）未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县，严重影响调查工作正常进行，或者两次未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县的；  
（五）经通知无正当理由不到案，严重影响调查工作正常进行，或者两次经通知无正当理由不到案的；  
（六）住址、工作单位和联系方式等发生变动，未按规定向监察机关报告，导致无法通知到案，严重影响调查工作正常进行的。  
依照[监察法第二十三条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-23)第一款第三项规定被责令候查的人员，违反责令候查规定，情节严重，依法应予留置的，省级监察机关应当报请国家监察委员会批准，设区的市级以下监察机关应当逐级报送省级监察机关批准。

**Article 109** : A person subject to ordered awaiting investigation who falls under any of the following circumstances may be found to have violated the provisions on ordered awaiting investigation as specified in Article 23, Paragraph 3 of the Supervision Law, and the circumstances are serious:  
(1) attempting to escape or commit suicide;  
(2) engaging in conduct such as forging, concealing, or destroying evidence, or colluding to make false statements, or interfering with witness testimony, seriously affecting the normal progress of the investigation;  
(3) retaliating against relevant persons such as informants, accusers, victims, witnesses, or expert evaluators;  
(4) leaving the city or county of residence without approval, seriously affecting the normal progress of the investigation, or leaving the city or county of residence without approval on two occasions;  
(5) failing to appear after being notified without justifiable reasons, seriously affecting the normal progress of the investigation, or failing to appear after being notified without justifiable reasons on two occasions;  
(6) changing the address, work unit, or contact information, etc., and failing to report to the supervisory organ as required, resulting in inability to notify the person to appear, seriously affecting the normal progress of the investigation.  
Where a person subject to ordered awaiting investigation in accordance with Article 23, Paragraph 1, Item 3 of the Supervision Law violates the provisions on ordered awaiting investigation with serious circumstances, and should be detained according to law, the provincial-level supervisory organ shall report to the National Commission of Supervision for approval, and supervisory organs at the level of a city with districts or below shall report level by level to the provincial-level supervisory organ for approval.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　被管护人员、被留置人员、被禁闭人员及其近亲属向监察机关申请变更为责令候查措施的，应当以书面方式提出。监察机关收到申请后，应当在三日以内作出决定。经审查，符合责令候查条件的，可以将管护、留置或者禁闭措施依法变更为责令候查措施；不符合责令候查条件的，应当告知申请人，并说明不同意的理由。

**Article 110** Where a person under protective custody, a person under detention, a person under confinement, or a close relative thereof applies to a supervisory organ for modification of the measure to the measure of ordered to await investigation, such application shall be made in writing. After receiving the application, the supervisory organ shall make a decision within three days. Upon review, if the conditions for ordered to await investigation are met, the measures of protective custody, detention, or confinement may be modified to the measure of ordered to await investigation according to law; if the conditions are not met, the applicant shall be informed and the reasons for disapproval shall be explained.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　对被责令候查人员不需要继续采取责令候查措施或者责令候查期满的，应当按规定报批后解除责令候查措施。调查人员应当向被责令候查人员宣布《解除责令候查决定书》，由其签名、捺指印。被责令候查人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
解除责令候查措施的，应当及时通知被责令候查人员所在单位和家属。当面通知的，由有关人员在《解除责令候查通知书》上签名。无法当面通知的，可以先以电话等方式通知，并通过邮寄、转交等方式送达《解除责令候查通知书》，要求有关人员在《解除责令候查通知书》上签名。有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 111** Where the measure of ordered to await investigation is no longer required for the person subject thereto or the period of ordered to await investigation expires, the measure of ordered to await investigation shall be lifted after approval according to the relevant provisions. The investigation personnel shall announce the Decision on Lifting the Measure of Ordered to Await Investigation to the person subject to ordered to await investigation, and request that person to sign and affix a fingerprint. If the person subject to ordered to await investigation refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.  
Where the measure of ordered to await investigation is lifted, the unit and family members of the person subject to ordered to await investigation shall be notified promptly. If notification is made in person, the relevant person shall sign the Notice on Lifting the Measure of Ordered to Await Investigation. If in-person notification is not possible, the notice may be given first by telephone or other means, and the Notice on Lifting the Measure of Ordered to Await Investigation shall be served by mail, forwarding, or other means, with the relevant person required to sign it. If the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　案件依法移送人民检察院审查起诉的，责令候查措施自移送之日自动解除，不再办理解除法律手续。

**Article 112** Where a case is transferred to a People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution according to law, the measure of ordered to await investigation shall be automatically lifted from the date of transfer, and no further formalities for lifting shall be required.

#### 第八节　管护

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　监察机关对于符合[监察法第二十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-25)第一款规定的未被留置人员，经依法审批，可以对其采取管护措施。

**Article 113** A supervisory organ may, after approval according to law, take protective custody measures against a person who is not under detention and meets the conditions prescribed in Article 25, paragraph 1 of the Supervision Law.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　采取管护措施时，调查人员不得少于二人，应当向被管护人员宣布《管护决定书》，告知被管护人员权利义务，要求其在《管护决定书》上签名、捺指印。被管护人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 114** When taking a protective custody measure, there shall be no fewer than two investigation personnel, who shall announce the Decision on Protective Custody to the person subject to protective custody, inform the person of his or her rights and obligations, and request that the person sign and affix a fingerprint on the Decision on Protective Custody. If the person subject to protective custody refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　采取管护措施后，应当立即将被管护人员送留置场所，至迟不得超过二十四小时。

**Article 115** After a protective custody measure is taken, the person subject to protective custody shall be immediately sent to a detention facility, no later than twenty-four hours.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　采取管护措施后，应当在二十四小时以内通知被管护人员所在单位和家属。当面通知的，由有关人员在《管护通知书》上签名。无法当面通知的，可以先以电话等方式通知，并通过邮寄、转交等方式送达《管护通知书》，要求有关人员在《管护通知书》上签名。有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
因可能伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据，干扰证人作证或者串供等有碍调查情形而不宜通知的，应当按规定报批，记录在案。有碍调查的情形消失后，应当立即通知被管护人员所在单位和家属。

**Article 116** After a protective custody measure is taken, the unit and family members of the person subject to protective custody shall be notified within twenty-four hours. If notification is made in person, the relevant person shall sign the Notice on Protective Custody. If in-person notification is not possible, the notice may be given first by telephone or other means, and the Notice on Protective Custody shall be served by mail, forwarding, or other means, with the relevant person required to sign it. If the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.  
If notification is inappropriate due to circumstances that may hinder the investigation, such as possible falsification, concealment, or destruction of evidence, interference with witness testimony, or collusion, approval shall be obtained according to the relevant provisions and the matter shall be recorded. After such circumstances that hinder the investigation disappear, the unit and family members of the person subject to protective custody shall be notified immediately.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　监察机关采取管护措施后，应当在二十四小时以内对被管护人员进行谈话、讯问。

**Article 117** After taking a protective custody measure, a supervisory organ shall conduct a conversation with and interrogate the person subject to protective custody within twenty-four hours.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　管护时间不得超过七日，自向被管护人员宣布之日起算。因案情复杂、疑难，在七日以内无法作出留置或者解除管护决定的，经审批可以延长一日至三日。  
延长管护时间的，应当在管护期满前向被管护人员宣布延长管护时间的决定，要求其在《延长管护时间决定书》上签名、捺指印。被管护人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
延长管护时间的，应当及时通知被管护人员所在单位和家属。

**Article 118** The period of protective custody shall not exceed seven days, commencing from the date of announcement to the person subject to protective custody. If, due to the complexity or difficulty of the case, a decision on detention or lifting of protective custody cannot be made within seven days, the period may be extended by one to three days after approval.  
Where the period of protective custody is extended, the decision to extend the period of protective custody shall be announced to the person subject to protective custody before the expiration of the original period, and the person shall be requested to sign and affix a fingerprint on the Decision on Extending the Period of Protective Custody. If the person subject to protective custody refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.  
Where the period of protective custody is extended, the unit and family members of the person subject to protective custody shall be notified promptly.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条　对被管护人员不需要继续采取管护措施的，应当按规定报批后解除管护或者变更为责令候查措施。管护期满的，应当按规定报批后予以解除。  
解除管护措施的，调查人员应当向被管护人员宣布解除管护措施的决定，由其在《解除管护决定书》上签名、捺指印；变更为责令候查措施的，应当向被管护人员宣布变更为责令候查措施的决定，由其在《变更管护决定书》上签名、捺指印。被管护人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
解除管护措施或者变更为责令候查措施的，应当及时通知被管护人员所在单位和家属、申请人。调查人员应当与交接人办理交接手续，并由其在《解除管护通知书》或者《变更管护通知书》上签名。无法通知或者有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。不得因办理交接手续延迟解除或者变更管护措施。

**Article 119** Where a protective custody measure is no longer required for the person subject thereto, it shall be lifted or modified to the measure of ordered to await investigation after approval according to the relevant provisions. Upon expiration of the protective custody period, it shall be lifted after approval according to the relevant provisions.  
Where the protective custody measure is lifted, the investigation personnel shall announce the decision to lift the protective custody measure to the person subject to protective custody, and request that the person sign and affix a fingerprint on the Decision on Lifting Protective Custody; where the measure is modified to the measure of ordered to await investigation, the decision to modify to the measure of ordered to await investigation shall be announced to the person subject to protective custody, and the person shall be requested to sign and affix a fingerprint on the Decision on Modification of Protective Custody. If the person subject to protective custody refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.  
Where the protective custody measure is lifted or modified to the measure of ordered to await investigation, the unit, family members, and the applicant of the person subject to protective custody shall be notified promptly. The investigation personnel shall go through the handover procedures with the receiving person, who shall sign the Notice on Lifting Protective Custody or the Notice on Modification of Protective Custody. If notification is impossible or the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument. The lifting or modification of the protective custody measure shall not be delayed due to the handover procedures.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条　在管护期满前，将管护措施变更为留置措施的，按照本条例关于采取留置措施的规定执行。

**Article 120** Where a protective custody measure is modified to a detention measure before the expiration of the protective custody period, the provisions of these Regulations on taking detention measures shall apply.

#### 第九节　留置

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条　监察机关调查严重职务违法或者职务犯罪，对于符合[监察法第二十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-24)第一款规定的，经依法审批，可以对被调查人采取留置措施。  
[监察法第二十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-24)第一款规定的已经掌握其部分违法犯罪事实及证据，是指同时具备下列情形：  
（一）有证据证明发生了违法犯罪事实；  
（二）有证据证明该违法犯罪事实是被调查人实施；  
（三）证明被调查人实施违法犯罪行为的证据已经查证属实。  
部分违法犯罪事实，既可以是单一违法犯罪行为的事实，也可以是数个违法犯罪行为中任何一个违法犯罪行为的事实。  
[监察法第二十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-24)第一款规定的重要问题，是指对被调查人涉嫌的严重职务违法或者职务犯罪，在定性处置、定罪量刑等方面有重要影响的事实、情节及证据。

**Article 121** Where a supervisory organ investigates serious illegal acts in the performance of duties or duty-related crimes, it may, after approval according to law, take detention measures against the person under investigation if the conditions prescribed in Article 24, paragraph 1 of the Supervision Law are met.  
The phrase "partial facts and evidence of illegal or criminal acts have already been obtained" as prescribed in Article 24, paragraph 1 of the Supervision Law means that the following circumstances all exist:  
(1) There is evidence proving that an illegal or criminal act has occurred;  
(2) There is evidence proving that the illegal or criminal act was committed by the person under investigation;  
(3) The evidence proving that the person under investigation committed the illegal or criminal act has been verified and found to be true.  
"Partial facts of illegal or criminal acts" may be facts of a single illegal or criminal act, or facts of any one of several illegal or criminal acts.  
The phrase "important issues" as prescribed in Article 24, paragraph 1 of the Supervision Law refers to facts, circumstances, and evidence that have a significant impact on the characterization, disposition, conviction, and sentencing of the serious illegal acts in the performance of duties or duty-related crimes suspected by the person under investigation.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条　被调查人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第二十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-24)第一款第二项所规定的可能逃跑、自杀：  
（一）着手准备自杀、自残或者逃跑的；  
（二）曾经有自杀、自残或者逃跑行为的；  
（三）有自杀、自残或者逃跑意图的；  
（四）其他可能逃跑、自杀的情形。

**Article 122** Where a person under investigation falls under any of the following circumstances, he or she may be found to be likely to escape or commit suicide as prescribed in Article 24, paragraph 1, item 2 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) He or she has begun to prepare for suicide, self-harm, or escape;  
(2) He or she has previously attempted suicide, self-harm, or escape;  
(3) He or she has the intention to commit suicide, self-harm, or escape;  
(4) Other circumstances indicating a likelihood of escape or suicide.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条　被调查人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第二十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-24)第一款第三项所规定的可能串供或者伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据：  
（一）曾经或者企图串供，伪造、隐匿、毁灭、转移证据的；  
（二）曾经或者企图威逼、恐吓、利诱、收买证人，干扰证人作证的；  
（三）有同案人或者与被调查人存在密切关联违法犯罪的涉案人员在逃，重要证据尚未收集完成的；  
（四）其他可能串供或者伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据的情形。

**Article 123** Where a person under investigation falls under any of the following circumstances, he or she may be found to be likely to collude or to falsify, conceal, or destroy evidence as prescribed in Article 24, paragraph 1, item 3 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) He or she has previously attempted or is attempting to collude, or to falsify, conceal, destroy, or transfer evidence;  
(2) He or she has previously attempted or is attempting to intimidate, threaten, induce, or bribe witnesses, or to interfere with witness testimony;  
(3) A co-offender or an involved person who is closely related to the person under investigation in illegal or criminal activities is at large, and important evidence has not yet been collected;  
(4) Other circumstances indicating a likelihood of collusion or falsification, concealment, or destruction of evidence.

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条　被调查人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第二十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-24)第一款第四项所规定的可能有其他妨碍调查行为：  
（一）可能继续实施违法犯罪行为的；  
（二）有危害国家安全、公共安全等现实危险的；  
（三）可能对举报人、控告人、被害人、证人、鉴定人等相关人员实施打击报复的；  
（四）无正当理由拒不到案，严重影响调查的；  
（五）其他可能妨碍调查的行为。

**Article 124** Where a person under investigation falls under any of the following circumstances, he or she may be found to be likely to engage in other acts that obstruct the investigation as prescribed in Article 24, paragraph 1, item 4 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) He or she may continue to commit illegal or criminal acts;  
(2) There is a real risk of endangering national security or public safety;  
(3) He or she may retaliate against whistleblowers, accusers, victims, witnesses, expert evaluators, or other relevant persons;  
(4) He or she, without justifiable reasons, refuses to appear before the supervisory organ, seriously affecting the investigation;  
(5) Other acts that may obstruct the investigation.

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条　对下列人员不得采取留置措施：  
（一）患有严重疾病、生活不能自理的；  
（二）怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女；  
（三）生活不能自理的人的唯一扶养人。  
上述情形消失后，根据调查需要可以对相关人员采取留置措施。

**Article 125** No detention measure shall be taken against the following persons:  
(1) Those suffering from a serious illness and unable to take care of themselves;  
(2) Women who are pregnant or are breastfeeding their own infants;  
(3) The sole supporter of a person who is unable to take care of himself or herself.  
After the above circumstances cease to exist, a detention measure may be taken against the relevant person as required by the investigation.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条　采取留置措施时，调查人员不得少于二人，应当向被留置人员宣布《留置决定书》，告知被留置人员权利义务，要求其在《留置决定书》上签名、捺指印。被留置人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 126** When taking a detention measure, there shall be no fewer than two investigation personnel, who shall announce the Decision on Detention to the person under detention, inform the person of his or her rights and obligations, and request that the person sign and affix a fingerprint on the Decision on Detention. If the person under detention refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条　采取留置措施后，应当在二十四小时以内通知被留置人员所在单位和家属。当面通知的，由有关人员在《留置通知书》上签名。无法当面通知的，可以先以电话等方式通知，并通过邮寄、转交等方式送达《留置通知书》，要求有关人员在《留置通知书》上签名。有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
因可能伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据，干扰证人作证或者串供等有碍调查情形而不宜通知的，应当按规定报批，记录在案。有碍调查的情形消失后，应当立即通知被留置人员所在单位和家属。

**Article 127** After a detention measure is taken, the unit and family members of the person under detention shall be notified within twenty-four hours. If notification is made in person, the relevant person shall sign the Notice on Detention. If in-person notification is not possible, the notice may be given first by telephone or other means, and the Notice on Detention shall be served by mail, forwarding, or other means, with the relevant person required to sign it. If the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.  
If notification is inappropriate due to circumstances that may hinder the investigation, such as possible falsification, concealment, or destruction of evidence, interference with witness testimony, or collusion, approval shall be obtained according to the relevant provisions and the matter shall be recorded. After such circumstances that hinder the investigation disappear, the unit and family members of the person under detention shall be notified immediately.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条　监察机关采取留置措施后，应当在二十四小时以内对涉嫌职务违法的被调查人进行谈话，对涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人进行讯问。

**Article 128** After taking a detention measure, a supervisory organ shall, within twenty-four hours, conduct a conversation with the person under investigation suspected of illegal acts in the performance of duties, and interrogate the person under investigation suspected of duty-related crimes.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条　留置时间不得超过三个月，自向被留置人员宣布之日起算。具有下列情形之一的，经审批可以延长一次，延长时间不得超过三个月：  
（一）案情重大，严重危害国家利益或者公共利益的；  
（二）案情复杂，涉案人员多、金额巨大，涉及范围广的；  
（三）重要证据尚未收集完成，或者重要涉案人员尚未到案，导致违法犯罪的主要事实仍须继续调查的；  
（四）其他需要延长留置时间的情形。  
省级以下监察机关采取留置措施的，延长留置时间应当报请上一级监察机关批准。  
延长留置时间的，应当在留置期满前向被留置人员宣布延长留置时间的决定，要求其在《延长留置时间决定书》上签名、捺指印。被留置人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
延长留置时间的，应当及时通知被留置人员所在单位和家属。

**Article 129** The period of detention shall not exceed three months, commencing from the date of announcement to the person under detention. Under any of the following circumstances, the period may be extended once after approval, and the extension shall not exceed three months:  
(1) The case is of great significance, seriously harming national interests or public interests;  
(2) The case is complex, involving many persons involved, a huge amount of money, and a wide scope;  
(3) Important evidence has not yet been collected, or important persons involved have not yet appeared before the organ, necessitating continued investigation into the main facts of the illegal or criminal act;  
(4) Other circumstances requiring an extension of the detention period.  
Where a supervisory organ below the provincial level takes a detention measure, an extension of the detention period shall be submitted to the supervisory organ at the next higher level for approval.  
Where the detention period is extended, the decision to extend the detention period shall be announced to the person under detention before the expiration of the original period, and the person shall be requested to sign and affix a fingerprint on the Decision on Extending the Detention Period. If the person under detention refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigation personnel shall record it on the instrument.  
Where the detention period is extended, the unit and family members of the person under detention shall be notified promptly.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条　对涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人可能判处十年有期徒刑以上刑罚，监察机关按照[本条例第一百二十九条](#art-129)规定延长期限届满，仍不能调查终结的，经审批可以再延长，再延长时间不得超过二个月。  
省级以下监察机关需要再延长留置时间的，应当逐级报送国家监察委员会批准。  
再延长留置时间的，应当在留置期满前向被留置人员宣布再延长留置时间的决定，要求其在《再延长留置时间决定书》上签名、捺指印。被留置人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
再延长留置时间的，应当及时通知被留置人员所在单位和家属。

**Article 130** Where a person under investigation suspected of duty-related crimes may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, and the supervisory organ, upon expiration of the extension period in accordance with Article 129 of these Regulations, is still unable to conclude the investigation, the period may be further extended upon approval, but the further extension shall not exceed two months.  
Where a supervisory organ at or below the provincial level needs to further extend the period of detention, it shall submit the matter level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for approval.  
Where the period of detention is further extended, the decision on further extension of the detention period shall be announced to the detained person before the expiration of the detention period, and the detained person shall be required to sign and affix his or her fingerprint on the "Decision on Further Extension of the Detention Period". If the detained person refuses to sign or affix his or her fingerprint, the investigator shall note this on the document.  
Where the period of detention is further extended, the unit to which the detained person belongs and his or her family members shall be notified in a timely manner.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条　报请批准延长或者再延长留置时间，应当在报请材料中写明被留置人员基本情况、主要案情和留置后调查工作进展情况、下一步调查工作计划、延长或者再延长留置时间的具体理由及起止时间。  
报请批准延长或者再延长留置时间，应当根据案件具体情况和实际工作需要，提出合理、必要的时间建议。  
上级监察机关收到报请批准延长或者再延长留置时间的申请后，应当及时研究，在原留置期限届满前按程序作出决定。

**Article 131** When reporting for approval of extension or further extension of the detention period, the reporting materials shall specify the basic information of the detained person, the main facts of the case, the progress of the investigation after detention, the plan for the next step of the investigation, and the specific reasons for extension or further extension of the detention period as well as the starting and ending dates.  
The proposal for extension or further extension of the detention period shall be reasonable and necessary based on the specific circumstances of the case and actual work needs.  
After receiving the application for approval of extension or further extension of the detention period, the superior supervisory organ shall promptly study it and make a decision according to the procedure before the expiration of the original detention period.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　省级以上监察机关在调查期间，发现涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人另有与留置时的罪行不同种的重大职务犯罪或者同种的影响罪名认定、量刑档次的重大职务犯罪，经审批可以依照[监察法第四十八条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-48)第三款的规定重新计算留置时间。留置时间重新计算以一次为限。  
依照前款规定重新计算留置时间的，国家监察委员会调查部门应当自发现之日起五日以内履行报批程序，省级监察机关应当自发现之日起五日以内报请国家监察委员会批准。  
重新计算留置时间的，应当自作出决定之日起五日以内向被留置人员宣布，要求其在《重新计算留置时间决定书》上签名、捺指印，并及时通知被留置人员所在单位和家属。被留置人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 132** Where, during the investigation, a supervisory organ at or above the provincial level discovers that the person under investigation suspected of duty-related crimes has additionally committed a major duty-related crime of a different type from the crime for which detention was imposed, or a major duty-related crime of the same type that affects the determination of the crime or the sentencing grade, it may, upon approval, recalculate the detention period in accordance with paragraph 3 of Article 48 of the Supervision Law. The recalculation of the detention period shall be limited to once.  
Where the detention period is recalculated in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the investigation department of the National Commission of Supervision shall complete the reporting and approval procedures within five days from the date of discovery; a supervisory organ at the provincial level shall report to the National Commission of Supervision for approval within five days from the date of discovery.  
Where the detention period is recalculated, the decision shall be announced to the detained person within five days from the date of the decision, requiring him or her to sign and affix his or her fingerprint on the "Decision on Recalculation of the Detention Period", and the unit to which the detained person belongs and his or her family members shall be notified in a timely manner. If the detained person refuses to sign or affix his or her fingerprint, the investigator shall note this on the document.

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　重新计算留置时间的，留置时间不得超过三个月。新发现的罪行具有[本条例第一百二十九条](#art-129)、第一百三十条规定情形的，可以依法延长和再延长留置时间。但是，此前已经根据[本条例第一百三十条](#art-130)规定再延长留置时间的，不得再次适用该规定再延长留置时间。

**Article 133** Where the detention period is recalculated, the detention period shall not exceed three months. If the newly discovered crime falls under the circumstances specified in Articles 129 and 130 of these Regulations, the detention period may be extended or further extended according to law. However, if the detention period has already been further extended under Article 130 of these Regulations, the provisions of that Article shall not be applied again for further extension.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　对被留置人员不需要继续采取留置措施的，应当按规定报批后解除留置或者变更为责令候查措施。留置期满的，应当按规定报批后予以解除。  
解除留置措施的，调查人员应当向被留置人员宣布解除留置措施的决定，由其在《解除留置决定书》上签名、捺指印；变更为责令候查措施的，应当向被留置人员宣布变更为责令候查措施的决定，由其在《变更留置决定书》上签名、捺指印。被留置人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
解除留置措施或者变更为责令候查措施的，应当及时通知被留置人员所在单位和家属、申请人。调查人员应当与交接人办理交接手续，并由其在《解除留置通知书》或者《变更留置通知书》上签名。无法通知或者有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。不得因办理交接手续延迟解除或者变更留置措施。  
案件依法移送人民检察院审查起诉的，留置措施自犯罪嫌疑人被执行拘留时自动解除，不再办理解除法律手续。

**Article 134** Where it is no longer necessary to maintain detention measures against a detained person, the detention shall be lifted or changed to a measure of awaiting investigation on bail after reporting and approval according to regulations. Upon expiration of the detention period, it shall be lifted after reporting and approval according to regulations.  
Where the detention measure is lifted, the investigator shall announce the decision to lift the detention measure to the detained person and require him or her to sign and affix his or her fingerprint on the "Decision on Lifting Detention"; where the detention measure is changed to a measure of awaiting investigation on bail, the investigator shall announce the decision to change to the measure of awaiting investigation on bail to the detained person and require him or her to sign and affix his or her fingerprint on the "Decision on Changing Detention". If the detained person refuses to sign or affix his or her fingerprint, the investigator shall note this on the document.  
Where the detention measure is lifted or changed to a measure of awaiting investigation on bail, the unit to which the detained person belongs, his or her family members, and the applicant shall be notified in a timely manner. The investigator shall go through the handover procedures with the handover personnel, who shall sign on the "Notice of Lifting Detention" or "Notice of Changing Detention". If notification is impossible or the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigator shall note this on the document. The lifting or changing of the detention measure shall not be delayed due to the handling of handover procedures.  
Where the case is transferred according to law to the People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution, the detention measure shall be automatically lifted when the criminal suspect is formally detained, and no formal lifting procedures need be completed.

#### 第十节　查询、冻结

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　监察机关调查严重职务违法或者职务犯罪，根据工作需要，按规定报批后，可以依法查询、冻结涉案单位和个人的存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产。

**Article 135** Where a supervisory organ investigates serious duty-related violations or duty-related crimes, it may, according to work needs and after reporting and approval according to regulations, inquire into and freeze the deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares and other property of the units and individuals involved in the case according to law.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　查询、冻结财产时，调查人员不得少于二人。调查人员应当出具《协助查询财产通知书》或者《协助冻结财产通知书》，送交银行或者其他金融机构、邮政部门等单位执行。有关单位和个人应当予以配合，并严格保密。  
查询财产应当在《协助查询财产通知书》中填写查询账号、查询内容等信息。没有具体账号的，应当填写足以确定账户或者权利人的自然人姓名、身份证件号码或者市场主体名称、统一社会信用代码等信息。  
冻结财产应当在《协助冻结财产通知书》中填写冻结账户名称、冻结账号、冻结数额、冻结期限起止时间等信息。冻结数额应当具体、明确，暂时无法确定具体数额的，应当在《协助冻结财产通知书》上明确写明“只收不付”。冻结证券和交易结算资金时，应当明确冻结的范围是否及于孳息。  
冻结财产，应当为被调查人及其所扶养的亲属保留必需的生活费用。

**Article 136** When inquiring into or freezing property, there shall be no fewer than two investigators. The investigators shall issue a "Notice on Assisting in Inquiry into Property" or a "Notice on Assisting in Freezing Property" and deliver it to banks, other financial institutions, postal departments or other units for execution. The relevant units and individuals shall cooperate and shall strictly maintain confidentiality.  
When inquiring into property, the account number, content of inquiry and other information shall be filled in the "Notice on Assisting in Inquiry into Property". If there is no specific account number, information sufficient to identify the account or the right holder, such as the natural person's name and identity document number or the market entity's name and unified social credit code, shall be filled in.  
When freezing property, the name of the frozen account, the frozen account number, the frozen amount, the starting and ending dates of the freezing period and other information shall be filled in the "Notice on Assisting in Freezing Property". The frozen amount shall be specific and definite; if the specific amount cannot be determined temporarily, it shall be clearly stated in the "Notice on Assisting in Freezing Property" that "only deposits are accepted, no withdrawals allowed". When freezing securities and transaction settlement funds, it shall be clearly stated whether the scope of freezing extends to the fruits thereof.  
When freezing property, the necessary living expenses shall be reserved for the person under investigation and the relatives supported by him or her.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　调查人员可以根据需要对查询结果进行打印、抄录、复制、拍照，要求相关单位在有关材料上加盖证明印章。对查询结果有疑问的，可以要求相关单位进行书面解释并加盖印章。

**Article 137** Investigators may, as needed, print, transcribe, copy, or photograph the inquiry results and require the relevant units to affix their certification seals on the relevant materials. If there is any doubt about the inquiry results, they may require the relevant units to provide a written explanation and affix their seals.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　监察机关对查询信息应当加强管理，规范信息交接、调阅、使用程序和手续，防止滥用和泄露。  
调查人员不得查询与案件调查工作无关的信息。

**Article 138** Supervisory organs shall strengthen the management of inquiry information, standardize the procedures and formalities for handover, access, and use of information, and prevent abuse and disclosure.  
Investigators shall not inquire into information unrelated to the investigation of the case.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　冻结财产的期限不得超过六个月。冻结期限到期未办理续冻手续的，冻结自动解除。  
有特殊原因需要延长冻结期限的，应当在到期前按原程序报批，办理续冻手续。每次续冻期限不得超过六个月。

**Article 139** The period for freezing property shall not exceed six months. If the renewal procedures are not completed before the expiration of the freezing period, the freeze shall be automatically lifted.  
If there are special reasons requiring an extension of the freezing period, the extension shall be reported for approval in accordance with the original procedure before the expiration, and renewal procedures shall be completed. Each renewal period shall not exceed six months.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　已被冻结的财产可以轮候冻结，不得重复冻结。轮候冻结的，监察机关应当要求有关银行或者其他金融机构等单位在解除冻结或者作出处理前予以通知。  
监察机关接受司法机关、其他监察机关等国家机关移送的涉案财物后，该国家机关采取的冻结期限届满，监察机关续行冻结的顺位与该国家机关冻结的顺位相同。

**Article 140** Property that has already been frozen may be subject to a subsequent freezing in turn, but shall not be frozen repeatedly. In the case of subsequent freezing in turn, the supervisory organ shall require the relevant bank, other financial institution or other unit to give notice before the freeze is lifted or disposal is made.  
After a supervisory organ accepts the property involved in a case transferred by a judicial organ, another supervisory organ or other state organ, and the freezing period adopted by such state organ expires, the priority of the supervisory organ's continuation of the freeze shall be the same as the priority of the freeze by that state organ.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　冻结财产应当通知权利人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人，要求其在《冻结财产告知书》上签名。冻结股票、债券、基金份额等财产，应当告知权利人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人有权申请出售。  
对于被冻结的股票、债券、基金份额等财产，权利人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人申请出售，不损害国家利益、被害人利益，不影响调查正常进行的，经审批可以在案件办结前由相关机构依法出售或者变现。对于被冻结的汇票、本票、支票即将到期的，经审批可以在案件办结前由相关机构依法出售或者变现。出售上述财产的，应当出具《许可出售冻结财产通知书》。  
出售或者变现所得价款应当继续冻结在其对应的银行账户中；没有对应的银行账户的，应当存入监察机关指定的专用账户保管，并将存款凭证送监察机关登记。监察机关应当及时向权利人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人出具《出售冻结财产通知书》，并要求其签名。拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 141** When freezing property, the right holder or his or her legal representative or authorized agent shall be notified and required to sign on the "Notice of Freezing Property". When freezing stocks, bonds, fund shares or other property, the right holder or his or her legal representative or authorized agent shall be informed of the right to apply for sale.  
With respect to frozen stocks, bonds, fund shares or other property, if the right holder or his or her legal representative or authorized agent applies for sale, and such sale does not harm the interests of the State or the victim and does not affect the normal conduct of the investigation, upon approval, the property may be sold or realized by the relevant institution according to law before the case is concluded. With respect to frozen bills of exchange, promissory notes or checks that are about to mature, upon approval, they may be sold or realized by the relevant institution according to law before the case is concluded. Where the above property is sold, a "Notice on Permitting Sale of Frozen Property" shall be issued.  
The proceeds from the sale or realization shall continue to be frozen in the corresponding bank account; if there is no corresponding bank account, they shall be deposited in a special account designated by the supervisory organ for safekeeping, and the deposit certificate shall be submitted to the supervisory organ for registration. The supervisory organ shall promptly issue a "Notice on Sale of Frozen Property" to the right holder or his or her legal representative or authorized agent and require him or her to sign. If the person refuses to sign, the investigator shall note this on the document.

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　对于冻结的财产，应当及时核查。经查明与案件无关的，经审批，应当在查明后三日以内将《解除冻结财产通知书》送交有关单位执行。解除情况应当告知被冻结财产的权利人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人。

**Article 142** The frozen property shall be promptly verified. If it is determined to be unrelated to the case, upon approval, the "Notice on Lifting Freeze on Property" shall be delivered to the relevant unit for execution within three days after such determination. The lifting of the freeze shall be notified to the right holder of the frozen property or his or her legal representative or authorized agent.

#### 第十一节　搜查

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　监察机关调查职务犯罪案件，为了收集犯罪证据、查获被调查人，按规定报批后，可以依法对被调查人以及可能隐藏被调查人或者犯罪证据的人的身体、物品、住处、工作地点和其他有关地方进行搜查。

**Article 143** When investigating a duty-related crime case, for the purpose of collecting evidence of the crime and apprehending the person under investigation, a supervisory organ may, after reporting and approval according to regulations, conduct a search of the body, articles, residence, workplace and other relevant places of the person under investigation and of any person who may conceal the person under investigation or criminal evidence according to law.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　搜查应当在调查人员主持下进行，调查人员不得少于二人。搜查女性的身体，由女性工作人员进行。  
搜查时，应当有被搜查人或者其家属、其所在单位工作人员或者其他见证人在场。调查人员应当向被搜查人或者其家属、见证人出示《搜查证》，要求其签名或者盖章。被搜查人或者其家属不在场，或者拒绝签名、盖章的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 144** A search shall be conducted under the direction of investigators, and there shall be no fewer than two investigators. A search of the body of a female shall be conducted by female staff members.  
When conducting a search, the person being searched or his or her family members, staff members of the unit to which he or she belongs, or other witnesses shall be present. The investigators shall present the "Search Warrant" to the person being searched or his or her family members and the witnesses and require them to sign or affix their seals. If the person being searched or his or her family members are not present, or refuse to sign or affix their seals, the investigators shall note this on the document.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　搜查时，应当要求在场人员予以配合，不得进行阻碍。对以暴力、威胁等方法阻碍搜查的，应当依法制止。对阻碍搜查构成违法犯罪的，依法追究法律责任。

**Article 145** When conducting a search, the persons present shall be required to cooperate and shall not obstruct the search. Any obstruction of the search by violence or threat shall be stopped according to law. If such obstruction constitutes an illegal act or crime, legal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　对搜查取证工作，应当全程同步录音录像。  
对搜查情况应当制作《搜查笔录》，由调查人员和被搜查人或者其家属、见证人签名或者盖章。被搜查人或者其家属不在场，或者拒绝签名、盖章的，调查人员应当在笔录中记明。  
对于查获的重要物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据及其放置、存储位置应当拍照，并在《搜查笔录》中作出文字说明。

**Article 146** The search and evidence collection work shall be recorded by synchronized audio and video recording throughout the process.  
A "Search Record" shall be made for the search, and shall be signed or sealed by the investigators and the person searched or his or her family members and the witnesses. If the person searched or his or her family members are not present, or refuse to sign or affix their seals, the investigators shall note this in the record.  
Important physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, electronic data discovered and the places where they are placed or stored shall be photographed, and a written explanation shall be made in the "Search Record".

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　搜查时，应当避免未成年人或者其他不适宜在搜查现场的人在场。  
搜查人员应当服从指挥、文明执法，不得擅自变更搜查对象和扩大搜查范围，严禁单独进入搜查区域。搜查的具体时间、方法，在实施前应当严格保密。

**Article 147** When conducting a search, minors or other persons who are not suitable to be present at the search scene shall be kept away.  
Search personnel shall follow orders, enforce the law in a civilized manner, shall not change the subject of the search or expand the scope of the search without authorization, and are strictly prohibited from entering the search area alone. The specific time and method of the search shall be strictly kept confidential before implementation.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　在搜查过程中查封、扣押财物和文件的，按照查封、扣押的有关规定办理。

**Article 148** Where property and documents are sealed up or seized during a search, the relevant provisions on sealing up and seizure shall apply.

#### 第十二节　调取

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　监察机关按规定报批后，可以依法向有关单位和个人调取用以证明案件事实的证据材料。

**Article 149** A supervisory organ may, after reporting and approval according to regulations, obtain from relevant units and individuals evidence materials used to prove the facts of the case according to law.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　调取证据材料时，调查人员不得少于二人。调查人员应当依法出具《调取证据通知书》，必要时附《调取证据清单》。  
有关单位和个人配合监察机关调取证据，应当严格保密。

**Article 150** When collecting evidentiary materials, the number of investigators shall not be less than two. The investigators shall issue the "Notice on Collection of Evidence" according to law, and attach the "List of Collected Evidence" when necessary.  
Relevant entities and individuals that cooperate with the supervisory authority in collecting evidence shall strictly maintain confidentiality.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　调取物证应当调取原物。原物不便搬运、保存，或者依法应当返还，或者因保密工作需要不能调取原物的，可以将原物封存，并拍照、录像。对原物拍照或者录像时，应当足以反映原物的外形、内容。  
调取书证、视听资料应当调取原件。取得原件确有困难或者因保密工作需要不能调取原件的，可以调取副本或者复制件。  
调取物证的照片、录像和书证、视听资料的副本、复制件的，应当书面记明不能调取原物、原件的原因，原物、原件存放地点，制作过程，是否与原物、原件相符，并由调查人员和物证、书证、视听资料原持有人签名或者盖章。持有人无法签名、盖章或者拒绝签名、盖章的，应当在笔录中记明，由见证人签名。

**Article 151** When collecting physical evidence, the original object shall be collected. If the original object is inconvenient to transport or preserve, or shall be returned according to law, or cannot be collected due to the need for confidentiality, the original object may be sealed and photographed or video recorded. When photographing or video recording the original object, it shall be sufficient to reflect the appearance and content of the original object.  
When collecting documentary evidence or audio-visual materials, the original copies shall be collected. If it is truly difficult to obtain the original copies or the original copies cannot be collected due to the need for confidentiality, duplicates or reproductions may be collected.  
When collecting photographs or video recordings of physical evidence and duplicates or reproductions of documentary evidence or audio-visual materials, the reasons for being unable to collect the original object or original copies, the place where the original object or original copies are stored, the production process, and whether they are consistent with the original object or original copies shall be recorded in writing, and shall be signed or sealed by the investigators and the original holders of the physical evidence, documentary evidence, or audio-visual materials. If the holder is unable to sign or seal, or refuses to sign or seal, it shall be recorded in the written record and signed by a witness.

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　调取外文材料作为证据使用的，应当交由具有资质的机构和人员出具中文译本。中文译本应当加盖翻译机构公章。

**Article 152** Where foreign-language materials are collected for use as evidence, they shall be submitted to a qualified institution or person for the production of a Chinese translation. The Chinese translation shall be affixed with the official seal of the translation institution.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　收集、提取电子数据，能够扣押原始存储介质的，应当予以扣押、封存并在笔录中记录封存状态。无法扣押原始存储介质的，可以采取调取、勘验检查措施，通过现场或者网络远程收集、提取电子数据，但应当在笔录中记明不能扣押的原因、原始存储介质的存放地点或者电子数据的来源等情况。  
由于客观原因无法或者不宜采取前款规定方式收集、提取电子数据的，可以采取打印、拍照或者录像等方式固定相关证据，并在笔录中说明原因。  
收集、提取的电子数据，足以保证完整性，无删除、修改、增加等情形的，可以作为证据使用。  
收集、提取电子数据，应当制作笔录，记录案由、对象、内容，收集、提取电子数据的时间、地点、方法、过程，并附电子数据清单，注明类别、文件格式、完整性校验值等，由调查人员、电子数据持有人（提供人）签名或者盖章；电子数据持有人（提供人）无法签名或者拒绝签名的，应当在笔录中记明，由见证人签名或者盖章。有条件的，应当对相关活动进行录像。

**Article 153** When collecting or extracting electronic data, if the original storage medium can be seized, it shall be seized and sealed, and the state of sealing shall be recorded in the written record. If the original storage medium cannot be seized, measures such as retrieval, inspection, and examination may be taken to collect or extract the electronic data on site or remotely via the network, but the reasons for inability to seize, the storage location of the original storage medium, or the source of the electronic data shall be recorded in the written record.  
Where collection or extraction of electronic data by the means specified in the preceding paragraph is impossible or inappropriate due to objective reasons, the relevant evidence may be fixed by means such as printing, photographing, or video recording, and the reasons shall be stated in the written record.  
Electronic data collected or extracted may be used as evidence if it sufficiently guarantees integrity and there is no deletion, modification, addition, or other such circumstances.  
When collecting or extracting electronic data, a written record shall be made, recording the case name, target, content, time, place, method, and process of collection or extraction, and shall be accompanied by a list of electronic data specifying the category, file format, integrity check value, etc. It shall be signed or sealed by the investigators and the holder (provider) of the electronic data; if the holder (provider) is unable to sign or refuses to sign, it shall be recorded in the written record and signed or sealed by a witness. If conditions permit, the relevant activities shall be video recorded.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　调取的物证、书证、视听资料等原件，经查明与案件无关的，经审批，应当在查明后三日以内退还，并办理交接手续。

**Article 154** If the original physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, etc., that have been collected are found to be irrelevant to the case, they shall, upon approval, be returned within three days after the finding, and the handover procedures shall be completed.

#### 第十三节　查封、扣押

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　监察机关按规定报批后，可以依法查封、扣押用以证明被调查人涉嫌违法犯罪以及情节轻重的财物、文件、电子数据等证据材料。  
对于被调查人到案时随身携带的物品，以及被调查人或者其他相关人员主动上交的财物和文件，依法需要扣押的，依照前款规定办理。对于被调查人随身携带的与案件无关的个人用品，应当逐件登记，随案移交或者退还。

**Article 155** After reporting and obtaining approval in accordance with the regulations, the supervisory authority may, according to law, seal or seize property, documents, electronic data, and other evidentiary materials used to prove that the person under investigation is suspected of committing an illegal act or crime and the seriousness of the circumstances.  
With respect to articles carried by the person under investigation when they appear before the authority, as well as property and documents voluntarily surrendered by the person under investigation or other relevant persons, if they need to be seized according to law, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply. Personal items carried by the person under investigation that are unrelated to the case shall be registered item by item and transferred along with the case or returned.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　对查封、扣押工作，应当全程同步录音录像。  
查封、扣押时，应当出具《查封/扣押通知书》，调查人员不得少于二人。持有人拒绝交出应当查封、扣押的财物和文件的，可以依法强制查封、扣押。  
调查人员对于查封、扣押的财物和文件，应当会同在场见证人和被查封、扣押财物持有人进行清点核对，开列《查封/扣押财物、文件清单》，由调查人员、见证人和持有人签名或者盖章。持有人不在场或者拒绝签名、盖章的，调查人员应当在清单上记明。  
查封、扣押财物，应当为被调查人及其所扶养的亲属保留必需的生活费用和物品。

**Article 156** The work of sealing and seizing shall be simultaneously audio and video recorded throughout the entire process.  
When sealing or seizing, the "Notice on Sealing/Seizure" shall be issued, and the number of investigators shall not be less than two. If the holder refuses to hand over the property or documents that shall be sealed or seized, compulsory sealing or seizure may be carried out according to law.  
For the property and documents sealed or seized, the investigators shall, together with the witnesses present and the holders of the sealed or seized property, conduct an inventory and check, and draw up a "List of Sealed/Seized Property and Documents," which shall be signed or sealed by the investigators, witnesses, and holders. If the holder is not present or refuses to sign or seal, the investigators shall record it on the list.  
When sealing or seizing property, necessary living expenses and articles shall be reserved for the person under investigation and their dependents.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　查封、扣押不动产和置于该不动产上不宜移动的设施、家具和其他相关财物，以及车辆、船舶、航空器和大型机械、设备等财物，必要时可以依法扣押其权利证书，经拍照或者录像后原地封存。调查人员应当在查封清单上记明相关财物的所在地址和特征，已经拍照或者录像及其权利证书被扣押的情况，由调查人员、见证人和持有人签名或者盖章。持有人不在场或者拒绝签名、盖章的，调查人员应当在清单上记明。  
查封、扣押前款规定财物的，必要时可以将被查封财物交给持有人或者其近亲属保管。调查人员应当告知保管人妥善保管，不得对被查封财物进行转移、变卖、毁损、抵押、赠予等处理。  
调查人员应当将《查封/扣押通知书》送达不动产、生产设备或者车辆、船舶、航空器等财物的登记、管理部门，告知其在查封期间禁止办理抵押、转让、出售等权属关系变更、转移登记手续。相关情况应当在查封清单上记明。被查封、扣押的财物已经办理抵押登记的，监察机关在执行没收、追缴、责令退赔等决定时应当及时通知抵押权人。

**Article 157** When sealing or seizing immovable property and facilities, furniture, and other relevant property placed on such immovable property that are not suitable for removal, as well as vehicles, vessels, aircraft, and large machinery, equipment, and other property, the title certificate thereof may be seized according to law if necessary, and the property may be sealed on site after photographing or video recording. The investigators shall record on the sealing list the location and characteristics of the relevant property, the fact that photographing or video recording has been carried out, and the seizure of the title certificate, and the list shall be signed or sealed by the investigators, witnesses, and holders. If the holder is not present or refuses to sign or seal, the investigators shall record it on the list.  
When sealing or seizing the property specified in the preceding paragraph, the sealed property may, if necessary, be handed over to the holder or their near relatives for custody. The investigators shall instruct the custodian to keep it properly and not to transfer, sell, damage, mortgage, donate, or otherwise dispose of the sealed property.  
The investigators shall serve the "Notice on Sealing/Seizure" on the registration or management authority of the immovable property, production equipment, or vehicles, vessels, aircraft, and other property, informing them that registration procedures for changes or transfers of ownership such as mortgage, transfer, or sale are prohibited during the sealing period. The relevant circumstances shall be recorded on the sealing list. If the sealed or seized property has undergone mortgage registration, the supervisory authority shall promptly notify the mortgagee when executing decisions on confiscation, recovery, or order for restitution.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　查封、扣押下列物品，应当依法进行相应的处理：  
（一）查封、扣押外币、金银珠宝、文物、名贵字画以及其他不易辨别真伪的贵重物品，具备当场密封条件的，应当当场密封，由二名以上调查人员在密封材料上签名并记明密封时间。不具备当场密封条件的，应当在笔录中记明，以拍照、录像等方法加以保全后进行封存。查封、扣押的贵重物品需要鉴定的，应当及时鉴定。  
（二）查封、扣押存折、银行卡、有价证券等支付凭证和具有一定特征能够证明案情的现金，应当记明特征、编号、种类、面值、张数、金额等，当场密封，由二名以上调查人员在密封材料上签名并记明密封时间。  
（三）查封、扣押易损毁、灭失、变质等不宜长期保存的物品以及有消费期限的卡、券，应当在笔录中记明，以拍照、录像等方法加以保全后进行封存，或者经审批委托有关机构变卖、拍卖。变卖、拍卖的价款存入专用账户保管，待调查终结后一并处理。  
（四）对于可以作为证据使用的录音录像、电子数据存储介质，应当记明案由、对象、内容，录制、复制的时间、地点、规格、类别、应用长度、文件格式及长度等，制作清单。具备查封、扣押条件的电子设备、存储介质应当密封保存。必要时，可以请有关机关协助。  
（五）对被调查人使用违法犯罪所得与合法收入共同购置的不可分割的财产，可以先行查封、扣押。对无法分割退还的财产，涉及违法的，可以经被调查人申请并经监察机关批准，由被调查人亲属或者被调查人委托的其他人员在监察机关监督下自行变现后上缴违法所得及孳息，也可以由监察机关在结案后委托有关单位拍卖、变卖，退还不属于违法所得的部分及孳息；涉及职务犯罪的，依法移送司法机关处置，并说明涉嫌犯罪所得及孳息数额。  
（六）查封、扣押危险品、违禁品，应当及时送交有关部门，或者根据工作需要严格封存保管。

**Article 158** The following items, when sealed or seized, shall be handled accordingly according to law:  
(1) When sealing or seizing foreign currency, gold, silver, jewelry, cultural relics, valuable calligraphy and paintings, and other valuable items that are difficult to distinguish as genuine or fake, if conditions for on-site sealing are met, they shall be sealed on site, with two or more investigators signing the sealing materials and recording the sealing time. If conditions for on-site sealing are not met, this shall be recorded in the written record, and the items shall be preserved by means such as photographing or video recording before being sealed. If the sealed or seized valuable items need to be appraised, the appraisal shall be conducted promptly.  
(2) When sealing or seizing passbooks, bank cards, securities, and other payment instruments, as well as cash that has certain characteristics and can prove the case, the characteristics, serial numbers, types, denominations, number of pieces, amounts, etc., shall be recorded, and the items shall be sealed on site, with two or more investigators signing the sealing materials and recording the sealing time.  
(3) When sealing or seizing items that are easily damaged, destroyed, deteriorated, or otherwise unsuitable for long-term preservation, as well as cards or vouchers with a consumption deadline, this shall be recorded in the written record, and the items shall be preserved by means such as photographing or video recording before being sealed, or, upon approval, entrusted to relevant institutions for sale or auction. The proceeds from the sale or auction shall be deposited in a dedicated account for safekeeping and shall be dealt with together after the investigation is concluded.  
(4) For audio or video recordings and electronic data storage media that may be used as evidence, the case name, target, content, time and place of recording or copying, specifications, category, length of application, file format and length, etc., shall be recorded, and a list shall be made. Electronic equipment and storage media that meet the conditions for sealing or seizure shall be sealed and stored. If necessary, assistance may be requested from relevant authorities.  
(5) For property that is indivisible and jointly purchased by the person under investigation using proceeds from illegal acts or crimes and lawful income, it may be sealed or seized in advance. For property that cannot be divided and returned, if it involves illegal acts, upon the application of the person under investigation and with the approval of the supervisory authority, the relatives of the person under investigation or other persons entrusted by the person under investigation may, under the supervision of the supervisory authority, sell the property themselves and turn over the illegal proceeds and their fruits; alternatively, the supervisory authority may, after the case is concluded, entrust relevant institutions to auction or sell the property and return the portion not belonging to illegal proceeds and their fruits. If it involves duty crimes, the property shall be transferred to the judicial authority for disposal according to law, with an explanation of the amount of suspected criminal proceeds and their fruits.  
(6) Dangerous goods and prohibited articles that are sealed or seized shall be promptly delivered to relevant departments, or strictly sealed and stored as required by work needs.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　对于需要启封的财物和文件，应当由二名以上调查人员共同办理。重新密封时，由二名以上调查人员在密封材料上签名、记明时间。

**Article 159** The unsealing of property and documents that need to be unsealed shall be handled jointly by two or more investigators. When resealing, two or more investigators shall sign the sealing materials and record the time.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　查封、扣押涉案财物，应当按规定将涉案财物详细信息、《查封/扣押财物、文件清单》录入并上传监察机关涉案财物信息管理系统。  
对于涉案款项，应当在采取措施后十五日以内存入监察机关指定的专用账户。对于涉案物品，应当在采取措施后三十日以内移交涉案财物保管部门保管。因特殊原因不能按时存入专用账户或者移交保管的，应当按规定报批，将保管情况录入涉案财物信息管理系统，在原因消除后及时存入或者移交。

**Article 160** When sealing or seizing property involved in a case, the detailed information of the property involved and the "List of Sealed/Seized Property and Documents" shall be entered into and uploaded to the supervisory authority's information management system for property involved in cases in accordance with the regulations.
