For funds involved in the case, they shall be deposited into the dedicated account designated by the supervisory authority within fifteen days after the measure is taken. For articles involved in the case, they shall be transferred to the custody department for property involved in the case within thirty days after the measure is taken. If, due to special reasons, they cannot be deposited into the dedicated account or transferred for custody on time, approval shall be obtained in accordance with the regulations, the custody situation shall be entered into the information management system for property involved in cases, and the deposit or transfer shall be made promptly after the reasons are eliminated.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　对于已移交涉案财物保管部门保管的涉案财物，根据调查工作需要，经审批可以临时调用，并应当确保完好。调用结束后，应当及时归还。调用和归还时，调查人员、保管人员应当当面清点查验。保管部门应当对调用和归还情况进行登记，全程录像并上传涉案财物信息管理系统。

**Article 161** For property involved in a case that has been transferred to the custody department for safekeeping, it may be temporarily retrieved upon approval as required by the investigation work, and its integrity shall be ensured. After the retrieval is completed, it shall be returned promptly. When retrieving and returning, the investigators and custody personnel shall check and verify the property in person. The custody department shall register the retrieval and return, video record the entire process, and upload it to the information management system for property involved in cases.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　对于被扣押的股票、债券、基金份额等财产，以及即将到期的汇票、本票、支票，依法需要出售或者变现的，按照本条例关于出售冻结财产的规定办理。

**Article 162** With respect to seized property such as stocks, bonds, fund shares, and bills of exchange, promissory notes, or checks that are about to mature, if they need to be sold or realized according to law, the provisions of these Regulations on the sale of frozen property shall apply.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　监察机关接受司法机关、其他监察机关等国家机关移送的涉案财物后，该国家机关采取的查封、扣押期限届满，监察机关续行查封、扣押的顺位与该国家机关查封、扣押的顺位相同。

**Article 163** After the supervisory authority accepts property involved in a case transferred by a judicial authority, another supervisory authority, or other state authority, if the period of sealing or seizure taken by that state authority expires, the priority of the supervisory authority's continued sealing or seizure shall be the same as the priority of that state authority's sealing or seizure.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　对查封、扣押的财物和文件，应当及时进行核查。经查明与案件无关的，经审批，应当在查明后三日以内解除查封、扣押，予以退还。解除查封、扣押的，应当向有关单位、原持有人或者近亲属送达《解除查封/扣押通知书》，附《解除查封/扣押财物、文件清单》，要求其签名或者盖章。

**Article 164** The sealed or seized property and documents shall be verified promptly. If it is found that they are irrelevant to the case, upon approval, the sealing or seizure shall be lifted and the items returned within three days after the finding. When lifting the sealing or seizure, a "Notice on Lifting of Sealing/Seizure" shall be served on the relevant entity, the original holder, or near relatives, accompanied by a "List of Property and Documents for Which Sealing/Seizure Is Lifted," and their signature or seal shall be requested.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　在立案调查之前，对监察对象及相关人员主动上交的涉案财物，经审批可以接收。  
接收时，应当由二名以上调查人员，会同持有人和见证人进行清点核对，当场填写《主动上交财物登记表》。调查人员、持有人和见证人应当在登记表上签名或者盖章。  
对于主动上交的财物，应当根据立案及调查情况及时决定是否依法查封、扣押。

**Article 165** Before a case is placed on file for investigation, property involved in a case voluntarily surrendered by a person subject to supervision or relevant persons may be accepted upon approval.  
When accepting, two or more investigators, together with the holder and a witness, shall count and check the property, and fill in a "Registration Form for Voluntarily Surrendered Property" on the spot. The investigators, holder, and witness shall sign or seal the registration form.  
With respect to the voluntarily surrendered property, a decision shall be made promptly, based on the case filing and investigation circumstances, on whether to seal or seize it according to law.

#### 第十四节　勘验检查、调查实验

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　监察机关按规定报批后，可以依法对与违法犯罪有关的场所、物品、人身、尸体、电子数据等进行勘验检查。

**Article 166** After reporting and obtaining approval in accordance with the regulations, the supervisory authority may, according to law, conduct inspections and examinations of places, articles, persons, corpses, electronic data, etc., that are related to illegal acts or crimes.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　依法需要勘验检查的，应当制作《勘验检查证》；需要委托勘验检查的，应当出具《委托勘验检查书》，送具有专门知识的人办理。

**Article 167** If inspection and examination are required according to law, a "Certificate for Inspection and Examination" shall be issued; if inspection and examination need to be entrusted, a "Letter of Entrustment for Inspection and Examination" shall be issued and sent to a person with specialized knowledge for handling.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　勘验检查应当由二名以上调查人员主持，邀请与案件无关的见证人在场。勘验检查情况应当制作笔录，并由参加勘验检查人员和见证人签名或者盖章。  
勘验检查现场、拆封电子数据存储介质应当全程同步录音录像。对现场情况应当拍摄现场照片、制作现场图，并由勘验检查人员签名或者盖章。

**Article 168** Inspection and examination shall be presided over by two or more investigators, and witnesses unrelated to the case shall be invited to be present. The circumstances of the inspection and examination shall be recorded in a written record, which shall be signed or sealed by the persons participating in the inspection and examination and the witnesses.  
The inspection and examination of the scene and the unsealing of electronic data storage media shall be simultaneously audio and video recorded throughout the entire process. Photographs of the scene shall be taken and a scene diagram shall be made, which shall be signed or sealed by the inspection and examination personnel.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条　为了确定被调查人或者相关人员的某些特征、伤害情况或者生理状态，可以依法对其人身进行检查。必要时可以聘请法医或者医师进行人身检查。检查女性身体，应当由女性工作人员或者医师进行。被调查人拒绝检查的，可以依法强制检查。  
人身检查不得采用损害被检查人生命、健康或者贬低其名誉、人格的方法。对人身检查过程中知悉的个人隐私，应当严格保密。  
对人身检查的情况应当制作笔录，由参加检查的调查人员、检查人员、被检查人员和见证人签名或者盖章。被检查人员拒绝签名、盖章的，调查人员应当在笔录中记明。

**Article 169** In order to determine certain characteristics, injuries, or physiological conditions of the person under investigation or relevant persons, a physical examination may be conducted on them according to law. If necessary, a forensic doctor or physician may be engaged to conduct the physical examination. A physical examination of a female shall be conducted by a female staff member or physician. If the person under investigation refuses the examination, a compulsory examination may be conducted according to law.  
The physical examination shall not use methods that harm the life or health of the person examined or degrade their reputation or dignity. Personal privacy learned during the physical examination shall be strictly kept confidential.  
A written record of the physical examination shall be made, which shall be signed or sealed by the investigators conducting the examination, the examiners, the person examined, and the witnesses. If the person examined refuses to sign or seal, the investigators shall record it in the written record.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条　为查明案情，在必要的时候，经审批可以依法进行调查实验。调查实验，可以聘请有关专业人员参加，也可以要求被调查人、被害人、证人参加。  
进行调查实验，应当全程同步录音录像，制作调查实验笔录，由参加实验的人签名或者盖章。进行调查实验，禁止一切足以造成危险、侮辱人格的行为。  
调查实验的条件与事件发生时的条件有明显差异，或者存在影响实验结论科学性的其他情形的，调查实验笔录不得作为认定案件的依据。

**Article 170** In order to ascertain the facts of a case, when necessary and upon approval, an investigation experiment may be conducted according to law. For an investigation experiment, relevant professionals may be engaged to participate, and the person under investigation, the victim, and witnesses may also be required to participate.  
When conducting an investigation experiment, audio and video recording shall be made synchronously throughout the process, and a record of the investigation experiment shall be produced, which shall be signed or sealed by the persons participating in the experiment. When conducting an investigation experiment, any act that may cause danger or humiliate human dignity shall be prohibited.  
If there is a significant difference between the conditions of the investigation experiment and the conditions at the time of the incident, or if there are other circumstances affecting the scientific nature of the experiment conclusions, the record of the investigation experiment shall not be used as the basis for determining the case.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条　调查人员在必要时，可以依法让被害人、证人和被调查人对与违法犯罪有关的物品、文件、尸体或者场所进行辨认；也可以让被害人、证人对被调查人进行辨认，或者让被调查人对涉案人员进行辨认。  
辨认工作应当由二名以上调查人员主持进行。在辨认前，应当向辨认人详细询问辨认对象的具体特征，避免辨认人见到辨认对象，并告知辨认人作虚假辨认应当承担的法律责任。几名辨认人对同一辨认对象进行辨认时，应当由辨认人个别进行。辨认应当形成笔录，并由调查人员、辨认人签名或者盖章。

**Article 171** When necessary, investigators may, according to law, have the victim, witnesses, and the person under investigation identify objects, documents, corpses, or places related to the illegal or criminal acts; they may also have the victim and witnesses identify the person under investigation, or have the person under investigation identify the persons involved in the case.  
The identification work shall be presided over by two or more investigators. Before the identification, the identifier shall be asked in detail about the specific characteristics of the identification target, the identifier shall be prevented from seeing the identification target, and the identifier shall be informed of the legal liability to be borne for making a false identification. When several identifiers identify the same identification target, the identification shall be conducted individually by the identifiers. A record of the identification shall be produced, and shall be signed or sealed by the investigators and the identifier.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　辨认人员时，被辨认的人数不得少于七人，照片不得少于十张。  
辨认人不愿公开进行辨认时，应当在不暴露辨认人的情况下进行辨认，并为其保守秘密。

**Article 172** When identifying persons, the number of persons to be identified shall not be less than seven, and the number of photographs shall not be less than ten.  
When the identifier is unwilling to conduct the identification publicly, the identification shall be conducted without exposing the identifier, and the identifier's identity shall be kept confidential.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　组织辨认物品时一般应当辨认实物。被辨认的物品系名贵字画等贵重物品或者存在不便搬运等情况的，可以对实物照片进行辨认。辨认人进行辨认时，应当在辨认出的实物照片与附纸骑缝上捺指印予以确认，在附纸上写明该实物涉案情况并签名、捺指印。  
辨认物品时，同类物品不得少于五件，照片不得少于五张。  
对于难以找到相似物品的特定物，可以将该物品照片交由辨认人进行确认后，在照片与附纸骑缝上捺指印，在附纸上写明该物品涉案情况并签名、捺指印。在辨认人确认前，应当向其详细询问物品的具体特征，并对确认过程和结果形成笔录。

**Article 173** When organizing the identification of objects, the physical object shall generally be identified. If the object to be identified is a valuable item such as a famous painting or calligraphy, or if there are circumstances such as inconvenience in moving, the photograph of the physical object may be identified. When the identifier conducts the identification, the identifier shall place a fingerprint on the joint edge between the photograph of the identified physical object and the attached paper to confirm, and shall write on the attached paper the circumstances of the object's involvement in the case and sign and place a fingerprint.  
When identifying objects, there shall not be less than five similar objects, and the number of photographs shall not be less than five.  
For a specific object that is difficult to find similar objects, the photograph of the object may be handed over to the identifier for confirmation, and then a fingerprint shall be placed on the joint edge between the photograph and the attached paper, and the circumstances of the object's involvement in the case shall be written on the attached paper, and the identifier shall sign and place a fingerprint. Before the identifier confirms, the specific characteristics of the object shall be asked in detail, and a record of the confirmation process and result shall be produced.

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　辨认笔录具有下列情形之一的，不得作为认定案件的依据：  
（一）辨认开始前使辨认人见到辨认对象的；  
（二）辨认活动没有个别进行的；  
（三）辨认对象没有混杂在具有类似特征的其他对象中，或者供辨认的对象数量不符合规定的，但特定辨认对象除外；  
（四）辨认中给辨认人明显暗示或者明显有指认嫌疑的；  
（五）辨认不是在调查人员主持下进行的；  
（六）违反有关规定，不能确定辨认笔录真实性的其他情形。  
辨认笔录存在其他瑕疵的，应当结合全案证据审查其真实性和关联性，作出综合判断。

**Article 174** A record of identification shall not be used as the basis for determining a case under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The identifier was allowed to see the identification target before the identification began;  
(2) The identification activity was not conducted individually;  
(3) The identification target was not mixed with other objects with similar characteristics, or the number of objects provided for identification does not meet the requirements, except for a specific identification target;  
(4) Obvious suggestions were given to the identifier during the identification, or there is obvious suspicion of leading;  
(5) The identification was not conducted under the direction of an investigator;  
(6) Other circumstances in which the authenticity of the record of identification cannot be determined due to violation of relevant provisions.  
If the record of identification has other defects, its authenticity and relevance shall be examined in light of all the evidence in the case, and a comprehensive judgment shall be made.

#### 第十五节　鉴定

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　监察机关为解决案件中的专门性问题，按规定报批后，可以依法进行鉴定。  
鉴定时应当出具《委托鉴定书》，由二名以上调查人员送交具有鉴定资格的鉴定机构、鉴定人进行鉴定。

**Article 175** To resolve specialized issues in a case, the supervisory organ may, after submitting for approval according to the provisions, conduct an expert evaluation according to law.  
When conducting the expert evaluation, a 'Letter of Entrustment for Expert Evaluation' shall be issued, and shall be submitted by two or more investigators to an expert evaluation institution or expert evaluator with the qualification for expert evaluation.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　监察机关可以依法开展下列鉴定：  
（一）对笔迹、印刷文件、污损文件、制成时间不明的文件和以其他形式表现的文件等进行鉴定；  
（二）对案件中涉及的财务会计资料及相关财物进行会计鉴定；  
（三）对被调查人、证人的行为能力进行精神病鉴定；  
（四）对人体造成的损害或者死因进行人身伤亡医学鉴定；  
（五）对录音录像资料进行鉴定；  
（六）对因电子信息技术应用而出现的材料及其派生物进行电子数据鉴定；  
（七）其他可以依法进行的专业鉴定。

**Article 176** The supervisory organ may conduct the following expert evaluations according to law:  
(1) Evaluation of handwriting, printed documents, damaged documents, documents of unknown creation time, and documents presented in other forms;  
(2) Accounting evaluation of financial accounting materials and related property involved in the case;  
(3) Psychiatric evaluation of the capacity for conduct of the person under investigation or witnesses;  
(4) Medical evaluation of personal injury or death for damage to the human body or cause of death;  
(5) Evaluation of audio and video recording materials;  
(6) Electronic data evaluation of materials arising from the application of electronic information technology and their derivatives;  
(7) Other professional evaluations that may be conducted according to law.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　监察机关应当为鉴定提供必要条件，向鉴定人送交有关检材和对比样本等原始材料，介绍与鉴定有关的情况。调查人员应当明确提出要求鉴定事项，但不得暗示或者强迫鉴定人作出某种鉴定意见。  
监察机关应当做好检材的保管和送检工作，记明检材送检环节的责任人，确保检材在流转环节的同一性和不被污染。

**Article 177** The supervisory organ shall provide necessary conditions for the expert evaluation, submit relevant original materials such as the materials to be examined and comparison samples to the expert evaluator, and introduce the circumstances related to the evaluation. The investigator shall clearly specify the matters requiring evaluation, but shall not suggest or compel the expert evaluator to produce a particular evaluation opinion.  
The supervisory organ shall properly manage the storage and submission of the materials to be examined, record the person responsible for each stage of submission, and ensure the identity and non-contamination of the materials to be examined during the process of transfer.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　鉴定人应当在出具的鉴定意见上签名，并附鉴定机构和鉴定人的资质证明或者其他证明文件。多个鉴定人的鉴定意见不一致的，应当在鉴定意见上记明分歧的内容和理由，并且分别签名。  
监察机关对于法庭审理中依法决定鉴定人出庭作证的，应当予以协调。  
鉴定人故意作虚假鉴定的，应当依法追究法律责任。

**Article 178** The expert evaluator shall sign the evaluation opinion issued, and attach the qualification certificate or other certifying documents of the evaluation institution and the evaluator. If the opinions of multiple evaluators are inconsistent, the content and reasons for the disagreement shall be recorded in the evaluation opinion, and each evaluator shall sign separately.  
If the court decides during trial that an expert evaluator shall testify in court according to law, the supervisory organ shall coordinate.  
If an expert evaluator intentionally makes a false evaluation, legal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　调查人员应当对鉴定意见进行审查。对经审查作为证据使用的鉴定意见，应当告知被调查人及相关单位、人员，送达《鉴定意见告知书》。  
被调查人或者相关单位、人员提出补充鉴定或者重新鉴定申请，经审查符合法定要求的，应当按规定报批，进行补充鉴定或者重新鉴定。  
对鉴定意见告知情况可以制作笔录，载明告知内容和被告知人的意见等。

**Article 179** The investigator shall review the evaluation opinion. If the evaluation opinion is to be used as evidence after review, the person under investigation and the relevant entities and persons shall be notified, and the 'Notice of Evaluation Opinion' shall be served.  
If the person under investigation or the relevant entity or person applies for a supplementary evaluation or re-evaluation, and upon review it meets the statutory requirements, approval shall be obtained according to the provisions, and a supplementary evaluation or re-evaluation shall be conducted.  
A record may be made of the notification of the evaluation opinion, stating the content of the notification and the opinions of the notified person, etc.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　经审查具有下列情形之一的，应当补充鉴定：  
（一）鉴定内容有明显遗漏的；  
（二）发现新的有鉴定意义的证物的；  
（三）对鉴定证物有新的鉴定要求的；  
（四）鉴定意见不完整，委托事项无法确定的；  
（五）其他需要补充鉴定的情形。

**Article 180** A supplementary evaluation shall be conducted upon review under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) There is a clear omission in the evaluation content;  
(2) New evidence objects of significance for evaluation are discovered;  
(3) There are new evaluation requirements for the evaluated evidence objects;  
(4) The evaluation opinion is incomplete, and the entrusted matters cannot be determined;  
(5) Other circumstances requiring supplementary evaluation.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　经审查具有下列情形之一的，应当重新鉴定：  
（一）鉴定程序违法或者违反相关专业技术要求的；  
（二）鉴定机构、鉴定人不具备鉴定资质和条件的；  
（三）鉴定人故意作出虚假鉴定或者违反回避规定的；  
（四）鉴定意见依据明显不足的；  
（五）检材虚假或者被损坏的；  
（六）其他应当重新鉴定的情形。  
决定重新鉴定的，应当另行确定鉴定机构和鉴定人。

**Article 181** A re-evaluation shall be conducted upon review under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The evaluation procedure is illegal or violates relevant professional and technical requirements;  
(2) The evaluation institution or evaluator does not have the qualification and conditions for evaluation;  
(3) The evaluator intentionally makes a false evaluation or violates the rules of recusal;  
(4) The basis for the evaluation opinion is clearly insufficient;  
(5) The materials to be examined are false or damaged;  
(6) Other circumstances requiring re-evaluation.  
If a re-evaluation is decided, a different evaluation institution and evaluator shall be designated.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　因无鉴定机构，或者根据法律法规等规定，监察机关可以指派、聘请具有专门知识的人就案件的专门性问题出具报告。

**Article 182** If there is no evaluation institution, or according to laws and regulations, the supervisory organ may assign or engage persons with specialized knowledge to issue a report on the specialized issues of the case.

#### 第十六节　技术调查

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　监察机关根据调查涉嫌重大贪污贿赂等职务犯罪需要，依照规定的权限和程序报经批准，可以依法采取技术调查措施，按照规定交公安机关或者国家有关执法机关依法执行。

**Article 183** When necessary for the investigation of suspected major duty crimes such as embezzlement or bribery, the supervisory organ may, after reporting and obtaining approval in accordance with the prescribed authority and procedures, take technical investigation measures according to law, and shall, according to the provisions, hand over the matter to the public security organ or relevant state law enforcement organs for execution according to law.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　依法采取技术调查措施的，监察机关应当出具《采取技术调查措施委托函》《采取技术调查措施决定书》和《采取技术调查措施适用对象情况表》，送交有关机关执行。其中，设区的市级以下监察机关委托有关执行机关采取技术调查措施，还应当提供《立案决定书》。

**Article 184** If technical investigation measures are taken according to law, the supervisory organ shall issue a 'Letter of Entrustment for Taking Technical Investigation Measures', a 'Decision on Taking Technical Investigation Measures', and a 'Table of Objects Applicable to Technical Investigation Measures', and submit them to the relevant organ for execution. Among these, if a supervisory organ at or below the level of a city divided into districts entrusts the relevant executing organ to take technical investigation measures, it shall also provide a 'Decision on Filing a Case'.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　技术调查措施的期限按照监察法的规定执行，期限届满前未办理延期手续的，到期自动解除。  
对于不需要继续采取技术调查措施的，监察机关应当按规定及时报批，将《解除技术调查措施决定书》送交有关机关执行。  
需要依法变更技术调查措施种类或者增加适用对象的，监察机关应当重新办理报批和委托手续，依法送交有关机关执行。

**Article 185** The duration of technical investigation measures shall be implemented in accordance with the provisions of the Supervision Law. If the extension procedures are not completed before the expiration of the duration, the measures shall be automatically lifted upon expiration.  
If technical investigation measures are no longer needed, the supervisory organ shall promptly report and obtain approval according to the provisions, and submit the 'Decision on Lifting Technical Investigation Measures' to the relevant organ for execution.  
If it is necessary to change the type of technical investigation measures or add applicable objects according to law, the supervisory organ shall re-process the reporting, approval, and entrustment procedures, and submit the matter to the relevant organ for execution according to law.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　对于采取技术调查措施收集的信息和材料，依法需要作为刑事诉讼证据使用的，监察机关应当按规定报批，出具《调取技术调查证据材料通知书》向有关执行机关调取。  
对于采取技术调查措施收集的物证、书证及其他证据材料，监察机关应当制作书面说明，写明获取证据的时间、地点、数量、特征以及采取技术调查措施的批准机关、种类等。调查人员应当在书面说明上签名。  
对于采取技术调查措施获取的证据材料，如果使用该证据材料可能危及有关人员的人身安全，或者可能产生其他严重后果的，应当采取不暴露有关人员身份、技术方法等保护措施。必要时，可以建议由审判人员在庭外进行核实。

**Article 186** If the information and materials collected by taking technical investigation measures need to be used as evidence in criminal proceedings according to law, the supervisory organ shall report and obtain approval according to the provisions, and issue a 'Notice on Obtaining Technical Investigation Evidence Materials' to obtain them from the relevant executing organ.  
For physical evidence, documentary evidence, and other evidence materials collected by taking technical investigation measures, the supervisory organ shall produce a written explanation, stating the time, place, quantity, and characteristics of obtaining the evidence, as well as the approving organ and type of technical investigation measures taken. The investigator shall sign the written explanation.  
If the use of evidence materials obtained by taking technical investigation measures may endanger the personal safety of the relevant persons, or may cause other serious consequences, protective measures such as not exposing the identity of the relevant persons or technical methods shall be taken. When necessary, it may be suggested that the judge verify the evidence outside the court.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　调查人员对采取技术调查措施过程中知悉的国家秘密、工作秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私和个人信息，应当严格保密。  
采取技术调查措施获取的证据、线索及其他有关材料，只能用于对违法犯罪的调查、起诉和审判，不得用于其他用途。  
对采取技术调查措施获取的与案件无关的材料，应当经审批及时销毁。对销毁情况应当制作记录，由调查人员签名。

**Article 187** Investigators shall strictly keep confidential the state secrets, work secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, and personal information learned during the process of taking technical investigation measures.  
The evidence, leads, and other relevant materials obtained by taking technical investigation measures may only be used for the investigation, prosecution, and adjudication of illegal and criminal acts, and shall not be used for other purposes.  
Materials obtained by taking technical investigation measures that are unrelated to the case shall be promptly destroyed upon approval. A record of the destruction shall be made and signed by the investigator.

#### 第十七节　通缉

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　县级以上监察机关对在逃的应当被留置人员，依法决定在本行政区域内通缉的，应当按规定报批，送交同级公安机关执行。送交执行时，应当出具《通缉决定书》，附《留置决定书》等法律文书和被通缉人员信息，以及承办单位、承办人员等有关情况。  
通缉范围超出本行政区域的，应当报有决定权的上级监察机关出具《通缉决定书》，并附《留置决定书》及相关材料，送交同级公安机关执行。

**Article 188** If a supervisory organ at or above the county level decides according to law to issue a wanted notice within its administrative region for a person who should be detained but is at large, it shall report and obtain approval according to the provisions, and submit the matter to the public security organ at the same level for execution. When submitting for execution, a 'Decision on Wanted Notice' shall be issued, accompanied by legal documents such as the 'Decision on Custody' and the information of the wanted person, as well as the undertaking unit and undertaking personnel and other relevant circumstances.  
If the scope of the wanted notice exceeds the administrative region, the matter shall be reported to the superior supervisory organ with the authority to decide to issue a 'Decision on Wanted Notice', accompanied by the 'Decision on Custody' and related materials, and submitted to the public security organ at the same level for execution.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　国家监察委员会依法需要提请公安部对在逃人员发布公安部通缉令的，应当先提请公安部采取网上追逃措施。如情况紧急，可以向公安部同时出具《通缉决定书》和《提请采取网上追逃措施函》。  
省级以下监察机关报请国家监察委员会提请公安部发布公安部通缉令的，应当先提请本地公安机关采取网上追逃措施。

**Article 189** If the National Commission of Supervision needs to request the Ministry of Public Security to issue a wanted order of the Ministry of Public Security for a person at large according to law, it shall first request the Ministry of Public Security to take online pursuit measures. If the situation is urgent, it may simultaneously submit the 'Decision on Wanted Notice' and the 'Letter Requesting Online Pursuit Measures' to the Ministry of Public Security.  
If a supervisory organ below the provincial level reports to the National Commission of Supervision to request the Ministry of Public Security to issue a wanted order of the Ministry of Public Security, it shall first request the local public security organ to take online pursuit measures.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　监察机关接到公安机关抓获被通缉人员的通知后，应当立即核实被抓获人员身份，并在接到通知后二十四小时以内派员办理交接手续。边远或者交通不便地区，至迟不得超过三日。  
公安机关在移交前，将被抓获人员送往当地监察机关留置场所临时看管的，当地监察机关应当接收，并保障临时看管期间的安全，对工作信息严格保密。  
监察机关需要提请公安机关协助将被抓获人员带回的，应当按规定报批，请本地同级公安机关依法予以协助。提请协助时，应当出具《提请协助采取留置措施函》，附《留置决定书》复印件及相关材料。

**Article 190** : Upon receipt of a notice from a public security organ that a wanted person has been captured, the supervisory organ shall promptly verify the identity of the captured person and shall, within twenty-four hours after receipt of the notice, dispatch personnel to handle the transfer procedures. In remote or traffic-inconvenient areas, the time limit shall be no more than three days at the latest.  
If, before the transfer, the public security organ sends the captured person to the local supervisory organ's detention facility for temporary custody, the local supervisory organ shall accept the person, ensure safety during the period of temporary custody, and maintain strict confidentiality regarding work information.  
Where the supervisory organ needs to request the public security organ's assistance in bringing back the captured person, it shall submit for approval in accordance with regulations and request the local public security organ at the same level to provide assistance according to law. When requesting assistance, it shall issue a "Letter Requesting Assistance in Taking Detention Measures," together with a copy of the "Detention Decision" and relevant materials.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　监察机关对于被通缉人员已经归案、死亡，或者依法撤销留置决定以及发现有其他不需要继续采取通缉措施情形的，应当经审批出具《撤销通缉通知书》，送交协助采取原措施的公安机关执行。需要撤销网上追逃措施的，监察机关应当出具《撤销网上追逃通知书》，送交协助采取原措施的公安机关执行。

**Article 191** : Where a wanted person has been arrested, is deceased, the detention decision has been revoked according to law, or other circumstances arise where continued wanted measures are no longer necessary, the supervisory organ shall, upon approval, issue a "Notice of Revocation of Wanted Notice" and deliver it to the public security organ that assisted in implementing the original measures for execution. Where it is necessary to revoke an online pursuit measure, the supervisory organ shall issue a "Notice of Revocation of Online Pursuit" and deliver it to the public security organ that assisted in implementing the original measures for execution.

#### 第十八节　限制出境

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　监察机关为防止被调查人及相关人员逃匿境外，按规定报批后，可以依法决定采取限制出境措施，交由移民管理机构依法执行。

**Article 192** : To prevent the person under investigation and related persons from fleeing abroad, the supervisory organ may, after reporting and obtaining approval in accordance with regulations, decide according to law to take measures to restrict exit from the country, and shall hand these over to the immigration administration authority for enforcement according to law.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　监察机关采取限制出境措施应当出具有关函件，与采取限制出境措施决定书等文书材料一并送交移民管理机构执行。其中，采取边控措施的，应当附《边控对象通知书》；采取法定不批准出境措施的，应当附《法定不准出境人员报备表》。

**Article 193** : When a supervisory organ takes measures to restrict exit from the country, it shall issue relevant official letters and deliver them together with the decision on taking exit restriction measures and other documents to the immigration administration authority for execution. Where border control measures are taken, a "Notice of Border Control Target" shall be attached; where statutory measures prohibiting exit are taken, a "Registration Form for Persons Statutory Prohibited from Exit" shall be attached.

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　限制出境措施有效期不超过三个月，到期自动解除。  
到期后仍有必要继续采取边控措施的，应当按原程序报批。承办部门应当出具有关函件，在到期前与《延长限制出境措施期限决定书》一并送交移民管理机构执行。延长期限每次不得超过三个月。  
到期后仍有必要继续采取法定不批准出境措施的，应当在报备期满三日前按规定再次办理法定不批准出境人员报备手续。

**Article 194** : The validity period of exit restriction measures shall not exceed three months and shall be automatically lifted upon expiration.  
Where it remains necessary to continue border control measures after expiration, approval shall be sought in accordance with the original procedure. The responsible department shall issue relevant official letters, and deliver them together with the "Decision on Extending the Period of Exit Restriction Measures" to the immigration administration authority for execution before expiration. Each extension shall not exceed three months.  
Where it remains necessary to continue statutory measures prohibiting exit after expiration, the registration procedures for persons statutorily prohibited from exit shall be handled again in accordance with regulations three days before the registration period expires.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　监察机关接到口岸移民管理机构查获被决定采取留置措施的边控对象的通知后，应当于二十四小时以内到达口岸办理移交手续。无法及时到达的，应当委托当地监察机关及时前往口岸办理移交手续。当地监察机关应当予以协助。

**Article 195** : Upon receipt of a notice from a port immigration administration authority that a border control target subject to a detention decision has been captured, the supervisory organ shall arrive at the port within twenty-four hours to handle the transfer procedures. If it cannot arrive in a timely manner, it shall entrust the local supervisory organ to go to the port promptly to handle the transfer procedures. The local supervisory organ shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　对于不需要继续采取限制出境措施的，应当按规定报批，及时予以解除。承办部门应当出具有关函件，与《解除限制出境措施决定书》一并送交移民管理机构执行。

**Article 196** : Where exit restriction measures are no longer necessary, approval shall be obtained in accordance with regulations, and the measures shall be lifted in a timely manner. The responsible department shall issue relevant official letters and deliver them together with the "Decision on Lifting Exit Restriction Measures" to the immigration administration authority for execution.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　县级以上监察机关在重要紧急情况下，经审批可以依法直接向口岸所在地口岸移民管理机构提请办理临时限制出境措施，期限不超过七日，不能延期。

**Article 197** : In important and urgent circumstances, a supervisory organ at or above the county level may, upon approval, directly request the port immigration administration authority at the location of the port to handle temporary exit restriction measures according to law, with a period not exceeding seven days and no extension permitted.

### 第五章　监察程序

#### 第一节　线索处置

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　监察机关应当对问题线索归口受理、集中管理、分类处置、定期清理。

**Article 198** : The supervisory organ shall accept problem leads through centralized channels, manage them in a centralized manner, classify and dispose of them, and conduct periodic clearance.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　监察机关对于报案或者举报应当依法接受。属于本级监察机关管辖的，依法予以受理；属于其他监察机关管辖的，应当在五个工作日以内予以转送。  
监察机关可以向下级监察机关发函交办检举控告，并进行督办，下级监察机关应当按期回复办理结果。

**Article 199** : The supervisory organ shall accept reports or tip-offs according to law. If the matter falls within the jurisdiction of the supervisory organ at the same level, it shall accept the case according to law; if it falls within the jurisdiction of another supervisory organ, it shall transfer the case within five working days.  
A supervisory organ may send a letter to a lower-level supervisory organ assigning a complaint or accusation for handling and supervise its handling, and the lower-level supervisory organ shall report the handling results within the prescribed time limit.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　对于涉嫌职务违法或者职务犯罪的公职人员自动投案的，应当依法接待和办理。

**Article 200** : Where a public official who is suspected of duty-related illegal conduct or duty-related crimes voluntarily surrenders, the organ shall receive and handle the matter according to law.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　监察机关对于执法机关、司法机关等其他机关移送的问题线索，应当及时审核，并按照下列方式办理：  
（一）本单位有管辖权的，及时研究提出处置意见；  
（二）本单位没有管辖权但其他监察机关有管辖权的，在五个工作日以内转送有管辖权的监察机关；  
（三）本单位对部分问题线索有管辖权的，对有管辖权的部分提出处置意见，并及时将其他问题线索转送有管辖权的机关；  
（四）监察机关没有管辖权的，及时退回移送机关。

**Article 201** : A supervisory organ shall promptly review problem leads transferred by law enforcement organs, judicial organs, or other organs, and handle them in the following manner:  
(1) If the organ has jurisdiction, it shall promptly study and propose disposal opinions;  
(2) If the organ does not have jurisdiction but another supervisory organ does, it shall transfer the leads to the supervisory organ with jurisdiction within five working days;  
(3) If the organ has jurisdiction over part of the problem leads, it shall propose disposal opinions regarding the part within its jurisdiction and promptly transfer the other problem leads to the organ with jurisdiction;  
(4) If no supervisory organ has jurisdiction, it shall promptly return the leads to the transferring organ.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　信访举报部门归口受理本机关管辖监察对象涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪问题的检举控告，统一接收有关监察机关以及其他单位移送的相关检举控告，移交本机关监督检查部门或者相关部门，并将移交情况通报案件监督管理部门。  
案件监督管理部门统一接收巡视巡察机构和审计机关、执法机关、司法机关等其他机关移送的职务违法和职务犯罪问题线索，按程序移交本机关监督检查部门或者相关部门办理。  
监督检查部门、调查部门在工作中发现的相关问题线索，属于本部门受理范围的，应当报送案件监督管理部门备案；属于本机关其他部门受理范围的，经审批后移交案件监督管理部门分办。

**Article 202** : The department for letters and visits and reports shall centrally receive complaints and accusations concerning duty-related illegal conduct and duty-related crimes against supervisees under the jurisdiction of the organ, uniformly accept relevant complaints and accusations transferred by relevant supervisory organs and other units, transfer them to the organ's supervision and inspection department or relevant departments, and notify the case supervision and management department of the transfer.  
The case supervision and management department shall uniformly receive leads on duty-related illegal conduct and duty-related crimes transferred by inspection and tour institutions, audit organs, law enforcement organs, judicial organs, and other organs, and transfer them according to procedures to the organ's supervision and inspection department or relevant departments for handling.  
If problem leads discovered by the supervision and inspection department or investigation department in the course of work fall within the scope of that department, they shall be submitted to the case supervision and management department for filing; if they fall within the scope of other departments of the organ, they shall, upon approval, be transferred to the case supervision and management department for distribution.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　案件监督管理部门应当对问题线索实行集中管理、动态更新，定期汇总、核对问题线索及处置情况，向监察机关主要负责人报告，并向相关部门通报。  
问题线索承办部门应当指定专人负责管理线索，逐件编号登记、建立管理台账。线索管理处置各环节应当由经手人员签名，全程登记备查，及时与案件监督管理部门核对。

**Article 203** : The case supervision and management department shall exercise centralized management and dynamic updating of problem leads, periodically summarize and verify problem leads and their disposal status, report to the principal responsible person of the supervisory organ, and notify relevant departments.  
The department responsible for handling problem leads shall designate specific personnel to manage the leads, register each lead with a number, and establish a management ledger. Each link of lead management and disposal shall be signed by the handling personnel, recorded in full for reference, and promptly cross-checked with the case supervision and management department.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　监督检查部门应当结合问题线索所涉及地区、部门、单位总体情况进行综合分析，提出处置意见并制定处置方案，经审批按照适当了解、谈话、函询、初步核实、暂存待查、予以了结等方式进行处置，或者按照职责移送调查部门处置。

**Article 204** : The supervision and inspection department shall conduct comprehensive analysis in light of the overall situation of the region, department, or unit involved in the problem leads, propose disposal opinions and formulate a disposal plan, and upon approval, dispose of them by means such as appropriate inquiry, conversation, correspondence inquiry, preliminary verification, temporary storage pending investigation, or closing the matter, or transfer them to the investigation department for disposal in accordance with its functions.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　采取适当了解方式处置问题线索，应当按规定报批后，依法依规向有关单位和个人了解情况，验证问题的真实性，不得采取限制人身、财产权利的措施，不得与被反映人接触。  
承办部门应当根据适当了解的情况，提出谈话、函询、初步核实、拟立案调查、予以了结、暂存待查，或者移送有关部门、机关处理等建议，按程序报批后办理。

**Article 205** : Where problem leads are disposed of by means of appropriate inquiry, after reporting and obtaining approval in accordance with regulations, the supervisory organ shall, according to law and regulations, inquire about the circumstances from relevant units and individuals to verify the authenticity of the problem, and shall not take measures restricting personal or property rights, nor contact the person against whom the report is made.  
The responsible department shall, based on the results of the appropriate inquiry, propose recommendations such as conversation, correspondence inquiry, preliminary verification, proposed case filing investigation, closing the matter, temporary storage pending investigation, or transfer to relevant departments or organs for handling, and shall handle the matter after approval in accordance with procedures.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　采取谈话方式处置问题线索的，按照[本条例第七十八条](#art-78)、第七十九条规定办理。  
函询应当以监察机关办公厅（室）名义发函给被反映人，并抄送其所在单位和派驻监察机构主要负责人。被函询人应当在收到函件后十五个工作日以内写出说明材料，由其所在单位主要负责人签署意见后发函回复。被函询人为所在单位主要负责人的，或者被函询人所作说明涉及所在单位主要负责人的，应当直接发函回复监察机关。  
被函询人已经退休的，按照第二款规定程序办理。  
监察机关根据工作需要，经审批可以对谈话、函询情况进行抽查核实。  
承办部门应当根据谈话、函询的情况，提出初步核实、拟立案调查、予以了结、暂存待查，或者移送有关部门、机关处理等建议，按程序报批后办理。

**Article 206** : Where problem leads are disposed of by means of conversation, the provisions of Articles 78 and 79 of these Regulations shall apply.  
For correspondence inquiry, a letter shall be sent in the name of the general office (chamber) of the supervisory organ to the person against whom the report is made, with copies to the principal responsible person of the unit to which the person belongs and the dispatched supervisory organ. The person receiving the correspondence inquiry shall, within fifteen working days after receiving the letter, prepare an explanatory statement, which shall be signed by the principal responsible person of the unit to which the person belongs and returned by letter. If the person receiving the correspondence inquiry is the principal responsible person of the unit, or if the explanation provided by the person involves the principal responsible person of the unit, the person shall directly return the letter to the supervisory organ.  
Where the person receiving the correspondence inquiry has already retired, the procedures under paragraph 2 shall apply.  
The supervisory organ may, as needed for work and upon approval, conduct spot checks to verify the situations of conversation or correspondence inquiry.  
The responsible department shall, based on the situations of conversation or correspondence inquiry, propose recommendations such as preliminary verification, proposed case filing investigation, closing the matter, temporary storage pending investigation, or transfer to relevant departments or organs for handling, and shall handle the matter after approval in accordance with procedures.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　监察机关对具有可查性的职务违法和职务犯罪问题线索，应当按规定报批后，依法开展初步核实工作。采取初步核实方式处置问题线索，应当确定初步核实对象，制定工作方案，明确需要核实的问题和采取的措施，成立核查组。  
在初步核实中应当注重收集客观性证据，确保真实性和准确性。在初步核实中发现或者受理被核查人新的具有可查性的问题线索的，应当经审批纳入原初核方案开展核查。  
核查组在初步核实工作结束后应当撰写初步核实情况报告，列明被核查人基本情况、反映的主要问题、办理依据、初步核实结果、存在疑点、处理建议，由全体人员签名。  
承办部门应当综合分析初步核实情况，提出拟立案调查、予以了结、暂存待查，或者移送有关部门、机关处理等建议，按照批准初步核实的程序报批。

**Article 207** : Where a supervisory organ has problem leads regarding duty-related illegal conduct or duty-related crimes that are verifiable, it shall, after reporting and obtaining approval in accordance with regulations, carry out preliminary verification according to law. Where problem leads are disposed of by means of preliminary verification, the target of preliminary verification shall be determined, a work plan shall be formulated, the issues to be verified and the measures to be taken shall be clarified, and a verification team shall be established.  
During preliminary verification, emphasis shall be placed on collecting objective evidence to ensure authenticity and accuracy. If, during preliminary verification, new verifiable problem leads concerning the person under verification are discovered or received, they shall, upon approval, be incorporated into the original preliminary verification plan for verification.  
Upon completion of the preliminary verification, the verification team shall draft a report on the preliminary verification, stating the basic information of the person under verification, the main issues reported, the basis for handling, the results of the preliminary verification, any existing doubts, and recommendations for handling, and all members shall sign it.  
The responsible department shall conduct a comprehensive analysis of the preliminary verification situation and propose recommendations such as proposed case filing investigation, closing the matter, temporary storage pending investigation, or transfer to relevant departments or organs for handling, and shall report for approval in accordance with the procedures for approving preliminary verification.

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　监察机关根据适当了解、谈话、函询或者初步核实情况，发现公职人员有职务违法行为但情节较轻的，可以按照[本条例第二百三十一条](#art-231)规定处理，予以了结。

**Article 208** : Where, based on the results of appropriate inquiry, conversation, correspondence inquiry, or preliminary verification, a supervisory organ discovers that a public official has committed duty-related illegal conduct but the circumstances are minor, it may handle the matter in accordance with Article 231 of these Regulations and close the matter.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　检举控告人使用本人真实姓名或者本单位名称，有电话等具体联系方式的，属于实名检举控告。监察机关对实名检举控告应当优先办理、优先处置，依法给予答复。虽有署名但不是检举控告人真实姓名（单位名称）或者无法验证的检举控告，按照匿名检举控告处理。  
信访举报部门对属于本机关受理的实名检举控告，应当在收到检举控告之日起十五个工作日以内按规定告知实名检举控告人受理情况，并做好记录。  
调查人员应当将实名检举控告的处理结果在办结之日起十五个工作日以内向检举控告人反馈，并记录反馈情况。对检举控告人提出异议的应当如实记录，并向其进行说明；对提供新证据材料的，应当依法核查处理。

**Article 209** : A complaint or accusation submitted by a complainant using his or her true name or the name of his or her unit, with a specific contact method such as a telephone number, shall be a real-name complaint or accusation. The supervisory organ shall give priority to handling and disposing of real-name complaints or accusations, and shall provide a response according to law. A complaint or accusation that bears a signature but is not the true name (or unit name) of the complainant, or cannot be verified, shall be treated as an anonymous complaint or accusation.  
The department for letters and visits and reports shall, for real-name complaints or accusations within the organ's jurisdiction, notify the complainant of the acceptance status in accordance with regulations within fifteen working days from the date of receiving the complaint or accusation, and keep a record.  
The investigator shall provide feedback to the complainant on the handling results of the real-name complaint or accusation within fifteen working days from the date of completion, and record the feedback. If the complainant raises objections, they shall be truthfully recorded and an explanation shall be provided; if new material evidence is provided, it shall be verified and handled according to law.

#### 第二节　立案

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　监察机关已经掌握监察对象涉嫌职务违法或者职务犯罪的部分事实和证据，认为需要追究其法律责任的，应当按规定报批后，依法立案调查。

**Article 210** Where a supervisory organ has already grasped some facts and evidence that the subject of supervision is suspected of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, and deems it necessary to investigate his/her legal liability, it shall report for approval in accordance with the provisions and then initiate an investigation according to law.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　监察机关立案调查职务违法或者职务犯罪案件，需要对涉嫌行贿犯罪、介绍贿赂犯罪或者共同职务犯罪的涉案人员立案调查的，应当一并办理立案手续。需要交由下级监察机关立案的，经审批交由下级监察机关办理立案手续。  
对单位涉嫌受贿、行贿等职务犯罪，需要追究法律责任的，依法对该单位办理立案调查手续。对事故（事件）中存在职务违法或者职务犯罪问题，需要追究法律责任，但相关责任人员尚不明确的，可以以事立案。对单位立案或者以事立案后，经调查确定相关责任人员的，按照管理权限报批确定被调查人。  
监察机关根据人民法院生效刑事判决、裁定和人民检察院不起诉决定认定的事实，需要对监察对象给予政务处分的，可以由相关监督检查部门依据司法机关的生效判决、裁定、决定及其认定的事实、性质和情节，提出给予政务处分的意见，按程序移送审理。对依法被追究行政法律责任的监察对象，需要给予政务处分的，应当依法办理立案手续。

**Article 211** Where a supervisory organ initiates an investigation into a case of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, and it is necessary to initiate an investigation into persons involved who are suspected of the crime of bribing, the crime of introducing bribes, or joint duty-related crimes, it shall handle the case-filing procedures simultaneously. If it is necessary to hand over the case to a lower-level supervisory organ for case-filing, it shall, after examination and approval, hand it over to the lower-level supervisory organ for handling the case-filing procedures.  
Where a unit is suspected of duty-related crimes such as acceptance of bribes or offering bribes, and it is necessary to investigate its legal liability, case-filing and investigation procedures shall be handled for the unit according to law. Where there are problems of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime in an accident (incident) and it is necessary to investigate legal liability, but the relevant responsible persons are not yet clear, case-filing may be made on the basis of the incident. After case-filing against a unit or on the basis of an incident, if the relevant responsible persons are determined through investigation, the person under investigation shall be determined after reporting for approval in accordance with the management authority.  
Where, based on the facts ascertained by the effective criminal judgments and rulings of the People's Court and the decision of the People's Procuratorate not to prosecute, it is necessary to impose a disciplinary sanction of a government nature on the subject of supervision, the relevant supervision and inspection department may, in accordance with the effective judgments, rulings, and decisions of the judicial organs and the facts, nature, and circumstances as determined therein, put forward an opinion on imposing a disciplinary sanction of a government nature, and transfer the case to the adjudication department according to procedures. Where a disciplinary sanction of a government nature is required for a subject of supervision who has been investigated for administrative legal liability according to law, the case-filing procedures shall be handled according to law.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　对案情简单、经过初步核实已查清主要职务违法事实，应当追究监察对象法律责任，不再需要开展调查的，立案和移送审理可以一并报批，履行立案程序后再移送审理。

**Article 212** Where a case is simple, the main facts of duty-related illegality have been ascertained through preliminary verification, and it is necessary to investigate the legal liability of the subject of supervision without further investigation, case-filing and transfer for adjudication may be reported and approved together; after fulfilling the case-filing procedures, the case shall be transferred for adjudication.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　上级监察机关需要指定下级监察机关立案调查的，应当按规定报批，向被指定管辖的监察机关出具《指定管辖决定书》，由其办理立案手续。

**Article 213** Where a superior supervisory organ needs to designate a lower-level supervisory organ to initiate an investigation, it shall report for approval in accordance with the provisions, issue a "Decision on Designated Jurisdiction" to the designated supervisory organ, and the latter shall handle the case-filing procedures.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　批准立案后，应当由二名以上调查人员出示证件，向被调查人宣布立案决定。宣布立案决定后，应当及时向被调查人所在单位等相关组织送达《立案通知书》，并向被调查人所在单位主要负责人通报。  
对涉嫌严重职务违法或者职务犯罪的公职人员立案调查并采取留置措施的，应当按规定通知被调查人所在单位和家属，并向社会公开发布。

**Article 214** After the case-filing is approved, two or more investigators shall produce their credentials and announce the decision on case-filing to the person under investigation. After the announcement of the decision on case-filing, a "Notice of Case-filing" shall be timely served on the relevant organization such as the unit to which the person under investigation belongs, and the principal responsible person of that unit shall be notified.  
Where a public official suspected of serious duty-related illegality or duty-related crime is placed under investigation with case-filing and custodial measures are taken, the unit to which the person under investigation belongs and his/her family members shall be notified in accordance with the provisions, and the matter shall be publicly released to the society.

#### 第三节　调查

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　监察机关对已经立案的职务违法或者职务犯罪案件应当依法进行调查，收集证据查明违法犯罪事实。  
调查职务违法或者职务犯罪案件，对被调查人没有采取留置措施的，应当在立案后一年以内作出处理决定；对被调查人解除留置措施的，应当在解除留置措施后一年以内作出处理决定。案情重大、复杂的案件，经上一级监察机关批准，可以适当延长，但延长期限不得超过六个月。  
被调查人在监察机关立案调查以后逃匿的，调查期限自被调查人到案之日起重新计算。

**Article 215** The supervisory organ shall investigate cases of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime that have been filed according to law, collect evidence, and ascertain the facts of the illegal or criminal conduct.  
Where, in investigating a case of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, no custodial measure has been taken against the person under investigation, a decision on handling shall be made within one year after the case-filing; where the custodial measure against the person under investigation is lifted, a decision on handling shall be made within one year after the lifting of the custodial measure. For cases that are major or complex, the time limit may be appropriately extended with the approval of the supervisory organ at the next higher level, but the extended period shall not exceed six months.  
Where the person under investigation absconds after the supervisory organ initiates the investigation, the investigation period shall be recalculated from the date on which the person under investigation appears.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　案件立案后，监察机关主要负责人应当依照法定程序批准确定调查方案。  
监察机关应当组成调查组依法开展调查。调查工作应当严格按照批准的方案执行，不得随意扩大调查范围、变更调查对象和事项，对重要事项应当及时请示报告。调查人员在调查工作期间，未经批准不得单独接触任何涉案人员及其特定关系人，不得擅自采取调查措施。

**Article 216** After a case is filed, the principal responsible person of the supervisory organ shall approve and determine the investigation plan in accordance with statutory procedures.  
The supervisory organ shall form an investigation group to carry out the investigation according to law. The investigation work shall be strictly carried out in accordance with the approved plan; the scope of investigation shall not be expanded, and the subject and matters of investigation shall not be changed arbitrarily; important matters shall be timely reported for instructions. During the investigation period, without approval, investigators shall not contact any involved persons or their specific related persons alone, nor shall they take investigation measures without authorization.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　调查组应当将调查认定的涉嫌违法犯罪事实形成书面材料，交给被调查人核对，听取其意见。被调查人应当在书面材料上签署意见。对被调查人签署不同意见或者拒不签署意见的，调查组应当作出说明或者注明情况。对被调查人提出申辩的事实、理由和证据应当进行核实，成立的予以采纳。  
调查组对于立案调查的涉嫌行贿犯罪、介绍贿赂犯罪或者共同职务犯罪的涉案人员，在查明其涉嫌犯罪问题后，依照前款规定办理。  
对于按照本条例规定，对立案和移送审理一并报批的案件，应当在报批前履行本条第一款规定的程序。

**Article 217** The investigation group shall form written materials of the facts of the suspected illegal or criminal conduct ascertained through investigation, hand them over to the person under investigation for verification, and hear his/her opinions. The person under investigation shall sign the written materials to indicate his/her opinions. Where the person under investigation signs a dissenting opinion or refuses to sign, the investigation group shall provide an explanation or note the circumstances. The facts, reasons, and evidence presented by the person under investigation for defense shall be verified; if established, they shall be accepted.  
For persons involved who are suspected of the crime of bribing, the crime of introducing bribes, or joint duty-related crimes in a case filed for investigation, after ascertaining the suspected criminal issues, the investigation group shall handle the matter in accordance with the preceding paragraph.  
For cases where case-filing and transfer for adjudication are reported and approved together in accordance with these Regulations, the procedures provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be fulfilled before the report and approval.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　调查组在调查工作结束后应当集体讨论，形成调查报告。调查报告应当列明被调查人基本情况、问题线索来源及调查依据、调查过程，涉嫌的主要职务违法或者职务犯罪事实，被调查人的态度和认识，处置建议及法律依据，并由调查组组长以及有关人员签名。  
对调查过程中发现的重要问题和形成的意见建议，应当形成专题报告。

**Article 218** After the investigation work is completed, the investigation group shall hold a collective discussion and form an investigation report. The investigation report shall include the basic information of the person under investigation, the source of the problem clue and the basis of investigation, the investigation process, the main facts of the suspected duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, the attitude and understanding of the person under investigation, the disposal suggestions and the legal basis, and shall be signed by the head of the investigation group and the relevant persons.  
For important issues discovered and opinions and suggestions formed during the investigation, a special report shall be prepared.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　调查组对被调查人涉嫌职务犯罪拟依法移送人民检察院审查起诉的，应当起草《起诉建议书》。《起诉建议书》应当载明被调查人基本情况，调查简况，认罪认罚情况，采取监察强制措施的时间，涉嫌职务犯罪事实以及证据，对被调查人从重、从轻、减轻或者免除处罚等情节，提出对被调查人移送起诉的理由和法律依据，采取刑事强制措施的建议，并注明移送案卷数及涉案财物等内容。  
调查组应当形成被调查人到案经过及量刑情节方面的材料，包括案件来源、到案经过，自动投案、如实供述、立功等量刑情节，认罪悔罪态度、退赃、避免和减少损害结果发生等方面的情况说明及相关材料。被检举揭发的问题已被立案、查破，被检举揭发人已被采取调查措施或者刑事强制措施、起诉或者审判的，还应当附有关法律文书。

**Article 219** Where the investigation group intends to transfer a person under investigation suspected of a duty-related crime to the People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution according to law, it shall draft a "Proposal for Prosecution". The "Proposal for Prosecution" shall include the basic information of the person under investigation, a brief description of the investigation, the circumstances of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, the time when supervisory compulsory measures were taken, the facts and evidence of the suspected duty-related crime, the circumstances of heavier, lighter, mitigated, or exempted punishment for the person under investigation, the reasons and legal basis for proposing the transfer for prosecution, the proposal for adopting criminal compulsory measures, and shall indicate the number of case files transferred and the property involved in the case.  
The investigation group shall form materials on the process by which the person under investigation appeared and the circumstances of sentencing, including the source of the case, the process of appearance, the circumstances of sentencing such as voluntary surrender, truthful confession, meritorious service, the attitude of admission of guilt and repentance, restitution of ill-gotten gains, avoidance and reduction of harmful consequences, and relevant materials. If the reported or disclosed issues have been filed, investigated, and solved, and the reported or disclosed person has been subjected to investigation measures or criminal compulsory measures, prosecution, or adjudication, the relevant legal documents shall be attached.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　经调查认为被调查人构成职务违法或者职务犯罪的，应当区分不同情况提出相应处理意见，经审批将调查报告、职务违法或者职务犯罪事实材料、涉案财物报告、涉案人员处理意见等材料，连同全部证据和文书手续移送审理。  
对涉嫌职务犯罪的案件材料应当按照刑事诉讼要求单独立卷，与《起诉建议书》、涉案财物报告、同步录音录像资料及其自查报告等材料一并移送审理。  
调查全过程形成的材料应当案结卷成、事毕归档。

**Article 220** Where, after investigation, it is believed that the person under investigation has committed duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, corresponding handling opinions shall be proposed based on different circumstances, and after approval, the investigation report, materials on the facts of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, the report on property involved in the case, the handling opinions on the persons involved, and other materials, together with all evidence and documentary formalities, shall be transferred for adjudication.  
Materials of cases suspected of duty-related crimes shall be separately filed in volumes in accordance with the requirements of criminal proceedings, and shall be transferred for adjudication together with the "Proposal for Prosecution", the report on property involved in the case, the synchronous audio and video recordings and their self-inspection reports, and other materials.  
Materials generated throughout the investigation process shall be bound into case files upon conclusion and archived upon completion.

#### 第四节　审理

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　案件审理部门收到移送审理的案件后，应当审核材料是否齐全、手续是否完备。对被调查人涉嫌职务犯罪的，还应当审核相关案卷材料是否符合职务犯罪案件立卷要求，是否在调查报告中单独表述已查明的涉嫌犯罪问题，是否形成《起诉建议书》。  
经审核符合移送条件的，应当予以受理；不符合移送条件的，经审批可以暂缓受理或者不予受理，并要求承办部门补充完善材料。

**Article 221** After receiving a case transferred for adjudication, the case adjudication department shall review whether the materials are complete and the formalities are in order. Where the person under investigation is suspected of a duty-related crime, it shall also review whether the relevant case file materials meet the requirements for filing volumes of duty-related crime cases, whether the ascertained suspected criminal issues are separately stated in the investigation report, and whether a "Proposal for Prosecution" has been formed.  
If upon review the case meets the conditions for transfer, it shall be accepted. If the conditions for transfer are not met, acceptance may be suspended or refused after approval, and the undertaking department shall be required to supplement and improve the materials.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　案件审理部门受理案件后，应当成立由二人以上组成的审理组，全面审理案卷材料。  
案件审理部门对于受理的案件，应当以监察法、政务处分法、刑法、《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》等法律法规为准绳，对案件事实和证据、性质认定、程序手续、涉案财物等进行全面审理。  
案件审理部门应当强化监督制约职能，对案件严格审核把关，坚持实事求是、独立审理，依法提出审理意见。坚持调查与审理相分离的原则，案件调查人员不得参与审理。

**Article 222** After accepting a case, the case adjudication department shall form an adjudication group composed of two or more persons to comprehensively adjudicate the case file materials.  
For the accepted case, the case adjudication department shall take the Supervision Law, the Law on Administrative Discipline of Public Officials, the Criminal Law, the Criminal Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations as the criteria, and conduct a comprehensive adjudication of the case facts and evidence, the determination of the nature, the procedures and formalities, and the property involved in the case.  
The case adjudication department shall strengthen the function of supervision and restraint, strictly examine and check the case, adhere to seeking truth from facts and independent adjudication, and propose adjudication opinions according to law. The principle of separation of investigation and adjudication shall be adhered to; case investigators shall not participate in adjudication.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　审理工作应当坚持民主集中制原则，经集体审议形成审理意见。

**Article 223** Adjudication work shall adhere to the principle of democratic centralism, and adjudication opinions shall be formed through collective deliberation.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　审理工作应当在受理之日起一个月以内完成，重大、复杂案件经批准可以适当延长。

**Article 224** Adjudication work shall be completed within one month from the date of acceptance; for major or complex cases, the time limit may be appropriately extended with approval.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　案件审理部门根据案件审理情况，经审批可以与被调查人谈话，告知其在审理阶段的权利义务，核对涉嫌违法犯罪事实，听取其辩解意见，了解有关情况。与被调查人谈话应当在具备安全保障条件的场所进行，被调查人为在押的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者在看守所、监狱服刑人员的，按照[本条例第八十三条](#art-83)规定办理。谈话时，案件审理人员不得少于二人。  
具有下列情形之一的，一般应当与被调查人谈话：  
（一）对被调查人采取留置措施，拟移送起诉的；  
（二）可能存在以非法方法收集证据情形的；  
（三）被调查人对涉嫌违法犯罪事实材料签署不同意见或者拒不签署意见的；  
（四）被调查人要求向案件审理人员当面陈述的；  
（五）其他有必要与被调查人进行谈话的情形。

**Article 225** The case adjudication department may, based on the adjudication circumstances and after approval, have a conversation with the person under investigation, inform him/her of the rights and obligations during the adjudication stage, verify the facts of the suspected illegal or criminal conduct, hear his/her defense opinions, and learn about the relevant circumstances. The conversation with the person under investigation shall be conducted in a place with safety and security conditions. If the person under investigation is a criminal suspect or defendant in custody or a person serving a sentence in a detention center or prison, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 83 of these Regulations. During the conversation, there shall be no fewer than two case adjudication personnel.  
Where any of the following circumstances exists, a conversation with the person under investigation shall generally be held:  
(1) The person under investigation is subject to custodial measures and is to be transferred for prosecution;  
(2) There may be circumstances where evidence is collected by illegal methods;  
(3) The person under investigation signs a dissenting opinion on the materials of the suspected illegal or criminal conduct or refuses to sign;  
(4) The person under investigation requests to make an oral statement to the case adjudication personnel;  
(5) Other circumstances where it is necessary to have a conversation with the person under investigation.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　经审理认为主要违法犯罪事实不清、证据不足的，应当经审批将案件退回承办部门重新调查。  
具有下列情形之一，需要补充完善证据的，经审批可以退回补充调查：  
（一）部分事实不清、证据不足的；  
（二）遗漏违法犯罪事实的；  
（三）其他需要进一步查清案件事实的情形。  
案件审理部门将案件退回重新调查或者补充调查的，应当出具审核意见，写明调查事项、理由、调查方向、需要补充收集的证据及其证明作用等，连同案卷材料一并送交承办部门。  
承办部门补充调查结束后，应当经审批将补证情况报告及相关证据材料，连同案卷材料一并移送案件审理部门；对确实无法查明的事项或者无法补充的证据，应当作出书面说明。重新调查终结后，应当重新形成调查报告，依法移送审理。  
重新调查完毕移送审理的，审理期限重新计算。补充调查期间不计入审理期限。

**Article 226** Where, upon adjudication, the main facts of the illegal or criminal conduct are unclear and the evidence is insufficient, the case shall be returned to the undertaking department for reinvestigation after approval.  
Where any of the following circumstances exists and it is necessary to supplement and improve the evidence, the case may be returned for supplementary investigation after approval:  
(1) Some facts are unclear and the evidence is insufficient;  
(2) Facts of illegal or criminal conduct are omitted;  
(3) Other circumstances where the case facts need to be further clarified.  
Where the case adjudication department returns the case for reinvestigation or supplementary investigation, it shall issue a review opinion, stating the matters to be investigated, the reasons, the direction of investigation, and the evidence to be additionally collected and its probative effect, etc., and submit it together with the case file materials to the undertaking department.  
After the supplementary investigation by the undertaking department is completed, a report on the supplementary evidence and the relevant evidentiary materials shall be submitted after approval, together with the case file materials, to the case adjudication department; for matters that cannot be ascertained or evidence that cannot be supplemented, a written explanation shall be provided. After the reinvestigation is concluded, a new investigation report shall be formed and transferred for adjudication according to law.  
Where the case is transferred for adjudication after reinvestigation, the adjudication period shall be recalculated. The period of supplementary investigation shall not be counted in the adjudication period.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　审理工作结束后应当形成审理报告，载明被调查人基本情况、调查简况、涉嫌违法或者犯罪事实、被调查人态度和认识、涉案财物处置、承办部门意见、审理意见等内容，提请监察机关集体审议。  
对被调查人涉嫌职务犯罪需要追究刑事责任的，应当形成《起诉意见书》，作为审理报告附件。《起诉意见书》应当忠实于事实真象，载明被调查人基本情况，调查简况，采取监察强制措施的时间，依法查明的犯罪事实和证据，从重、从轻、减轻或者免除处罚等情节，涉案财物情况，涉嫌罪名和法律依据，采取刑事强制措施的建议，以及其他需要说明的情况。  
案件审理部门经审理认为现有证据不足以证明被调查人存在违法犯罪行为，且通过退回补充调查仍无法达到证明标准的，应当提出撤销案件的建议。

**Article 227** After the adjudication work is completed, an adjudication report shall be formed, stating the basic information of the person under investigation, a brief description of the investigation, the facts of the suspected illegality or crime, the attitude and understanding of the person under investigation, the disposal of property involved in the case, the opinion of the undertaking department, the adjudication opinion, and other contents, and shall be submitted to the supervisory organ for collective deliberation.  
Where the person under investigation is suspected of a duty-related crime and criminal liability needs to be investigated, a "Proposal for Prosecution Opinion" shall be formed as an appendix to the adjudication report. The "Proposal for Prosecution Opinion" shall be faithful to the truth of the facts, stating the basic information of the person under investigation, a brief description of the investigation, the time when supervisory compulsory measures were taken, the facts of the crime and evidence ascertained according to law, the circumstances of heavier, lighter, mitigated, or exempted punishment, the situation of property involved in the case, the suspected crime and the legal basis, the proposal for adopting criminal compulsory measures, and other matters that need to be explained.  
Where the case adjudication department, upon adjudication, believes that the existing evidence is insufficient to prove that the person under investigation has committed an illegal or criminal act, and the standard of proof still cannot be met through return for supplementary investigation, it shall propose a suggestion to dismiss the case.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　上级监察机关办理下级监察机关管辖案件的，可以经审理后按程序直接进行处置，也可以经审理形成处置意见后，交由下级监察机关办理。

**Article 228** Where a superior supervisory organ handles a case under the jurisdiction of a lower-level supervisory organ, it may, after adjudication, directly dispose of the case in accordance with procedures, or after forming a disposal opinion through adjudication, hand it over to the lower-level supervisory organ for handling.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　被指定管辖的监察机关在调查结束后应当将案件移送审理，提请监察机关集体审议。  
上级监察机关将其所管辖的案件指定管辖的，被指定管辖的下级监察机关应当按照前款规定办理后，将案件报上级监察机关依法作出政务处分决定。上级监察机关在作出决定前，应当进行审理。  
上级监察机关将下级监察机关管辖的案件指定其他下级监察机关管辖的，被指定管辖的监察机关应当按照第一款规定办理后，将案件送交有管理权限的监察机关依法作出政务处分决定。有管理权限的监察机关应当进行审理，审理意见与被指定管辖的监察机关意见不一致的，双方应当进行沟通；经沟通不能取得一致意见的，报请有权决定的上级监察机关决定。经协商，有管理权限的监察机关在被指定管辖的监察机关审理阶段可以提前阅卷，沟通了解情况。  
对于前款规定的重大、复杂案件，被指定管辖的监察机关经集体审议后将处理意见报有权决定的上级监察机关审核同意的，有管理权限的监察机关可以经集体审议后依法处置。

**Article 229** The supervisory organ designated with jurisdiction shall, after the investigation is completed, transfer the case for adjudication and submit it to the supervisory organ for collective deliberation.  
Where a superior supervisory organ designates jurisdiction over a case under its own jurisdiction, the lower-level supervisory organ designated with jurisdiction shall, after handling in accordance with the preceding paragraph, report the case to the superior supervisory organ to make a decision on disciplinary sanction of a government nature according to law. The superior supervisory organ shall conduct adjudication before making the decision.  
Where a superior supervisory organ designates jurisdiction over a case under the jurisdiction of a lower-level supervisory organ to another lower-level supervisory organ, the supervisory organ designated with jurisdiction shall, after handling in accordance with paragraph 1, send the case to the supervisory organ with management authority to make a decision on disciplinary sanction of a government nature according to law. The supervisory organ with management authority shall conduct adjudication; if its adjudication opinion differs from that of the supervisory organ designated with jurisdiction, the two parties shall communicate; if no consensus can be reached through communication, the matter shall be reported to the superior supervisory organ with the authority to decide for a decision. Upon consultation, the supervisory organ with management authority may review the case files in advance during the adjudication stage of the supervisory organ designated with jurisdiction to communicate and understand the situation.  
For major or complex cases as provided for in the preceding paragraph, where the supervisory organ designated with jurisdiction, after collective deliberation, submits the handling opinion to the superior supervisory organ with the authority to decide for review and approval, the supervisory organ with management authority may, after collective deliberation, dispose of the case according to law.

#### 第五节　处置

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　监察机关根据监督、调查结果，依据监察法、政务处分法等规定进行处置。

**Article 230** A supervisory organ shall, based on the results of supervision and investigation, take action in accordance with the provisions of the Supervision Law, the Law on Administrative Sanctions for Public Officials, and other regulations.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　监察机关对于公职人员有职务违法行为但情节较轻的，可以依法进行谈话提醒、批评教育、责令检查，或者予以诫勉。上述方式可以单独使用，也可以依据规定合并使用。  
谈话提醒、批评教育应当由监察机关相关负责人或者承办部门负责人进行，可以由被谈话提醒、批评教育人所在单位有关负责人陪同；经批准也可以委托其所在单位主要负责人进行。对谈话提醒、批评教育情况应当制作记录。  
被责令检查的公职人员应当作出书面检查并进行整改。整改情况在一定范围内通报。  
诫勉由监察机关以谈话或者书面方式进行。采取谈话方式予以诫勉的，应当由监察机关相关负责人或者承办部门负责人进行；经批准也可以委托诫勉对象所在单位主要负责人进行。对谈话情况应当制作记录。

**Article 231** Where a public official commits a work-related illegal act but the circumstances are minor, the supervisory organ may, in accordance with law, conduct a talk as a reminder, criticism and education, order a written self-examination, or issue an admonishment. The aforesaid methods may be used individually or in combination according to regulations.  
The talk as a reminder and criticism and education shall be conducted by the responsible person of the supervisory organ or the responsible person of the undertaking department, and may be accompanied by the relevant responsible person of the unit to which the person being reminded or criticized belongs; upon approval, it may also be delegated to the principal responsible person of the unit to which the person belongs. Records shall be made of the talk as a reminder and criticism and education.  
A public official ordered to conduct a written self-examination shall make a written self-examination and make corrections. The correction situation shall be notified within a certain scope.  
Admonishment shall be carried out by the supervisory organ by way of a talk or in writing. Where an admonishment is given by way of a talk, it shall be conducted by the responsible person of the supervisory organ or the responsible person of the undertaking department; upon approval, it may also be delegated to the principal responsible person of the unit to which the admonished person belongs. Records shall be made of the talk.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　对违法的公职人员依法需要给予政务处分的，应当根据情节轻重作出警告、记过、记大过、降级、撤职、开除的政务处分决定，制作政务处分决定书。

**Article 232** Where a public official who has committed an illegal act needs to be given an administrative sanction according to law, a decision on administrative sanction of warning, demerit recording, serious demerit recording, demotion, removal from office, or expulsion shall be made based on the severity of the circumstances, and a written decision on the administrative sanction shall be produced.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　监察机关应当将政务处分决定书在作出后一个月以内送达被处分人和被处分人所在机关、单位，并依法履行宣布、书面告知程序。  
政务处分决定自作出之日起生效。有关机关、单位、组织应当依法及时执行处分决定，并将执行情况向监察机关报告。处分决定应当在作出之日起一个月以内执行完毕，特殊情况下经监察机关批准可以适当延长办理期限，最迟不得超过六个月。

**Article 233** A supervisory organ shall serve the written decision on the administrative sanction on the sanctioned person and the organ or unit to which the sanctioned person belongs within one month after making the decision, and shall perform the procedures of announcement and written notification according to law.  
The administrative sanction decision shall take effect from the date of its making. The relevant organs, units, and organizations shall promptly execute the sanction decision according to law and report the execution status to the supervisory organ. The sanction decision shall be fully executed within one month from the date of its making; under special circumstances, with the approval of the supervisory organ, the execution period may be appropriately extended, but shall not exceed six months at the latest.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　监察机关对不履行或者不正确履行职责造成严重后果或者恶劣影响的领导人员，可以按照管理权限采取通报、诫勉、政务处分等方式进行问责；提出组织处理的建议。

**Article 234** Where leading personnel fail to perform or fail to properly perform their duties, resulting in serious consequences or adverse effects, the supervisory organ may, in accordance with the management authority, take accountability measures by means of notification, admonishment, administrative sanctions, etc.; and may propose organizational handling measures.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　监察机关依法向监察对象所在单位提出监察建议的，应当经审批制作监察建议书。  
监察建议书一般应当包括下列内容：  
（一）监督调查情况；  
（二）发现的主要问题及其产生的原因；  
（三）整改建议内容和要求；  
（四）整改期限和反馈整改情况的要求；  
（五）提出异议的期限和方式。

**Article 235** Where a supervisory organ, in accordance with law, issues a supervisory recommendation to the unit to which the supervisee belongs, it shall, upon approval, produce a written supervisory recommendation.  
A written supervisory recommendation shall generally include the following:  
(1) the situation of supervision and investigation;  
(2) the main problems discovered and their causes;  
(3) the content and requirements of the rectification recommendation;  
(4) the time limit for rectification and the requirements for reporting the rectification status; and  
(5) the time limit and method for raising objections.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　监察机关在研究提出监察建议过程中，应当坚持问题导向、系统观念，加强分析研判，保证监察建议质量。  
监察机关可以采取专题调研、部门会商、征求特约监察员等有关人员意见，以及与被建议单位或者其他有关方面沟通等方式，提高监察建议的针对性、可行性。

**Article 236** In the process of studying and formulating supervisory recommendations, a supervisory organ shall adhere to a problem-oriented approach and systematic thinking, strengthen analysis and judgment, and ensure the quality of the supervisory recommendations.  
A supervisory organ may adopt methods such as special investigations, departmental consultations, soliciting opinions from specially invited supervisors and other relevant personnel, and communicating with the recommended unit or other relevant parties, to enhance the pertinence and feasibility of the supervisory recommendations.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　监察机关经调查，对没有证据证明或者现有证据不足以证明被调查人存在违法犯罪行为的，应当依法撤销案件。省级以下监察机关撤销案件后，应当在七个工作日以内向上一级监察机关报送备案报告。上一级监察机关监督检查部门负责备案工作。  
省级以下监察机关拟撤销上级监察机关指定管辖或者交办案件的，应当将《撤销案件意见书》连同案卷材料，在法定调查期限到期七个工作日前报指定管辖或者交办案件的监察机关审查。对于重大、复杂案件，在法定调查期限到期十个工作日前报指定管辖或者交办案件的监察机关审查。  
指定管辖或者交办案件的监察机关由监督检查部门负责审查工作。指定管辖或者交办案件的监察机关同意撤销案件的，下级监察机关应当作出撤销案件决定，制作《撤销案件决定书》；指定管辖或者交办案件的监察机关不同意撤销案件的，下级监察机关应当执行该决定。  
监察机关对于撤销案件的决定应当向被调查人宣布，由其在《撤销案件决定书》上签名、捺指印，立即解除监察强制措施，并通知其所在单位。  
撤销案件后又发现重要事实或者有充分证据，认为被调查人有违法犯罪事实需要追究法律责任的，应当重新立案调查。

**Article 237** Where, after investigation, there is no evidence or the existing evidence is insufficient to prove that the person under investigation has committed an illegal or criminal act, the supervisory organ shall revoke the case according to law. After a supervisory organ at or below the provincial level revokes a case, it shall submit a filing report to the supervisory organ at the next higher level within seven working days. The supervision and inspection department of the supervisory organ at the next higher level shall be responsible for the filing work.  
Where a supervisory organ at or below the provincial level intends to revoke a case designated for jurisdiction or assigned by a supervisory organ at a higher level, it shall submit the "Opinion on Revocation of Case" together with the case files to the supervisory organ that designated the jurisdiction or assigned the case for review seven working days before the expiration of the statutory investigation period. For major or complex cases, it shall submit the materials ten working days before the expiration of the statutory investigation period.  
The supervisory organ that designated the jurisdiction or assigned the case shall have its supervision and inspection department responsible for the review. If the supervisory organ that designated the jurisdiction or assigned the case agrees to the revocation, the lower-level supervisory organ shall make a decision to revoke the case and produce a "Decision on Revocation of Case"; if it does not agree, the lower-level supervisory organ shall comply with that decision.  
The supervisory organ shall announce the decision to revoke the case to the person under investigation, have him/her sign and affix fingerprints on the "Decision on Revocation of Case", immediately lift the supervisory compulsory measures, and notify the unit to which he/she belongs.  
If, after the case is revoked, important facts are discovered or sufficient evidence is obtained, and it is believed that the person under investigation has committed an illegal or criminal act requiring legal liability, the case shall be re-filed for investigation.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　对于涉嫌行贿等犯罪的非监察对象，案件调查终结后依法移送起诉。综合考虑行为性质、手段、后果、时间节点、认罪悔罪态度等具体情况，对于情节较轻，经审批不予移送起诉的，应当采取批评教育、责令具结悔过等方式处置；应当给予行政处罚的，依法移送有关行政执法部门。  
对于有行贿行为的涉案单位和人员，按规定记入相关信息记录，可以作为信用评价的依据。  
对于涉案单位和人员通过行贿等非法手段取得的财物及孳息，应当依法予以没收、追缴或者责令退赔，不得没收、追缴与案件无关的财物。对于涉案单位和人员主动上交的涉案财物，应当严格核查，确系违法所得及孳息的，依法予以没收、追缴。对于违法取得的经营资格、资质、荣誉、奖励、学历学位、职称或者职务职级等其他不正当利益，应当建议有关机关、单位、组织依照法律法规及有关规定予以纠正处理。

**Article 238** For non-supervisees suspected of committing crimes such as bribery, the case shall be transferred for prosecution according to law after the conclusion of the investigation. Taking into comprehensive consideration the specific circumstances such as the nature of the act, the means, the consequences, the time point, and the attitude of admitting guilt and repentance, where the circumstances are minor and, upon approval, the case is not transferred for prosecution, measures such as criticism and education or ordering a written statement of repentance shall be taken; where an administrative penalty should be imposed, the case shall be transferred to the relevant administrative law enforcement department according to law.  
For the units and individuals involved in bribery acts, relevant information shall be recorded in accordance with regulations, which may serve as a basis for credit evaluation.  
For property and its proceeds obtained by the units and individuals involved through illegal means such as bribery, they shall be confiscated, recovered, or ordered to be returned according to law; property unrelated to the case shall not be confiscated or recovered. For property involved in the case voluntarily surrendered by the units and individuals involved, strict verification shall be conducted; if it is confirmed to be illegal gains and proceeds, it shall be confiscated or recovered according to law.
