For other illegitimate benefits obtained illegally, such as business qualifications, credentials, honors, awards, academic degrees, professional titles, or positions, the supervisory organ shall recommend that the relevant organs, units, or organizations correct and handle them in accordance with laws, regulations, and relevant provisions.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　对查封、扣押、冻结的涉嫌职务犯罪所得财物及孳息应当妥善保管，并制作《移送司法机关涉案财物清单》随案移送人民检察院。对作为证据使用的实物应当随案移送；对不宜移送的，应当将清单、照片和其他证明文件随案移送。  
对于移送人民检察院的涉案财物，价值不明的，应当在移送起诉前委托进行价格认定。在价格认定过程中，需要对涉案财物先行作出真伪鉴定或者出具技术、质量检测报告的，应当委托有关鉴定机构或者检测机构进行真伪鉴定或者技术、质量检测。  
对不属于犯罪所得但属于违法取得的财物及孳息，应当依法予以没收、追缴或者责令退赔，并出具有关法律文书。  
对经认定不属于违法所得的财物及孳息，应当及时予以返还，并办理签收手续。

**Article 239** Property and its proceeds suspected of being derived from duty crimes that have been sealed up, seized, or frozen shall be properly kept, and a "List of Property Involved in the Case Transferred to Judicial Organs" shall be prepared and transferred together with the case to the People's Procuratorate. Physical evidence used as evidence shall be transferred with the case; where it is not suitable for transfer, the list, photographs, and other supporting documents shall be transferred with the case.  
For the property involved in the case transferred to the People's Procuratorate, if the value is unclear, a price appraisal shall be commissioned before the transfer for prosecution. During the price appraisal process, if it is necessary to first conduct authenticity appraisal or issue a technical or quality inspection report on the property involved, the relevant appraisal institution or testing institution shall be commissioned to carry out authenticity appraisal or technical/quality inspection.  
For property and its proceeds that are not criminal proceeds but are illegally obtained, they shall be confiscated, recovered, or ordered to be returned according to law, and relevant legal documents shall be issued.  
For property and its proceeds that are determined not to be illegal gains, they shall be promptly returned, and receipt procedures shall be completed.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　监察机关经调查，对违法取得的财物及孳息决定追缴或者责令退赔的，可以依法要求公安、自然资源、住房城乡建设、市场监管、金融监管等部门以及银行等机构、单位予以协助。  
追缴涉案财物以追缴原物为原则，原物已经转化为其他财物的，应当追缴转化后的财物。涉案财物已被用于清偿合法债务、转让或者设置其他权利负担，善意第三人通过正常市场交易、支付合理对价，并实际取得相应权利的，不得对善意取得的财物进行追缴。  
有证据证明依法应当追缴、没收的涉案财物无法找到、被他人善意取得、价值灭失减损或者与其他合法财产混合且不可分割的，可以依法追缴、没收被调查人的其他等值财产。  
追缴或者责令退赔应当自处置决定作出之日起一个月以内执行完毕。因被调查人的原因逾期执行的除外。  
人民检察院、人民法院依法将不认定为犯罪所得的相关涉案财物退回监察机关的，监察机关应当依法处理。

**Article 240** Where, after investigation, a supervisory organ decides to recover or order the return of illegally obtained property and its proceeds, it may, in accordance with law, request assistance from public security, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, market regulation, financial regulatory departments, as well as banks and other institutions and units.  
Recovery of property involved in the case shall be based on the principle of recovering the original property; if the original property has been converted into other property, the converted property shall be recovered. Where the property involved has been used to discharge lawful debts, transferred, or encumbered with other rights, and a bona fide third party has acquired the corresponding rights through normal market transactions with reasonable consideration, the property acquired in good faith shall not be recovered.  
If there is evidence proving that the property involved that should be recovered or confiscated according to law cannot be found, has been acquired by a third party in good faith, has lost or diminished in value, or is mixed with other lawful property and inseparable, the supervisory organ may recover or confiscate other property of equivalent value belonging to the person under investigation.  
Recovery or the order to return property shall be completed within one month from the date of the disposal decision, unless delayed due to reasons attributable to the person under investigation.  
Where the People's Procuratorate or the People's Court returns to the supervisory organ property involved that is not identified as criminal proceeds, the supervisory organ shall handle it according to law.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　监察对象对监察机关作出的涉及本人的处理决定不服的，可以在收到处理决定之日起一个月以内，向作出决定的监察机关申请复审。复审机关应当依法受理，并在受理后一个月以内作出复审决定。监察对象对复审决定仍不服的，可以在收到复审决定之日起一个月以内，向上一级监察机关申请复核。复核机关应当依法受理，并在受理后二个月以内作出复核决定。  
上一级监察机关的复核决定和国家监察委员会的复审、复核决定为最终决定。

**Article 241** Where a supervisee refuses to accept a handling decision made by a supervisory organ concerning him/her, he/she may apply for a review to the supervisory organ that made the decision within one month from the date of receipt of the handling decision. The review organ shall accept the application according to law and make a review decision within one month from the date of acceptance. If the supervisee still refuses to accept the review decision, he/she may apply for a reexamination to the supervisory organ at the next higher level within one month from the date of receipt of the review decision. The reexamination organ shall accept the application according to law and make a reexamination decision within two months from the date of acceptance.  
The reexamination decision of the supervisory organ at the next higher level and the review or reexamination decisions of the National Commission of Supervision shall be final.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　复审、复核机关承办部门应当成立工作组，调阅原案卷宗，必要时可以进行调查取证。承办部门应当集体研究，提出办理意见，经审批作出复审、复核决定。决定应当送达申请人，抄送相关单位，并在一定范围内宣布。  
复审、复核期间，不停止原处理决定的执行。复审、复核机关经审查认定处理决定有错误或者不当的，应当依法撤销、变更原处理决定，或者责令原处理机关及时予以纠正。复审、复核机关经审查认定处理决定事实清楚、适用法律正确的，应当予以维持。  
坚持复审复核与调查审理分离，原案调查、审理人员不得参与复审复核。

**Article 242** The undertaking department of the review or reexamination organ shall establish a working group, access the case files of the original case, and may conduct investigation and evidence collection if necessary. The undertaking department shall conduct collective research, put forward handling opinions, and after approval, make a review or reexamination decision. The decision shall be served on the applicant, copied to relevant units, and announced within a certain scope.  
During the period of review or reexamination, the execution of the original handling decision shall not be suspended. If the review or reexamination organ determines upon examination that the original handling decision is erroneous or inappropriate, it shall revoke or modify the original handling decision according to law, or order the original decision-making organ to promptly make corrections. If the review or reexamination organ determines that the original handling decision is based on clear facts and correctly applied law, it shall uphold the decision.  
The principle of separation between review/reexamination and investigation/adjudication shall be adhered to; the investigators and adjudicators of the original case shall not participate in the review or reexamination.

#### 第六节　移送审查起诉

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　监察机关决定对涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人移送起诉的，应当出具《起诉意见书》，连同案卷材料、证据及到案经过材料等，一并移送同级人民检察院。  
监察机关案件审理部门负责与人民检察院审查起诉的衔接工作，调查、案件监督管理等部门应当予以协助。  
国家监察委员会派驻或者派出的监察机构、监察专员调查的职务犯罪案件，应当依法移送省级人民检察院审查起诉。

**Article 243** Where a supervisory organ decides to transfer a person under investigation suspected of duty crimes for prosecution, it shall issue a "Proposal for Prosecution" and transfer it together with the case files, evidence, materials on the circumstances of the person's surrender or arrest, etc., to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.  
The case adjudication department of the supervisory organ shall be responsible for the liaison with the People's Procuratorate regarding the review for prosecution; the investigation, case supervision and management, and other departments shall provide assistance.  
Duty crime cases investigated by the dispatched or accredited supervisory institutions or supervisory commissioners of the National Commission of Supervision shall be transferred to the provincial People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution according to law.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人和涉案人员符合[监察法第三十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-34)、第三十五条规定情形的，结合其案发前的一贯表现、违法犯罪行为的情节、后果和影响等因素，监察机关经综合研判和集体审议，报请上一级监察机关批准，可以在移送人民检察院时依法提出从轻、减轻或者免除处罚等从宽处罚建议。报请批准时，应当一并提供主要证据材料、忏悔反思材料。  
上级监察机关相关监督检查部门负责审查工作，重点审核拟认定的从宽处罚情形、提出的从宽处罚建议，经审批在十五个工作日以内作出批复。

**Article 244** Where a person under investigation suspected of duty crimes or an involved person falls under the circumstances specified in Articles 34 and 35 of the Supervision Law, the supervisory organ, after comprehensive evaluation and collective deliberation, taking into account factors such as his/her consistent conduct before the incident, the circumstances, consequences, and impact of the illegal or criminal act, and upon approval by the supervisory organ at the next higher level, may propose a recommendation for lenient punishment such as a lighter or mitigated penalty or exemption from punishment when transferring the case to the People's Procuratorate according to law. When submitting for approval, the main evidence materials and repentance reflection materials shall be provided together.  
The relevant supervision and inspection department of the higher-level supervisory organ shall be responsible for the review, focusing on examining the proposed circumstances for lenient punishment and the recommendation for lenient punishment, and shall make a reply within fifteen working days after approval.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人具有下列情形之一，如实交代自己主要犯罪事实的，可以认定为[监察法第三十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-34)第一项规定的自动投案，真诚悔罪悔过：  
（一）职务犯罪问题未被监察机关掌握或者监察机关正在就有关问题线索进行适当了解时，向监察机关投案的；  
（二）在监察机关谈话、函询过程中，如实交代监察机关未掌握的涉嫌职务犯罪问题的；  
（三）在初步核实阶段，尚未受到监察机关谈话时投案的；  
（四）职务犯罪问题虽被监察机关立案，但尚未受到讯问或者采取监察强制措施，向监察机关投案的；  
（五）因伤病等客观原因无法前往投案，先委托他人代为表达投案意愿，或者以书信、网络、电话、传真等方式表达投案意愿，后到监察机关接受处理的；  
（六）涉嫌职务犯罪潜逃后又投案，包括在被通缉、抓捕过程中投案的；  
（七）经查实确已准备去投案，或者正在投案途中被有关机关抓获的；  
（八）经他人规劝或者在他人陪同下投案的；  
（九）虽未向监察机关投案，但向其所在党组织、单位或者有关负责人员投案，向有关巡视巡察机构投案，以及向公安机关、人民检察院、人民法院投案的；  
（十）具有其他应当视为自动投案的情形的。  
被调查人自动投案后不能如实交代自己的主要犯罪事实，或者自动投案并如实供述自己的罪行后又翻供的，不能适用前款规定。

**Article 245** Where a person under investigation suspected of duty crimes meets any of the following circumstances and truthfully confesses his/her main criminal facts, he/she may be deemed to have voluntarily surrendered and sincerely repented and shown remorse as specified in item (1) of Article 34 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) Surrendering to the supervisory organ before the supervisory organ has knowledge of the duty crime issue or while the supervisory organ is making appropriate inquiries into the relevant leads;  
(2) During the process of talks or correspondence inquiries by the supervisory organ, truthfully confessing to suspected duty crime issues not known to the supervisory organ;  
(3) Surrendering during the preliminary verification stage before being summoned for a talk by the supervisory organ;  
(4) Although the duty crime issue has been placed on file by the supervisory organ, surrendering before being interrogated or subjected to supervisory compulsory measures;  
(5) Due to objective reasons such as illness or injury, being unable to surrender in person and first entrusting another person to express the intention to surrender, or expressing the intention to surrender by letter, internet, telephone, fax, etc., and later appearing before the supervisory organ for handling;  
(6) Surrendering after fleeing while suspected of duty crimes, including surrendering during the process of being wanted or arrested;  
(7) It is verified that the person was indeed preparing to surrender, or was apprehended by the relevant authorities while on the way to surrender;  
(8) Surrendering upon persuasion by others or accompanied by others;  
(9) Although not surrendering to the supervisory organ, surrendering to the Party organization, unit, or relevant responsible persons of the unit, surrendering to the relevant inspection and tour inspection agencies, or surrendering to the public security organ, the People's Procuratorate, or the People's Court; or  
(10) Having other circumstances that shall be deemed as voluntary surrender.  
If, after voluntary surrender, the person under investigation fails to truthfully confess his/her main criminal facts, or after voluntarily surrendering and truthfully confessing his/her crimes, later retracts the confession, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall not apply.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第三十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-34)第二项规定的积极配合调查工作，如实供述监察机关还未掌握的违法犯罪行为：  
（一）监察机关所掌握线索针对的犯罪事实不成立，在此范围外被调查人主动交代其他罪行的；  
（二）主动交代监察机关尚未掌握的犯罪事实，与监察机关已掌握的犯罪事实属不同种罪行的；  
（三）主动交代监察机关尚未掌握的犯罪事实，与监察机关已掌握的犯罪事实属同种罪行的；  
（四）监察机关掌握的证据不充分，被调查人如实交代有助于收集定案证据的。

**Article 246** Where a person under investigation suspected of duty crimes meets any of the following circumstances, he/she may be deemed to have actively cooperated with the investigation and truthfully confessed to illegal or criminal acts not yet known to the supervisory organ as specified in item (2) of Article 34 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) The criminal facts targeted by the leads known to the supervisory organ are not established, and the person under investigation voluntarily confesses to other crimes beyond that scope;  
(2) Voluntarily confessing to criminal facts not yet known to the supervisory organ, which are of a different type from the criminal facts already known to the supervisory organ;  
(3) Voluntarily confessing to criminal facts not yet known to the supervisory organ, which are of the same type as the criminal facts already known to the supervisory organ; or  
(4) The evidence held by the supervisory organ is insufficient, and the truthfully confession by the person under investigation helps in collecting evidence for the final determination of the case.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第三十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-34)第三项规定的积极退赃，减少损失：  
（一）全额退赃的；  
（二）退赃能力不足，但被调查人及其亲友在监察机关追缴赃款赃物过程中积极配合，且大部分已追缴到位的；  
（三）犯罪后主动采取措施避免损失发生，或者积极采取有效措施减少、挽回大部分损失的。

**Article 247** Where a person under investigation suspected of duty crimes meets any of the following circumstances, he/she may be deemed to have actively returned illicit money and reduced losses as specified in item (3) of Article 34 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) Returning all illicit money or property;  
(2) Having insufficient capacity to return illicit money or property, but the person under investigation and his/her relatives and friends actively cooperate in the process of the supervisory organ recovering the illicit money or property, and most of it has been recovered; or  
(3) Taking proactive measures after committing the crime to prevent losses from occurring, or actively taking effective measures to reduce or recover most of the losses.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　涉嫌职务犯罪的被调查人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第三十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-34)第四项规定的具有重大立功表现：  
（一）检举揭发他人重大犯罪行为且经查证属实的；  
（二）提供其他重大案件的重要线索且经查证属实的；  
（三）阻止他人重大犯罪活动的；  
（四）协助抓捕其他重大职务犯罪案件被调查人、重大犯罪嫌疑人（包括同案犯）的；  
（五）为国家挽回重大损失等对国家和社会有其他重大贡献的。  
前款所称重大犯罪一般是指依法可能被判处无期徒刑以上刑罚的犯罪行为；重大案件一般是指在本省、自治区、直辖市或者全国范围内有较大影响的案件；查证属实一般是指有关案件已被监察机关或者司法机关立案调查、侦查，被调查人、犯罪嫌疑人被监察机关采取监察强制措施或者被司法机关采取刑事强制措施，或者被告人被人民法院作出有罪判决，并结合案件事实、证据进行判断。  
[监察法第三十四条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-34)第四项规定的案件涉及国家重大利益，是指案件涉及国家主权和领土完整、国家安全、外交、社会稳定、经济发展等情形。

**Article 248** Where a person under investigation suspected of duty crimes meets any of the following circumstances, he/she may be deemed to have performed major meritorious service as specified in item (4) of Article 34 of the Supervision Law:  
(1) Reporting and exposing a major criminal act committed by another person, which is verified to be true;  
(2) Providing important leads for other major cases, which are verified to be true;  
(3) Preventing another person from committing a major criminal act;  
(4) Assisting in the arrest of other persons under investigation for major duty crime cases or major criminal suspects (including accomplices); or  
(5) Having made other significant contributions to the state and society, such as saving the state from major losses.  
For the purposes of the preceding paragraph, "major crime" generally refers to a criminal act that may be punishable by fixed-term imprisonment of life imprisonment or above according to law; "major case" generally refers to a case that has a relatively significant impact within the province, autonomous region, municipality directly under the Central Government, or nationwide; "verified to be true" generally refers to a situation where the relevant case has been placed on file for investigation or investigation by the supervisory organ or judicial organ, the person under investigation or criminal suspect has been subjected to supervisory compulsory measures by the supervisory organ or criminal compulsory measures by the judicial organ, or the defendant has been convicted by the People's Court, and such determination is made in combination with the facts and evidence of the case.  
The circumstances specified in item (4) of Article 34 of the Supervision Law where a case involves major national interests refer to situations where the case involves national sovereignty and territorial integrity, national security, foreign affairs, social stability, economic development, etc.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　涉嫌行贿等犯罪的涉案人员具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[监察法第三十五条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-35)规定的揭发有关被调查人职务违法犯罪行为，查证属实或者提供重要线索，有助于调查其他案件：  
（一）揭发所涉案件以外的被调查人职务犯罪行为，经查证属实的；  
（二）提供的重要线索指向具体的职务犯罪事实，对调查其他案件起到实质性推动作用的；  
（三）提供的重要线索有助于加快其他案件办理进度，或者对其他案件固定关键证据、挽回损失、追逃追赃等起到积极作用的。

**Article 249** Where an involved person suspected of committing crimes such as bribery meets any of the following circumstances, he/she may be deemed to have exposed the duty-related illegal or criminal acts of the relevant person under investigation as specified in Article 35 of the Supervision Law, which has been verified to be true, or provided important leads that assist in the investigation of other cases:  
(1) Exposing duty-related criminal acts of a person under investigation in a case other than the one involved, which has been verified to be true;  
(2) Providing important leads that point to specific duty crime facts and have a substantive effect on advancing the investigation of other cases; or  
(3) Providing important leads that help accelerate the progress of handling other cases, or play a positive role in securing key evidence, recovering losses, pursuing fugitives and recovering illicit assets in other cases.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　从宽处罚建议一般应当在移送起诉时作为《起诉意见书》内容一并提出，特殊情况下也可以在案件移送后、人民检察院提起公诉前，单独形成从宽处罚建议书移送人民检察院。对于从宽处罚建议所依据的证据材料，应当一并移送人民检察院。  
监察机关对于被调查人在调查阶段认罪认罚，但不符合监察法规定的提出从宽处罚建议条件，在移送起诉时没有提出从宽处罚建议的，应当在《起诉意见书》中写明其自愿认罪认罚的情况。

**Article 250** : Recommendations for lenient punishment shall generally be submitted together with the content of the "Opinion on Prosecution" when the case is transferred for prosecution. Under special circumstances, they may also be separately formed as a recommendation for lenient punishment and transferred to the People's Procuratorate after the case is transferred but before the People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution. The evidence materials on which the recommendation for lenient punishment is based shall also be transferred to the People's Procuratorate.  
If the supervisory organ, when the person under investigation admits guilt and accepts punishment during the investigation stage but does not meet the conditions for recommending lenient punishment as prescribed by the Supervision Law, and therefore does not make such a recommendation when transferring the case for prosecution, it shall state in the "Opinion on Prosecution" the fact that the person voluntarily admitted guilt and accepted punishment.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　监察机关一般应当在正式移送起诉十日前，向拟移送的人民检察院采取书面通知等方式预告移送事宜。监察机关发现被调查人因身体等原因存在不适宜羁押等可能影响刑事强制措施执行情形的，应当通报人民检察院；被调查人已被采取留置措施的，可以在移送起诉前依法变更为责令候查措施。对于未采取监察强制措施的案件，可以根据案件具体情况，向人民检察院提出对被调查人采取刑事强制措施的建议。

**Article 251** : The supervisory organ shall generally notify the People's Procuratorate to which the case is to be transferred in writing or by other means, notifying it of the upcoming transfer at least ten days before the formal transfer. If the supervisory organ finds that the person under investigation is unsuitable for custody due to physical conditions or other reasons that may affect the execution of criminal compulsory measures, it shall inform the People's Procuratorate; if the person under investigation has been subjected to detention measures, these measures may be converted to the measure of ordered to await investigation according to law before the case is transferred for prosecution. For cases where supervisory compulsory measures are not taken, it may, based on the specific circumstances of the case, propose to the People's Procuratorate that criminal compulsory measures be imposed on the person under investigation.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　监察机关办理的职务犯罪案件移送起诉，需要指定起诉、审判管辖的，应当与同级人民检察院协商有关程序事宜。需要由同级人民检察院的上级人民检察院指定管辖的，应当商请同级人民检察院办理指定管辖事宜。  
监察机关一般应当在移送起诉二十日前，将商请指定管辖函送交同级人民检察院。商请指定管辖函应当附案件基本情况，对于被调查人已被其他机关立案侦查的犯罪认为需要并案审查起诉的，一并进行说明。  
派驻或者派出的监察机构、监察专员调查的职务犯罪案件需要指定起诉、审判管辖的，应当报派出机关办理指定管辖手续。

**Article 252** : Where a duty crime case handled by a supervisory organ requires the designation of jurisdiction for prosecution and adjudication when transferred for prosecution, the supervisory organ shall consult with the People's Procuratorate at the same level regarding relevant procedural matters. Where it is necessary for the People's Procuratorate at a higher level than the People's Procuratorate at the same level to designate jurisdiction, the supervisory organ shall request the People's Procuratorate at the same level to handle the matter of designating jurisdiction.  
The supervisory organ shall generally submit a letter requesting the designation of jurisdiction to the People's Procuratorate at the same level twenty days before the case is transferred for prosecution. The letter requesting the designation of jurisdiction shall be accompanied by a summary of the basic circumstances of the case. If it is considered necessary to consolidate the examination and prosecution of crimes for which the person under investigation has already been placed on file for investigation by another authority, an explanation shall also be provided.  
Where a duty crime case investigated by a dispatched or delegated supervisory agency or commissioner requires the designation of jurisdiction for prosecution and adjudication, it shall report to the dispatching authority to handle the procedures for designating jurisdiction.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　上级监察机关指定下级监察机关进行调查，移送起诉时需要人民检察院依法指定管辖的，应当按规定办理有关程序事宜。

**Article 253** : Where a supervisory organ at a higher level designates a supervisory organ at a lower level to conduct an investigation, and when the case is transferred for prosecution, it is necessary for the People's Procuratorate to designate jurisdiction according to law, the relevant procedural matters shall be handled in accordance with the provisions.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　监察机关对已经移送起诉的职务犯罪案件，发现遗漏被调查人罪行需要补充移送起诉的，应当经审批出具《补充起诉意见书》，连同相关案卷材料、证据等一并移送同级人民检察院。  
对于经人民检察院指定管辖的案件需要补充移送起诉的，可以直接移送原受理移送起诉的人民检察院；需要追加犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的，应当再次商请人民检察院办理指定管辖手续。

**Article 254** : Where a supervisory organ, in respect of a duty crime case that has already been transferred for prosecution, discovers that a crime of the person under investigation has been omitted and needs to be supplemented for prosecution, it shall, after approval, issue a "Supplementary Opinion on Prosecution" and transfer it together with the relevant case files and evidence to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.  
For cases that require a supplementary prosecution and have had their jurisdiction designated by the People's Procuratorate, the supplementary prosecution may be directly submitted to the People's Procuratorate that originally received the transferred case. If additional criminal suspects or defendants need to be added, the supervisory organ shall again request the People's Procuratorate to handle the procedures for designating jurisdiction.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　对于涉嫌行贿犯罪、介绍贿赂犯罪或者共同职务犯罪等关联案件的涉案人员，移送起诉时一般应当随主案确定管辖。  
主案与关联案件由不同监察机关立案调查的，调查关联案件的监察机关在移送起诉前，应当报告或者通报调查主案的监察机关，由其统一协调案件管辖事宜。因特殊原因，关联案件不宜随主案确定管辖的，调查主案的监察机关应当及时通报和协调有关事项。

**Article 255** : For persons involved in related cases such as suspected bribery, introduction of bribes, or joint duty crimes, when the case is transferred for prosecution, the jurisdiction shall generally be determined together with the main case.  
Where the main case and the related case are placed on file for investigation by different supervisory organs, the supervisory organ investigating the related case shall, before transferring the case for prosecution, report to or notify the supervisory organ investigating the main case, which shall uniformly coordinate the jurisdiction matters. If, due to special reasons, the related case is not suitable for jurisdiction to be determined together with the main case, the supervisory organ investigating the main case shall promptly notify and coordinate the relevant matters.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　监察机关对于人民检察院在审查起诉中书面提出的下列要求应当予以配合：  
（一）认为可能存在以非法方法收集证据情形，要求监察机关对证据收集的合法性作出说明或者提供相关证明材料的；  
（二）排除非法证据后，要求监察机关另行指派调查人员重新取证的；  
（三）对物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据及勘验检查、辨认、调查实验等笔录存在疑问，要求调查人员提供获取、制作的有关情况的；  
（四）要求监察机关对案件中某些专门性问题进行鉴定，或者对勘验检查进行复验、复查的；  
（五）认为主要犯罪事实已经查清，仍有部分证据需要补充完善，要求监察机关补充提供证据的；  
（六）人民检察院依法提出的其他工作要求。

**Article 256** : The supervisory organ shall cooperate with the People's Procuratorate in respect of the following requirements raised in writing by the People's Procuratorate during the review of prosecution:  
(1) Where it is believed that there may be evidence collected by illegal means and the People's Procuratorate requests the supervisory organ to provide explanations or provide relevant evidentiary materials regarding the legality of evidence collection;  
(2) Where after excluding illegal evidence, the People's Procuratorate requests the supervisory organ to assign other investigators to re-collect evidence;  
(3) Where there are doubts about physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, electronic data, and records of inspections, examinations, identifications, investigation experiments, etc., and the People's Procuratorate requests the investigator to provide information regarding the acquisition and production;  
(4) Where the People's Procuratorate requests the supervisory organ to conduct an expert evaluation on certain specialized issues in the case, or to re-examine or re-check the inspection and examination;  
(5) Where the People's Procuratorate believes that the main facts of the crime have been ascertained but some evidence still needs to be supplemented or improved, and requests the supervisory organ to supplement and provide evidence;  
(6) Other work requirements raised by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with law.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　监察机关对于人民检察院依法退回补充调查的案件，应当向主要负责人报告，并积极开展补充调查工作。

**Article 257** : Where the People's Procuratorate returns a case to the supervisory organ for supplementary investigation according to law, the supervisory organ shall report to the principal person in charge and proactively carry out the supplementary investigation.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　对人民检察院退回补充调查的案件，经审批分别作出下列处理：  
（一）认定犯罪事实的证据不够充分的，应当在补充证据后，制作补充调查报告书，连同相关材料一并移送人民检察院审查，对无法补充完善的证据，应当作出书面情况说明并加盖监察机关或者承办部门公章；  
（二）在补充调查中发现新的同案犯或者增加、变更犯罪事实，需要追究刑事责任的，应当重新提出处理意见，移送人民检察院审查；  
（三）犯罪事实的认定出现重大变化，认为不应当追究被调查人刑事责任的，应当在补充调查期限内重新提出处理意见，将处理结果书面通知人民检察院并说明理由；  
（四）认为移送起诉的犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分的，应当说明理由，移送人民检察院依法审查。

**Article 258** : Where a case is returned by the People's Procuratorate for supplementary investigation, after approval, the following actions shall be taken respectively:  
(1) If the evidence for determining the criminal facts is insufficient, after supplementing the evidence, a supplementary investigation report shall be prepared and transferred together with the relevant materials to the People's Procuratorate for review. For evidence that cannot be supplemented or improved, a written explanation shall be made and stamped with the seal of the supervisory organ or the responsible department;  
(2) If new accomplices are discovered or criminal facts are added or changed during the supplementary investigation and criminal liability needs to be investigated, a new handling opinion shall be proposed and transferred to the People's Procuratorate for review;  
(3) If there is a significant change in the determination of criminal facts and it is believed that the person under investigation should not be investigated for criminal liability, a new handling opinion shall be proposed within the time limit for supplementary investigation, and the handling result shall be notified in writing to the People's Procuratorate with reasons given;  
(4) If it is believed that the criminal facts transferred for prosecution are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient, reasons shall be stated and the case shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate for review according to law.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　人民检察院在审查起诉过程中发现新的职务违法或者职务犯罪问题线索并移送监察机关的，监察机关应当依法处置。

**Article 259** : Where the People's Procuratorate discovers new leads on duty-related violations or duty crimes during the review of prosecution and transfers them to the supervisory organ, the supervisory organ shall handle them according to law.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　在案件审判过程中，人民检察院书面要求监察机关补充提供证据，对证据进行补正、解释，或者协助人民检察院补充侦查的，监察机关应当予以配合。监察机关不能提供有关证据材料的，应当书面说明情况。  
人民法院在审判过程中就证据收集合法性问题要求有关调查人员出庭说明情况时，监察机关应当依法予以配合。

**Article 260** : During the adjudication of a case, if the People's Procuratorate requests in writing that the supervisory organ supplement evidence, correct or explain evidence, or assist the People's Procuratorate in supplementary investigation, the supervisory organ shall cooperate.  
When the People's Court, during adjudication, requires relevant investigators to appear in court to explain the legality of evidence collection, the supervisory organ shall cooperate according to law.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　监察机关认为人民检察院不起诉决定有错误的，应当在收到不起诉决定书后三十日以内，依法向其上一级人民检察院提请复议。监察机关应当将上述情况及时向上一级监察机关书面报告。

**Article 261** : Where a supervisory organ believes that the People's Procuratorate's decision not to prosecute is erroneous, it shall, within thirty days after receiving the decision not to prosecute, request a review from the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level according to law. The supervisory organ shall promptly report the above situation in writing to the supervisory organ at the next higher level.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　对于监察机关移送起诉的案件，人民检察院作出不起诉决定，人民法院作出无罪判决，或者监察机关经人民检察院退回补充调查后不再移送起诉，涉及对被调查人已生效政务处分事实认定的，监察机关应当依法对政务处分决定进行审核。认为原政务处分决定认定事实清楚、适用法律正确的，不再改变；认为原政务处分决定确有错误或者不当的，依法予以撤销或者变更。

**Article 262** : Where, for a case transferred for prosecution by the supervisory organ, the People's Procuratorate makes a decision not to prosecute, the People's Court renders a judgment of not guilty, or the supervisory organ, after being returned by the People's Procuratorate for supplementary investigation, does not transfer the case again, and this involves the determination of facts for an effective disciplinary sanction already imposed on the person under investigation, the supervisory organ shall review the disciplinary sanction decision according to law. If it finds that the original disciplinary sanction decision was based on clear facts and correct application of law, it shall not change it; if it finds that the original disciplinary sanction decision was indeed erroneous or improper, it shall revoke or modify it according to law.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　对于贪污贿赂、失职渎职等职务犯罪案件，被调查人逃匿，在通缉一年后不能到案，或者被调查人死亡，依法应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产的，承办部门在调查终结后应当依法移送审理。  
监察机关应当经集体审议，出具《没收违法所得意见书》，连同案卷材料、证据等，一并移送人民检察院依法提出没收违法所得的申请。  
监察机关将《没收违法所得意见书》移送人民检察院后，在逃的被调查人自动投案或者被抓获的，监察机关应当及时通知人民检察院。

**Article 263** : For duty crime cases such as embezzlement, bribery, dereliction of duty, and malfeasance, if the person under investigation absconds and cannot be brought to justice after being wanted for one year, or if the person under investigation dies, and it is necessary to recover their illegal gains and other property involved in the case according to law, the responsible department shall, after the investigation is concluded, transfer the case for review according to law.  
The supervisory organ shall, after collective deliberation, issue a "Opinion on Confiscation of Illegal Gains" and transfer it together with the case files, evidence, etc., to the People's Procuratorate to file an application for confiscation of illegal gains according to law.  
After the supervisory organ transfers the "Opinion on Confiscation of Illegal Gains" to the People's Procuratorate, if the person under investigation who has absconded voluntarily surrenders or is captured, the supervisory organ shall promptly notify the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　监察机关立案调查拟适用缺席审判程序的贪污贿赂犯罪案件，应当逐级报送国家监察委员会同意。  
监察机关承办部门认为在境外的被调查人犯罪事实已经查清，证据确实、充分，依法应当追究刑事责任的，应当依法移送审理。  
监察机关应当经集体审议，出具《起诉意见书》，连同案卷材料、证据等，一并移送人民检察院审查起诉。  
在审查起诉或者缺席审判过程中，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人向监察机关自动投案或者被抓获的，监察机关应当立即通知人民检察院、人民法院。

**Article 264** : Where a supervisory organ places on file for investigation a case of embezzlement or bribery crime for which the absent trial procedure is to be applied, it shall report step by step to the National Commission of Supervision for approval.  
If the responsible department of the supervisory organ believes that the criminal facts of the person under investigation who is abroad have been fully ascertained, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and criminal liability shall be investigated according to law, it shall transfer the case for review according to law.  
The supervisory organ shall, after collective deliberation, issue a "Opinion on Prosecution" and transfer it together with the case files, evidence, etc., to the People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution.  
During the review of prosecution or absent trial, if the criminal suspect or defendant voluntarily surrenders to the supervisory organ or is captured, the supervisory organ shall immediately notify the People's Procuratorate and the People's Court.

### 第六章　反腐败国际合作

#### 第一节　工作职责和领导体制

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　国家监察委员会统筹协调与其他国家、地区、国际组织开展反腐败国际交流、合作。  
国家监察委员会组织《联合国反腐败公约》等反腐败国际条约的实施以及履约审议等工作，承担《联合国反腐败公约》司法协助中央机关有关工作。  
国家监察委员会组织协调有关单位建立集中统一、高效顺畅的反腐败国际追逃追赃和防逃协调机制，统筹协调、督促指导各级监察机关反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作，具体履行下列职责：  
（一）制定反腐败国际追逃追赃和防逃工作计划，研究工作中的重要问题；  
（二）会同有关单位开展反腐败国际追逃追赃等重大涉外案件办理工作；  
（三）办理由国家监察委员会管辖的涉外案件；  
（四）指导地方各级监察机关依法开展涉外案件办理工作；  
（五）汇总和通报全国职务犯罪外逃案件信息和追逃追赃工作信息；  
（六）建立健全反腐败国际追逃追赃和防逃合作网络；  
（七）承担监察机关开展国际刑事司法协助的主管机关职责；  
（八）承担其他与反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作相关的职责。

**Article 265** : The National Commission of Supervision shall coordinate and plan international anti-corruption exchanges and cooperation with other countries, regions, and international organizations.  
The National Commission of Supervision shall organize the implementation of the United Nations Convention against Corruption and other international anti-corruption treaties as well as the compliance review, and shall undertake the relevant work of the central authority for mutual legal assistance under the United Nations Convention against Corruption.  
The National Commission of Supervision shall organize and coordinate relevant units to establish a centralized, unified, efficient, and smooth coordination mechanism for international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery and prevention of flight, and shall coordinate, supervise, and guide the handling of foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery by supervisory organs at all levels, specifically performing the following duties:  
(1) Formulating work plans for international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery and prevention of flight, and studying important issues in the work;  
(2) Cooperating with relevant units in handling major foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery;  
(3) Handling foreign-related cases within the jurisdiction of the National Commission of Supervision;  
(4) Guiding local supervisory organs at all levels in handling foreign-related cases according to law;  
(5) Summarizing and reporting information on duty crime fugitive cases nationwide and information on fugitive repatriation and asset recovery work;  
(6) Establishing and improving the cooperation network for international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery and prevention of flight;  
(7) Undertaking the duties of the competent authority for international criminal judicial assistance carried out by supervisory organs;  
(8) Undertaking other duties related to the handling of foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　地方各级监察机关在国家监察委员会领导下，统筹协调、督促指导本地区反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作，具体履行下列职责：  
（一）落实上级监察机关关于反腐败国际追逃追赃和防逃工作部署，制定工作计划；  
（二）按照管辖权限或者上级监察机关指定管辖，办理涉外案件；  
（三）按照上级监察机关要求，协助配合其他监察机关开展涉外案件办理工作；  
（四）汇总和通报本地区职务犯罪外逃案件信息和追逃追赃工作信息；  
（五）承担本地区其他与反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作相关的职责。  
省级监察委员会应当会同有关单位，建立健全本地区反腐败国际追逃追赃和防逃协调机制。  
国家监察委员会派驻或者派出的监察机构、监察专员统筹协调、督促指导驻在单位反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作，参照第一款规定执行。

**Article 266** : Local supervisory organs at all levels, under the leadership of the National Commission of Supervision, shall coordinate, supervise, and guide the handling of foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery in their respective regions, and specifically perform the following duties:  
(1) Implementing the work arrangements of the supervisory organ at the higher level regarding international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery and prevention of flight, and formulating work plans;  
(2) Handling foreign-related cases in accordance with the jurisdictional authority or the jurisdiction designated by the supervisory organ at the higher level;  
(3) In accordance with the requirements of the supervisory organ at the higher level, assisting and cooperating with other supervisory organs in handling foreign-related cases;  
(4) Summarizing and reporting information on duty crime fugitive cases in the region and information on fugitive repatriation and asset recovery work;  
(5) Undertaking other duties related to the handling of foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery in the region.  
Provincial supervisory commissions shall, together with relevant units, establish and improve the coordination mechanism for international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery and prevention of flight in the region.  
Dispatched or delegated supervisory agencies and commissioners of the National Commission of Supervision shall coordinate, supervise, and guide the handling of foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery by the units to which they are dispatched, and shall follow the provisions of the first paragraph by reference.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　国家监察委员会国际合作局归口管理监察机关反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作。地方各级监察委员会应当明确专责部门，归口管理本地区涉外案件办理工作。  
国家监察委员会派驻或者派出的监察机构、监察专员和地方各级监察机关办理涉外案件中有关执法司法国际合作事项，应当逐级报送国家监察委员会审批。由国家监察委员会依法直接或者协调有关单位与有关国家（地区）相关机构沟通，以双方认可的方式实施。

**Article 267** : The International Cooperation Bureau of the National Commission of Supervision shall centrally manage the handling of foreign-related cases such as international anti-corruption fugitive repatriation and asset recovery by supervisory organs. Local supervisory commissions at all levels shall designate specialized departments to centrally manage the handling of foreign-related cases in their respective regions.  
Matters concerning international cooperation in law enforcement and judiciary in the handling of foreign-related cases by dispatched or delegated supervisory agencies and commissioners of the National Commission of Supervision and local supervisory organs at all levels shall be reported level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for approval. The National Commission of Supervision shall, according to law, directly communicate with relevant institutions of the relevant countries (regions) or coordinate relevant units to do so, and implement in a manner mutually recognized by both parties.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　监察机关应当建立追逃追赃和防逃工作内部联络机制。承办部门在调查过程中，发现被调查人或者重要涉案人员外逃、违法所得及其他涉案财产被转移到境外的，可以请追逃追赃部门提供工作协助。监察机关将案件移送人民检察院审查起诉后，仍有重要涉案人员外逃或者未追缴的违法所得及其他涉案财产的，应当由追逃追赃部门继续办理，或者由追逃追赃部门指定协调有关单位办理。

**Article 268** : Supervisory organs shall establish internal liaison mechanisms for fugitive repatriation and asset recovery and prevention of flight. If, during an investigation, the responsible department discovers that the person under investigation or a key person involved in the case has fled abroad, or that illegal gains and other property involved in the case have been transferred abroad, it may request the fugitive repatriation and asset recovery department to provide work assistance. After the supervisory organ transfers the case to the People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution, if there are still key persons involved who have fled abroad or illegal gains and other property involved in the case that have not been recovered, the fugitive repatriation and asset recovery department shall continue to handle the matter, or it shall designate and coordinate relevant units to handle it.

#### 第二节　国（境）内工作

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　监察机关应当将防逃工作纳入日常监督内容，督促相关机关、单位建立健全防逃责任机制。  
监察机关在监督、调查工作中，应当根据情况制定对监察对象、重要涉案人员的防逃方案，防范人员外逃和资金外流风险。监察机关应当会同同级组织人事、外事、公安、移民管理等单位健全防逃预警机制，对存在外逃风险的监察对象早发现、早报告、早处置。

**Article 269** : Supervisory organs shall incorporate the work of preventing flight into daily supervision, and urge relevant authorities and units to establish and improve the responsibility mechanism for preventing flight.  
In supervision and investigation work, supervisory organs shall, based on the circumstances, formulate plans for preventing flight of supervisory targets and key persons involved in the case, to guard against the risk of persons fleeing abroad and funds flowing out. Supervisory organs shall, together with the organizational and personnel, foreign affairs, public security, immigration management, and other units at the same level, improve the early warning mechanism for preventing flight, and achieve early detection, early reporting, and early handling of supervisory targets with flight risks.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　监察机关应当加强与同级人民银行、公安等单位的沟通协作，推动预防、打击利用离岸公司和地下钱庄等向境外转移违法所得及其他涉案财产，对涉及职务违法和职务犯罪的行为依法进行调查。

**Article 270** The supervisory organ shall strengthen communication and cooperation with the People's Bank of China, public security organs, and other units at the same level to promote the prevention and crackdown on the transfer of illegal gains and other property involved in the case to overseas through offshore companies, underground banks, and other means, and shall conduct investigations according to law into acts involving duty-related illegal acts and duty-related crimes.

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　国家监察委员会派驻或者派出的监察机构、监察专员和地方各级监察委员会发现监察对象出逃、失踪、出走，或者违法所得及其他涉案财产被转移至境外的，应当在二十四小时以内将有关信息逐级报送至国家监察委员会国际合作局，并迅速开展相关工作。

**Article 271** Where the dispatched or assigned supervisory organs or supervisors of the National Commission of Supervision, or local supervisory commissions at all levels discover that a supervisee has absconded, disappeared, or left, or that illegal gains or other property involved in the case have been transferred overseas, they shall, within twenty-four hours, report the relevant information level by level to the International Cooperation Bureau of the National Commission of Supervision and promptly carry out relevant work.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　监察机关追逃追赃部门统一接收巡视巡察机构、审计机关、执法机关、司法机关等单位移交的外逃信息。  
监察机关对涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪的外逃人员，应当明确承办部门，建立案件档案。

**Article 272** The fugitive pursuit and asset recovery department of the supervisory organ shall uniformly receive information on fugitives transferred by inspection and patrol agencies, audit organs, law enforcement organs, judicial organs, and other units.  
The supervisory organ shall, for fugitives suspected of duty-related illegal acts and duty-related crimes, designate the department in charge and establish case files.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　监察机关应当依法全面收集外逃人员涉嫌职务违法和职务犯罪证据。

**Article 273** The supervisory organ shall, according to law, comprehensively collect evidence of fugitives suspected of duty-related illegal acts and duty-related crimes.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　开展反腐败国际追逃追赃等涉外案件办理工作，应当把思想教育贯穿始终，落实宽严相济刑事政策，依法适用认罪认罚从宽制度，促使外逃人员回国投案或者配合调查、主动退赃。开展相关工作，应当尊重所在国家（地区）的法律规定。

**Article 274** In carrying out the handling of foreign-related cases such as international fugitive pursuit and asset recovery for anti-corruption, ideological education shall be permeated throughout the entire process, the criminal policy of combining leniency with severity shall be implemented, and the system of leniency for pleading guilty and accepting punishment shall be applied according to law, so as to encourage fugitives to return to China and surrender themselves, or to cooperate with investigations and voluntarily return illicit proceeds. When carrying out relevant work, the laws and regulations of the country (region) where the fugitive is located shall be respected.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　外逃人员归案、违法所得及其他涉案财产被追缴后，承办案件的监察机关应当将情况逐级报送国家监察委员会国际合作局。监察机关应当依法对涉案人员和违法所得及其他涉案财产作出处置，或者请有关单位依法处置。对不需要继续采取相关措施的，应当及时解除或者撤销。

**Article 275** After a fugitive is brought to justice and illegal gains and other property involved in the case are recovered, the supervisory organ handling the case shall report the situation level by level to the International Cooperation Bureau of the National Commission of Supervision. The supervisory organ shall, according to law, dispose of the persons involved and the illegal gains and other property involved in the case, or request relevant units to dispose of them according to law. Where relevant measures are no longer necessary, they shall be promptly lifted or revoked.

#### 第三节　对外合作

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　监察机关对依法应当留置或者已经决定留置的外逃人员，需要申请发布国际刑警组织红色通报的，应当逐级报送国家监察委员会审核。国家监察委员会审核后，依法通过公安部向国际刑警组织提出申请。  
需要延期、暂停、撤销红色通报的，申请发布红色通报的监察机关应当逐级报送国家监察委员会审核，由国家监察委员会依法通过公安部联系国际刑警组织办理。

**Article 276** Where a supervisory organ needs to apply for the issuance of an INTERPOL Red Notice against a fugitive who shall be detained according to law or has been decided to be detained, it shall report the matter level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for review. After review, the National Commission of Supervision shall, according to law, submit the application to INTERPOL through the Ministry of Public Security.  
Where an extension, suspension, or cancellation of a Red Notice is needed, the supervisory organ that applied for the issuance of the Red Notice shall report the matter level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for review, and the National Commission of Supervision shall, according to law, contact INTERPOL through the Ministry of Public Security for handling.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　地方各级监察机关通过引渡方式办理相关涉外案件的，应当按照《中华人民共和国引渡法》、相关双边及多边国际条约等规定准备引渡请求书及相关材料，逐级报送国家监察委员会审核。由国家监察委员会依法通过外交途径向外国提出引渡请求。

**Article 277** Where local supervisory commissions at all levels handle relevant foreign-related cases through extradition, they shall prepare extradition requests and related materials in accordance with the Extradition Law of the People's Republic of China, relevant bilateral and multilateral international treaties, and other provisions, and submit them level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for review. The National Commission of Supervision shall, according to law, submit extradition requests to foreign states through diplomatic channels.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　地方各级监察机关通过刑事司法协助方式办理相关涉外案件的，应当按照《中华人民共和国国际刑事司法协助法》（以下简称国际刑事司法协助法）、相关双边及多边国际条约等规定准备刑事司法协助请求书及相关材料，逐级报送国家监察委员会审核。由国家监察委员会依法直接或者通过对外联系机关等渠道，向外国提出刑事司法协助请求。  
国家监察委员会收到外国提出的刑事司法协助请求书及所附材料，经审查认为符合有关规定的，作出决定并交由省级监察机关执行，或者转交其他有关主管机关。省级监察机关应当立即执行，或者交由下级监察机关执行，并将执行结果或者妨碍执行的情形及时报送国家监察委员会。在执行过程中，需要依法采取查询、调取、查封、扣押、冻结等措施或者需要返还涉案财物的，根据我国法律规定和国家监察委员会的执行决定办理有关法律手续。

**Article 278** Where local supervisory commissions at all levels handle relevant foreign-related cases through criminal judicial assistance, they shall prepare criminal judicial assistance requests and related materials in accordance with the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law of the People's Republic of China (hereinafter referred to as the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law), relevant bilateral and multilateral international treaties, and other provisions, and submit them level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for review. The National Commission of Supervision shall, according to law, submit criminal judicial assistance requests to foreign states directly or through channels such as the foreign liaison authority.  
Where the National Commission of Supervision receives a criminal judicial assistance request and accompanying materials submitted by a foreign state, and upon review finds that they comply with relevant provisions, it shall make a decision and refer the request to a provincial-level supervisory commission for execution, or transfer it to another competent authority. The provincial-level supervisory commission shall execute it immediately, or refer it to a lower-level supervisory commission for execution, and shall promptly report the execution result or any circumstances hindering execution to the National Commission of Supervision. During the execution, if measures such as inquiry, retrieval, sealing, seizure, or freezing need to be taken according to law, or if property involved in the case needs to be returned, the relevant legal formalities shall be handled in accordance with China's legal provisions and the execution decision of the National Commission of Supervision.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　地方各级监察机关通过执法合作方式办理相关涉外案件的，应当将合作事项及相关材料逐级报送国家监察委员会审核。由国家监察委员会依法直接或者协调有关单位，向有关国家（地区）相关机构提交并开展合作。

**Article 279** Where local supervisory commissions at all levels handle relevant foreign-related cases through law enforcement cooperation, they shall report the cooperation matters and related materials level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for review. The National Commission of Supervision shall, according to law, directly submit them to the relevant institutions of the concerned country (region) or coordinate relevant units to do so, and conduct cooperation.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　地方各级监察机关通过境外追诉方式办理相关涉外案件的，应当提供外逃人员相关违法线索和证据，逐级报送国家监察委员会审核。由国家监察委员会按照国际刑事司法协助法等规定直接或者协调有关单位向有关国家（地区）相关机构提交，请其依法对外逃人员调查、起诉、审判，移管被判刑人或者遣返外逃人员。

**Article 280** Where local supervisory commissions at all levels handle relevant foreign-related cases through overseas prosecution, they shall provide relevant clues and evidence of the fugitive's illegal acts and submit them level by level to the National Commission of Supervision for review. The National Commission of Supervision shall, in accordance with the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law and other provisions, directly submit them to the relevant institutions of the concerned country (region) or coordinate relevant units to do so, and request them to investigate, prosecute, and adjudicate the fugitive according to law, to transfer the sentenced person, or to repatriate the fugitive.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　监察机关对依法应当追缴的境外违法所得及其他涉案财产，应当责令涉案人员以合法方式退赔。涉案人员拒不退赔的，可以依法通过下列方式追缴：  
（一）在开展引渡等追逃合作时，随附请求有关国家（地区）移交相关违法所得及其他涉案财产；  
（二）依法启动违法所得没收程序，申请由人民法院对相关违法所得及其他涉案财产作出没收裁定，请有关国家（地区）承认和执行，并予以返还；  
（三）请有关国家（地区）依法追缴相关违法所得及其他涉案财产，并予以返还；  
（四）通过其他合法方式追缴。

**Article 281** Where illegal gains and other property involved in the case located overseas shall be recovered according to law, the supervisory organ shall order the persons involved to return or make restitution in a lawful manner. If the persons involved refuse to do so, the supervisory organ may recover them according to law through the following methods:  
(1) When conducting cooperation in fugitive pursuit such as extradition, additionally request the relevant country (region) to transfer the relevant illegal gains and other property involved in the case;  
(2) Initiate the confiscation procedure for illegal gains according to law, apply to the People's Court for a confiscation order concerning the relevant illegal gains and other property involved in the case, and request the relevant country (region) to recognize and enforce the order and return the property;  
(3) Request the relevant country (region) to recover the relevant illegal gains and other property involved in the case according to law and return them;  
(4) Recover through other lawful methods.

### 第七章　对监察机关和监察人员的监督

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　监察机关和监察人员必须自觉坚持党的领导，在党组织的管理、监督下开展工作，依法接受本级人民代表大会及其常务委员会的监督，接受民主监督、司法监督、社会监督、舆论监督，加强内部监督制约机制建设，确保权力受到严格的约束和监督。

**Article 282** A supervisory organ and its personnel must consciously uphold the leadership of the Party, carry out work under the management and supervision of Party organizations, accept supervision by the People's Congress at the corresponding level and its standing committee according to law, accept democratic supervision, judicial supervision, social supervision, and public opinion supervision, strengthen the construction of internal supervision and restriction mechanisms, and ensure that power is strictly constrained and supervised.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　各级监察委员会应当按照[监察法第六十条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-60)第二款规定，由主要负责人在本级人民代表大会常务委员会全体会议上报告专项工作。  
在报告专项工作前，应当与本级人民代表大会有关专门委员会、常务委员会有关工作机构沟通协商，并配合开展专题调查研究等工作。各级人民代表大会常务委员会审议专项工作报告时，本级监察委员会应当根据要求派出负责人列席相关会议，听取意见。  
各级监察委员会应当认真研究处理本级人民代表大会常务委员会反馈的审议意见，并按照要求书面报告研究处理情况。本级人民代表大会常务委员会对专项工作报告作出决议的，监察委员会应当在决议规定的期限内，将执行决议的情况向其报告。

**Article 283** The supervisory commission at each level shall, in accordance with the provisions of Article 60, paragraph 2 of the Supervision Law, have its principal responsible person report on special work at a plenary meeting of the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level.  
Before reporting on special work, it shall communicate and consult with the relevant special committees of the People's Congress and the relevant working bodies of the standing committee, and cooperate in conducting special research and other work. When the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level deliberates the special work report, the supervisory commission at the corresponding level shall, as required, send a responsible person to attend the relevant meeting to hear opinions.  
The supervisory commission at each level shall seriously study and handle the deliberation opinions fed back by the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level, and report in writing on the study and handling situation as required. If the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level adopts a resolution on the special work report, the supervisory commission shall report to it on the implementation of the resolution within the time limit specified in the resolution.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　各级监察委员会应当积极接受、配合本级人民代表大会常务委员会组织的执法检查。对本级人民代表大会常务委员会的执法检查报告，应当认真研究处理，并向其报告研究处理情况。本级人民代表大会常务委员会对执法检查报告作出决议的，监察委员会应当在决议规定的期限内，将执行决议的情况向其报告。

**Article 284** The supervisory commission at each level shall actively accept and cooperate with law enforcement inspections organized by the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level. It shall seriously study and handle the law enforcement inspection reports of the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level, and report to it on the study and handling situation. If the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level adopts a resolution on the law enforcement inspection report, the supervisory commission shall report to it on the implementation of the resolution within the time limit specified in the resolution.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　各级监察委员会在本级人民代表大会常务委员会会议审议与监察工作有关的议案和报告时，应当派相关负责人到会听取意见，回答询问。  
本级人民代表大会常务委员会就与监察工作有关的重大问题，召开全体会议、联组会议或者分组会议进行专题询问的，监察委员会负责人应当到会，听取意见，回答询问。各级监察委员会应当及时向本级人民代表大会常务委员会提交专题询问中提出意见的研究处理情况报告。  
监察机关对依法交由监察机关答复的质询案应当按照要求进行答复。口头答复的，由监察机关主要负责人或者委派相关负责人到会答复。书面答复的，由监察机关主要负责人签署。

**Article 285** When the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level deliberates motions and reports related to supervision work, the supervisory commission at each level shall send relevant responsible persons to attend the meeting to hear opinions and answer inquiries.  
If the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level holds a plenary meeting, joint group meeting, or group meeting to conduct special inquiries on major issues related to supervision work, the responsible person of the supervisory commission shall attend the meeting, hear opinions, and answer inquiries. The supervisory commission at each level shall promptly submit to the standing committee of the People's Congress at the corresponding level a report on the study and handling of opinions raised in the special inquiries.  
For interpellation cases that are assigned to the supervisory organ for reply according to law, the supervisory organ shall make a reply as required. If the reply is made orally, the principal responsible person of the supervisory organ or a responsible person designated by the principal responsible person shall attend the meeting to reply. If the reply is made in writing, it shall be signed by the principal responsible person of the supervisory organ.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　各级监察机关应当通过互联网政务媒体、报刊、广播、电视等途径，向社会及时准确公开下列监察工作信息：  
（一）监察法规；  
（二）依法应当向社会公开的案件调查信息；  
（三）检举控告地址、电话、网站等信息；  
（四）其他依法应当公开的信息。

**Article 286** The supervisory organ at each level shall, through channels such as internet government media, newspapers, radio, and television, promptly and accurately disclose to the public the following supervision work information:  
(1) Supervision regulations;  
(2) Case investigation information that shall be disclosed to the public according to law;  
(3) Information on the address, telephone, and website for reporting and accusing;  
(4) Other information that shall be disclosed according to law.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　各级监察机关可以根据工作需要，按程序选聘特约监察员履行监督、咨询等职责。特约监察员名单应当向社会公布。  
监察机关应当为特约监察员依法开展工作提供必要条件和便利。特约监察员对监察机关提出的意见、建议和批评，监察机关应当及时办理和反馈。

**Article 287** The supervisory organ at each level may, based on work needs, select and appoint special supervisors according to procedures to perform duties such as supervision and consultation. The list of special supervisors shall be disclosed to the public.  
The supervisory organ shall provide necessary conditions and convenience for special supervisors to carry out work according to law. The supervisory organ shall promptly handle and give feedback on opinions, suggestions, and criticisms raised by special supervisors.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　监察机关实行严格的人员准入制度，严把政治关、品行关、能力关、作风关、廉洁关。监察人员必须忠诚坚定、担当尽责、遵纪守法、清正廉洁。

**Article 288** A supervisory organ shall implement a strict personnel access system, and strictly examine political integrity, moral conduct, competence, work style, and probity. Supervisory personnel must be loyal and steadfast, responsible and dedicated, abide by discipline and law, and be upright and clean.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　监察机关应当建立信访举报、监督检查、调查、案件监督管理、案件审理等部门相互协调制约的工作机制。  
监督检查和调查部门实行分工协作、相互制约。监督检查部门主要负责联系地区、部门、单位的日常监督检查和对涉嫌一般违法问题线索处置。调查部门主要负责对涉嫌严重职务违法和职务犯罪问题线索进行初步核实和立案调查。  
案件监督管理部门负责对监督检查、调查工作全过程进行监督管理，做好线索管理、组织协调、监督检查、督促办理、统计分析等工作。案件监督管理部门发现监察人员在监督检查、调查中有违规办案行为的，及时督促整改；涉嫌违纪违法的，根据管理权限移交相关部门处理。

**Article 289** A supervisory organ shall establish a working mechanism for mutual coordination and restriction among departments such as letters and visits reporting, supervision and inspection, investigation, case supervision and management, and case adjudication.  
The supervision and inspection department and the investigation department shall work with division of labor and cooperation, and mutual restriction. The supervision and inspection department is mainly responsible for daily supervision and inspection of the areas, departments, and units under its contact and for handling clues of suspected ordinary illegal acts. The investigation department is mainly responsible for conducting preliminary verification and case filing investigation into clues of suspected serious duty-related illegal acts and duty crimes.  
The case supervision and management department is responsible for supervising and managing the entire process of supervision, inspection, and investigation work, and for handling clue management, organization and coordination, supervision and inspection, urging handling, statistical analysis, and other work. If the case supervision and management department discovers that supervisory personnel have irregular case-handling behaviors during supervision, inspection, or investigation, it shall promptly urge rectification; if the behaviors are suspected of violating discipline or law, the department shall transfer the case to the relevant department for handling according to the management authority.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　监察机关应当部署使用覆盖信访举报、线索处置、监督检查、调查、案件审理等监察执法主要流程和关键要素的监察一体化工作平台，推动数字技术融入监察工作，通过信息化手段加强对监督、调查、处置工作的全过程监督管理。

**Article 290** A supervisory organ shall deploy and use an integrated supervision work platform that covers the main processes and key elements of supervision law enforcement, such as letters and visits reporting, clue handling, supervision and inspection, investigation, and case adjudication, promote the integration of digital technology into supervision work, and strengthen the whole-process supervision and management of supervision, investigation, and disposition work through information technology means.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　监察机关应当对监察权运行关键环节进行经常性监督检查，适时开展专项督查。案件监督管理、案件审理等部门应当按照各自职责，对问题线索处置、调查措施使用、涉案财物管理等进行监督检查，建立常态化、全覆盖的案件质量评查机制。

**Article 291** A supervisory organ shall conduct regular supervision and inspection on key links in the operation of supervisory power, and carry out special inspections when appropriate. The case supervision and management department, the case adjudication department, and other departments shall, according to their respective duties, supervise and inspect the handling of problem clues, the use of investigation measures, and the management of property involved in the case, and establish a normalized and full-coverage case quality evaluation mechanism.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　监察机关应当加强对监察人员执行职务和遵纪守法情况的监督，按照管理权限依法对监察人员涉嫌违法犯罪问题进行调查处置。

**Article 292** A supervisory organ shall strengthen supervision over the performance of duties and compliance with discipline and law by supervisory personnel, and investigate and handle cases of suspected violations of law or crime by supervisory personnel according to the management authority and in accordance with law.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　监察机关及其监督检查、调查部门负责人应当定期检查调查期间的录音录像、谈话笔录、讯问笔录、询问笔录、涉案财物登记资料，加强对调查全过程的监督，发现问题及时纠正并报告。  
对谈话、讯问和询问的同步录音录像，应当重点检查是否存在以下情形：  
（一）以暴力、威胁等非法方法收集证据；  
（二）未保证被调查人的饮食和必要的休息时间；  
（三）谈话笔录、讯问笔录、询问笔录记载的起止时间与谈话、讯问、询问录音录像资料反映的起止时间不一致；  
（四）谈话笔录、讯问笔录、询问笔录与谈话、讯问、询问录音录像资料内容存在实质性差异。

**Article 293** : The supervisory organs and the heads of their supervision and inspection departments and investigation departments shall regularly inspect the audio and video recordings, conversation records, interrogation records, inquiry records, and registration materials of the property involved in the case during the investigation period, strengthen full-process supervision over the investigation, and promptly rectify any problems found and report them.  
For the synchronous audio and video recordings of conversations, interrogations, and inquiries, the focus shall be on checking whether the following circumstances exist:  
(1) Collecting evidence by illegal methods such as violence or threats;  
(2) Failing to ensure the respondent's meals and necessary rest time;  
(3) The start and end times recorded in the conversation records, interrogation records, or inquiry records are inconsistent with the start and end times reflected in the audio and video recordings of the conversations, interrogations, or inquiries;  
(4) There are material differences between the contents of the conversation records, interrogation records, or inquiry records and the contents of the audio and video recordings of the conversations, interrogations, or inquiries.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　对监察人员打听案情、过问案件、说情干预的，办理监察事项的监察人员应当及时向上级负责人报告。有关情况应当登记备案。  
发现办理监察事项的监察人员未经批准接触被调查人、涉案人员及其特定关系人，或者存在交往情形的，知情的监察人员应当及时向上级负责人报告。有关情况应当登记备案。

**Article 294** : Where a supervisory personnel inquires about case details, interferes with cases, or intercedes for others, the supervisory personnel handling supervision matters shall promptly report to the superior person in charge. The relevant circumstances shall be recorded and filed.  
If it is discovered that the supervisory personnel handling supervision matters have contacted the respondent, persons involved in the case, or their specific related persons without approval, or have engaged in social interactions with them, the supervisory personnel who are aware of such circumstances shall promptly report to the superior person in charge. The relevant circumstances shall be recorded and filed.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　办理监察事项的监察人员有[监察法第六十七条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-67)所列情形之一的，应当自行提出回避；没有自行提出回避的，监察机关应当依法决定其回避，监察对象、检举人及其他有关人员也有权要求其回避。  
选用借调人员、看护人员、调查场所，应当严格执行回避制度。

**Article 295** : Where the supervisory personnel handling supervision matters fall under any of the circumstances listed in Article 67 of the Supervision Law, they shall voluntarily propose recusal; if they fail to do so, the supervisory organ shall decide on their recusal according to law, and the target of supervision, the accuser, and other relevant persons also have the right to request their recusal.  
When selecting seconded personnel, custodial personnel, and investigation venues, the recusal system shall be strictly implemented.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　监察人员自行提出回避，或者监察对象、检举人及其他有关人员要求监察人员回避的，应当书面或者口头提出，并说明理由。口头提出的，应当形成记录。  
监察机关主要负责人的回避，由上级监察机关主要负责人决定；其他监察人员的回避，由本级监察机关主要负责人决定。

**Article 296** : Where a supervisory personnel voluntarily proposes recusal, or the target of supervision, the accuser, or other relevant persons request the recusal of a supervisory personnel, such proposal or request shall be made in writing or orally, and the reasons shall be stated. If made orally, a record shall be made.  
The recusal of the principal leading person of a supervisory organ shall be decided by the principal leading person of the supervisory organ at the next higher level; the recusal of other supervisory personnel shall be decided by the principal leading person of the supervisory organ at the same level.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　上级监察机关应当通过专项检查、业务考评、开展复查等方式，强化对下级监察机关及监察人员执行职务和遵纪守法情况的监督。

**Article 297** : Supervisory organs at higher levels shall, through special inspections, performance evaluations, reviews, and other means, strengthen supervision over the performance of duties and compliance with laws and discipline by lower-level supervisory organs and their personnel.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　监察机关应当对监察人员有计划地进行政治、理论和业务培训。培训应当坚持理论联系实际、按需施教、讲求实效，突出政治机关特色，建设忠诚干净担当、敢于善于斗争的高素质专业化监察队伍，全面提高监察工作规范化法治化正规化水平。

**Article 298** : Supervisory organs shall conduct political, theoretical, and professional training for supervisory personnel in a planned manner. The training shall adhere to the principle of integrating theory with practice, teaching according to needs, emphasizing practical results, highlighting the characteristics of political organs, and building a high-quality professional supervision team that is loyal, clean, responsible, and brave and adept at struggle, so as to comprehensively improve the standardization, legalization, and regularization of supervision work.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　监察机关应当严格执行保密制度，控制监察事项知悉范围和时间。监察人员不准私自留存、隐匿、查阅、摘抄、复制、携带问题线索和涉案资料，严禁泄露监察工作秘密。  
监察机关应当建立健全检举控告保密制度，对检举控告人的姓名（单位名称）、工作单位、住址、电话和邮箱等有关情况以及检举控告内容必须严格保密。

**Article 299** : Supervisory organs shall strictly implement the confidentiality system and control the scope and duration of knowledge of supervision matters. Supervisory personnel shall not privately keep, conceal, consult, extract, copy, or carry problem clues and case-related materials, and it is strictly forbidden to disclose the secrets of supervision work.  
Supervisory organs shall establish and improve the confidentiality system for reports and accusations, and must strictly keep confidential the name (or name of the entity), work unit, address, telephone number, email address, and other relevant information of the reporter or accuser, as well as the contents of the report or accusation.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　监察机关涉密人员离岗离职后，应当遵守脱密期管理规定，严格履行保密义务，不得泄露相关秘密。

**Article 300** : After leaving their posts or resigning, the personnel of supervisory organs who have access to secrets shall comply with the management provisions on the declassification period, strictly fulfill their confidentiality obligations, and shall not disclose relevant secrets.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　监察人员离任三年以内，不得从事与监察和司法工作相关联且可能发生利益冲突的职业。  
监察人员离任后，不得担任原任职监察机关办理案件的诉讼代理人或者辩护人，但是作为当事人的监护人或者近亲属代理诉讼或者进行辩护的除外。

**Article 301** : Within three years of leaving office, supervisory personnel shall not engage in professions related to supervision and judicial work that may give rise to a conflict of interest.  
After leaving office, supervisory personnel shall not serve as litigation agents or defenders in cases handled by the supervisory organ where they previously worked, except where they act as guardians or close relatives of a party to conduct litigation or defense.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　监察人员应当严格遵守有关规范领导干部配偶、子女及其配偶经商办企业行为的规定。

**Article 302** : Supervisory personnel shall strictly comply with the provisions on regulating leading cadres' spouses, children, and their spouses engaging in business activities.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　监察机关及其工作人员在履行职责过程中应当依法保护企业产权和自主经营权，严禁利用职权非法干扰企业生产经营。需要企业经营者协助调查的，应当依法保障其人身权利、财产权利和其他合法权益，避免或者尽量减少对涉案企业正常生产经营活动的影响。  
监察机关查封、扣押、冻结以及追缴涉案财物，应当严格区分企业财产与经营者个人财产，被调查人个人财产与家庭成员财产，违法所得、其他涉案财产与合法财产。  
查封经营性涉案财物，企业继续使用对该涉案财物价值无重大影响的，可以允许其使用。对于按规定不应交由企业保管使用的涉案财物，监察机关应当采取合理的保管保值措施。对于正在运营或者正在用于科技创新、产品研发的设备和技术资料等，一般不予查封、扣押，确需调取违法犯罪证据的，可以采取拍照、复制等方式。

**Article 303** : Supervisory organs and their staff shall, in the course of performing their duties, protect the property rights and autonomous operation rights of enterprises according to law, and it is strictly forbidden to abuse their authority to illegally interfere with the production and operation of enterprises. If it is necessary for business operators to assist in investigations, their personal rights, property rights, and other lawful rights and interests shall be guaranteed according to law, and the impact on the normal production and operation of the enterprise involved shall be avoided or minimized.  
When seizing, impounding, freezing, or recovering the property involved in a case, supervisory organs shall strictly distinguish between enterprise property and the operator's personal property, between the respondent's personal property and family members' property, and between illegal gains and other relevant property and lawful property.  
Where operational property involved in a case is seized, if continued use by the enterprise does not significantly affect the value of such property, the enterprise may be permitted to use it. For property involved that should not be handed over to the enterprise for custody and use according to regulations, the supervisory organ shall adopt reasonable measures for custody and preservation. For equipment and technical materials that are currently in operation or being used for technological innovation and product development, seizure or impoundment shall generally not be applied; if it is indeed necessary to obtain evidence of illegal or criminal acts, methods such as photographing and copying may be adopted.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　监察机关根据已经掌握的事实及证据，发现涉嫌严重职务违法或者职务犯罪的监察人员可能实施下列行为之一的，经依法审批，可以在具备安全保障条件的场所对其采取禁闭措施：  
（一）继续实施违法犯罪行为的；  
（二）为被调查人或者涉案人员通风报信等泄露监察工作秘密的；  
（三）威胁、恐吓、蓄意报复举报人、控告人、被害人、证人、鉴定人等相关人员的；  
（四）其他可能造成更为严重的后果或者恶劣影响的行为。

**Article 304** : Where a supervisory organ, based on the facts and evidence already obtained, finds that a supervisory personnel suspected of serious duty-related violations or crimes may commit any of the following acts, it may, after approval according to law, impose confinement measures on such personnel in a venue with safety and security conditions:  
(1) Continuing to commit illegal or criminal acts;  
(2) Tipping off the respondent or persons involved in the case, thereby disclosing the secrets of supervision work;  
(3) Threatening, intimidating, or deliberately retaliating against reporters, accusers, victims, witnesses, expert evaluators, and other relevant persons;  
(4) Other acts that may cause more serious consequences or adverse effects.

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　采取禁闭措施时，调查人员不得少于二人，应当向被禁闭人员宣布《禁闭决定书》，告知被禁闭人员权利义务，要求其在《禁闭决定书》上签名、捺指印。被禁闭人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
禁闭的期限不得超过七日，自向被禁闭人员宣布之日起算。

**Article 305** : When imposing confinement measures, there shall be no fewer than two investigators. They shall announce the "Confinement Decision" to the confined person, inform the confined person of their rights and obligations, and require them to sign and fingerprint on the "Confinement Decision." If the confined person refuses to sign or fingerprint, the investigators shall note it on the document.  
The duration of confinement shall not exceed seven days, calculated from the date of announcement to the confined person.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　采取禁闭措施后，应当在二十四小时以内通知被禁闭人员所在单位和家属。当面通知的，由有关人员在《禁闭通知书》上签名。无法当面通知的，可以先以电话等方式通知，并通过邮寄、转交等方式送达《禁闭通知书》，要求有关人员在《禁闭通知书》上签名。有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
因可能伪造、隐匿、毁灭证据，干扰证人作证或者串供等有碍调查情形而不宜通知的，应当按规定报批，记录在案。有碍调查的情形消失后，应当立即通知被禁闭人员所在单位和家属。

**Article 306** : After imposing confinement measures, the unit and family members of the confined person shall be notified within 24 hours. If notification is made in person, the relevant person shall sign on the "Confinement Notice." If it is not possible to notify in person, notification may first be made by telephone or other means, and the "Confinement Notice" shall be served by mail, delivery, or other means, requiring the relevant person to sign on the "Confinement Notice." If the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigators shall note it on the document.  
If it is inappropriate to notify due to circumstances that may hinder the investigation, such as possible forgery, concealment, or destruction of evidence, interference with witness testimony, or collusion, the matter shall be reported for approval as per regulations and recorded. After the circumstances hindering the investigation disappear, the unit and family members of the confined person shall be notified immediately.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条　对被禁闭人员不需要继续采取禁闭措施的，应当按规定报批后解除禁闭或者变更为责令候查措施。禁闭期满的，应当按规定报批后予以解除。  
解除禁闭措施的，调查人员应当向被禁闭人员宣布解除禁闭措施的决定，由其在《解除禁闭决定书》上签名、捺指印；变更为责令候查措施的，应当向被禁闭人员宣布变更为责令候查措施的决定，由其在《变更禁闭决定书》上签名、捺指印。被禁闭人员拒绝签名、捺指印的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。  
解除禁闭措施或者变更为责令候查措施的，应当及时通知被禁闭人员所在单位和家属、申请人。调查人员应当与交接人办理交接手续，并由其在《解除禁闭通知书》或者《变更禁闭通知书》上签名。无法通知或者有关人员拒绝签名的，调查人员应当在文书上记明。不得因办理交接手续延迟解除或者变更禁闭措施。  
在禁闭期满前，对被禁闭人员采取管护、留置措施的，按照本条例关于采取管护、留置措施的规定执行。

**Article 307** : Where confinement measures no longer need to be maintained for the confined person, the measures shall be released or converted to the measure of ordering to await investigation after reporting for approval as per regulations. Upon expiration of the confinement period, the confinement shall be released after reporting for approval as per regulations.  
When releasing the confinement measures, the investigators shall announce the decision to release the confinement measures to the confined person, and require them to sign and fingerprint on the "Decision on Release from Confinement"; when converting to the measure of ordering to await investigation, the investigators shall announce the decision to convert to the measure of ordering to await investigation to the confined person, and require them to sign and fingerprint on the "Decision on Change of Confinement." If the confined person refuses to sign or fingerprint, the investigators shall note it on the document.  
When releasing confinement measures or converting to the measure of ordering to await investigation, the unit and family members of the confined person and the applicant shall be promptly notified. The investigators shall go through the handover procedures with the person receiving the handover, and require them to sign on the "Release from Confinement Notice" or "Change of Confinement Notice." If it is impossible to notify or the relevant person refuses to sign, the investigators shall note it on the document. The release or change of confinement measures shall not be delayed due to the handover procedures.  
Before the expiration of the confinement period, if custody or detention measures are imposed on the confined person, the matter shall be executed in accordance with the provisions of these Regulations on the imposition of custody and detention measures.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条　被调查人及其近亲属、利害关系人认为监察机关及其工作人员存在[监察法第六十九条](../../宪法与国家机构/法律及决定/中华人民共和国监察法.md#art-69)第一款规定的有关情形，向监察机关提出申诉的，由监察机关案件监督管理部门依法受理。监察机关应当自受理申诉之日起一个月以内作出处理决定。  
前款规定的利害关系人，是指与有关涉案财产存在利害关系的自然人、法人或者其他组织。

**Article 308** : Where the respondent, their close relatives, or interested parties believe that a supervisory organ or its staff has the circumstances specified in Article 69, paragraph 1 of the Supervision Law, and submit a petition to the supervisory organ, the petition shall be accepted according to law by the case supervision and management department of the supervisory organ. The supervisory organ shall make a handling decision within one month from the date of accepting the petition.  
The "interested parties" as mentioned in the preceding paragraph refer to natural persons, legal persons, or other organizations that have a stake in the relevant property involved in the case.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条　监察机关案件监督管理部门受理申诉后，应当组织成立核查组，对申诉反映的问题进行核实。根据工作需要，核查组可以调阅相关措施文书等材料，听取申诉人意见和承办部门工作人员的情况说明。案件监督管理部门应当集体研究，提出办理意见，经审批作出决定。

**Article 309** : After accepting a petition, the case supervision and management department of the supervisory organ shall organize the establishment of a verification team to verify the issues reflected in the petition. According to work needs, the verification team may consult relevant measure documents and other materials, and hear the opinions of the petitioner and the explanations of the staff of the handling department. The case supervision and management department shall conduct collective research, put forward handling opinions, and make a decision after approval.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条　监察机关应当自申诉处理决定作出之日起七日以内，向申诉人送达申诉处理决定书，要求其在申诉处理决定书上签名。申诉人拒绝签名的，工作人员应当在文书上记明。

**Article 310** : The supervisory organ shall, within seven days from the date of making the handling decision on the petition, serve the petition handling decision letter to the petitioner, and require the petitioner to sign on the petition handling decision letter. If the petitioner refuses to sign, the staff shall note it on the document.
