<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条　申诉人对申诉处理决定不服的，可以自收到申诉处理决定书之日起一个月以内向上一级监察机关申请复查。上一级监察机关应当进行核实，并自收到复查申请之日起二个月以内作出处理决定。

**Article 311** : If a petitioner is dissatisfied with the petition handling decision, they may apply for a review to the supervisory organ at the next higher level within one month from the date of receiving the petition handling decision letter. The supervisory organ at the next higher level shall conduct verification and make a handling decision within two months from the date of receiving the review application.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条　监察机关应当加强留置场所管理和监督工作，依法规范管理、使用留置场所。  
留置场所应当建立健全保密、消防、医疗、防疫、餐饮及安保等方面安全制度，制定突发事件处置预案，采取安全防范措施，严格落实安全工作责任制。  
发生被强制到案人员、被管护人员、被留置人员或者被禁闭人员死亡、伤残、脱逃等办案安全事故、事件的，应当及时做好处置、处理工作。相关情况应当立即报告监察机关主要负责人，并在二十四小时以内逐级上报至国家监察委员会。

**Article 312** : Supervisory organs shall strengthen the management and supervision of detention places, and standardize the management and use of detention places according to law.  
Detention places shall establish and improve safety systems for confidentiality, fire protection, medical care, epidemic prevention, catering, and security, formulate emergency response plans, adopt safety prevention measures, and strictly implement the safety work responsibility system.  
If safety accidents or incidents occur during case handling, such as the death, injury, or escape of persons compelled to appear, persons under custody, persons under detention, or confined persons, the disposal and handling work shall be carried out promptly. The relevant circumstances shall be immediately reported to the principal leading person of the supervisory organ, and reported level by level to the National Commission of Supervision within 24 hours.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条　监察机关在维护监督执法调查工作纪律方面失职失责的，依法追究责任。监察人员涉嫌严重职务违法、职务犯罪或者对案件处置出现重大失误的，既应当追究直接责任，还应当严肃追究负有责任的领导人员责任。  
监察机关应当建立办案质量责任制，对滥用职权、失职失责造成严重后果的，实行终身责任追究。

**Article 313** : Where a supervisory organ fails to perform its duties or is derelict in maintaining discipline in supervision, law enforcement, and investigation, liability shall be pursued according to law. Where a supervisor is suspected of serious duty-related illegality or duty-related crime, or commits a major error in handling a case, both direct liability shall be pursued and the liability of the responsible leading personnel shall be seriously pursued.  
A supervisory organ shall establish a case-handling quality responsibility system, and for those who abuse their powers or are derelict in duties causing serious consequences, lifelong liability shall be pursued.

### 第八章　法律责任

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条　有关单位拒不执行监察机关依法作出的下列处理决定的，应当由其主管部门、上级机关责令改正，对单位给予通报批评，对负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员依法给予处理：  
（一）政务处分决定；  
（二）问责决定；  
（三）谈话提醒、批评教育、责令检查，或者予以诫勉的决定；  
（四）采取调查措施的决定；  
（五）复审、复核决定；  
（六）监察机关依法作出的其他处理决定。

**Article 314** : Where a relevant unit refuses to execute any of the following handling decisions lawfully made by a supervisory organ, the competent department or the superior organ shall order it to make corrections, circulate a notice of criticism against the unit, and handle the responsible leading personnel and the directly responsible personnel according to law:  
(1) a decision on a disciplinary sanction for government affairs;  
(2) a decision on accountability;  
(3) a decision on interview reminders, criticism education, ordering a check, or admonishment;  
(4) a decision on taking investigative measures;  
(5) a decision on review or re-examination;  
(6) other handling decisions lawfully made by the supervisory organ.

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条　监察对象对控告人、申诉人、批评人、检举人、证人、监察人员进行打击、压制等报复陷害的，监察机关应当依法给予政务处分。构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 315** : Where a subject of supervision retaliates against or oppresses an accuser, appellant, critic, informant, witness, or supervisor, or commits other acts of retaliation, the supervisory organ shall impose a disciplinary sanction for government affairs according to law. If a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条　控告人、检举人、证人采取捏造事实、伪造材料等方式诬告陷害的，监察机关应当依法给予政务处分，或者移送有关机关处理。构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。  
监察人员因依法履行职责遭受不实举报、诬告陷害、侮辱诽谤，致使名誉受到损害的，监察机关应当会同有关部门及时澄清事实，消除不良影响，并依法追究相关单位或者个人的责任。

**Article 316** : Where an accuser, informant, or witness fabricates facts or forges materials to make false accusations, the supervisory organ shall impose a disciplinary sanction for government affairs according to law, or transfer the matter to the relevant organ for handling. If a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.  
Where a supervisor suffers damage to reputation due to false reports, false accusations, insults, or defamation arising from lawfully performing duties, the supervisory organ shall, together with the relevant department, promptly clarify the facts, eliminate adverse effects, and pursue the liability of the relevant unit or individual according to law.

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条　监察机关应当建立健全办案安全责任制。承办部门主要负责人和调查组组长是调查安全第一责任人。调查组应当指定专人担任安全员。  
地方各级监察机关履行管理、监督职责不力发生严重办案安全事故、事件的，或者办案中存在严重违规违纪违法行为的，省级监察机关主要负责人应当按规定向国家监察委员会作出检讨，并予以通报、严肃追责问责。  
案件监督管理部门应当对办案安全责任制落实情况组织经常性检查和不定期抽查，发现问题及时报告并督促整改。

**Article 317** : A supervisory organ shall establish and improve a case-handling safety responsibility system. The principal responsible person of the undertaking department and the head of the investigation team shall be the first persons responsible for investigation safety. The investigation team shall designate a specific person as the safety officer.  
Where a local supervisory organ at any level, due to failure to perform management and supervision duties, causes a serious case-handling safety accident or incident, or commits serious violations of rules, discipline, or law in case handling, the principal responsible person of the provincial-level supervisory organ shall make a self-criticism to the National Commission of Supervision as prescribed, and shall be notified of the matter, with serious accountability and liability pursuit.  
The case supervision and management department shall organize regular inspections and irregular spot checks on the implementation of the case-handling safety responsibility system, promptly report any problems found, and urge rectification.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条　监察人员在履行职责中有下列行为之一的，依法严肃处理；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任：  
（一）贪污贿赂、徇私舞弊的；  
（二）不履行或者不正确履行监督职责，应当发现的问题没有发现，或者发现问题不报告、不处置，造成严重影响的；  
（三）未经批准、授权处置问题线索，发现重大案情隐瞒不报，或者私自留存、处理涉案材料的；  
（四）利用职权或者职务上的影响干预调查工作的；  
（五）违法窃取、泄露调查工作信息，或者泄露举报事项、举报受理情况以及举报人信息的；  
（六）对被调查人或者涉案人员等逼供、诱供，或者侮辱、打骂、虐待、体罚或者变相体罚的；  
（七）违反规定处置查封、扣押、冻结的财物的；  
（八）违反规定导致发生办案安全事故、事件，或者发生安全事故、事件后隐瞒不报、报告失实、处置不当的；  
（九）违反规定采取强制到案、责令候查、管护、留置或者禁闭措施，或者法定期限届满，不予以解除或者变更的；  
（十）违反规定采取技术调查、限制出境措施，或者不按规定解除技术调查、限制出境措施的；  
（十一）利用职权非法干扰企业生产经营或者侵害企业经营者人身权利、财产权利和其他合法权益的；  
（十二）其他职务违法和职务犯罪行为。

**Article 318** : Where a supervisor commits any of the following acts in performing duties, he or she shall be seriously dealt with according to law; if a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law:  
(1) engaging in corruption, bribery, favoritism, or malpractice;  
(2) failing to perform or correctly perform supervision duties, failing to discover problems that should have been discovered, or failing to report or handle problems discovered, causing serious impact;  
(3) disposing of problem leads without approval or authorization, concealing major case situations without reporting, or privately retaining or handling case-related materials;  
(4) using his or her powers or the influence of his or her position to interfere with investigation work;  
(5) unlawfully stealing or disclosing investigation information, or disclosing reporting matters, the acceptance of reports, or the identities of informants;  
(6) extorting confessions or inducing confessions from the person under investigation or involved persons, or insulting, beating, abusing, physically punishing, or covertly physically punishing them;  
(7) disposing of sealed, seized, or frozen property in violation of regulations;  
(8) causing a case-handling safety accident or incident due to violation of regulations, or concealing, making false reports, or improperly handling such accident or incident after its occurrence;  
(9) taking measures of compulsory appearance, ordered awaiting investigation, custodial care, detention, or confinement in violation of regulations, or failing to lift or change such measures after the expiration of the statutory time limit;  
(10) taking technical investigation measures or exit restriction measures in violation of regulations, or failing to lift technical investigation measures or exit restriction measures as prescribed;  
(11) unlawfully interfering with the production or operation of enterprises or infringing upon the personal rights, property rights, or other lawful rights and interests of business operators by using his or her powers;  
(12) other acts of duty-related illegality or duty-related crime.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条　对监察人员在履行职责中存在违法行为的，可以根据情节轻重，依法进行谈话提醒、批评教育、责令检查、诫勉，或者给予政务处分。构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 319** : Where a supervisor has unlawful acts in performing duties, depending on the seriousness of the circumstances, he or she may be subjected to interview reminders, criticism and education, ordered to make a check, admonishment, or be given a disciplinary sanction for government affairs according to law. If a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条　监察机关及其工作人员在行使职权时，具有下列情形之一的，受害人可以申请国家赔偿：  
（一）违法采取管护、禁闭措施，或者依照法定条件和程序采取管护、禁闭措施，但是管护时间、禁闭时间超过法定时限，其后决定撤销案件的；  
（二）采取留置措施后，决定撤销案件的；  
（三）违法没收、追缴或者违法查封、扣押、冻结财物造成损害的；  
（四）违法行使职权，造成被调查人、涉案人员或者证人身体伤害或者死亡的；  
（五）其他侵犯公民、法人和其他组织合法权益造成损害的。  
受害人死亡的，其继承人和其他有扶养关系的亲属有权要求赔偿；受害的法人或者其他组织终止的，其权利承受人有权要求赔偿。

**Article 320** : Where a supervisory organ or its staff member has any of the following circumstances when exercising its functions, the victim may apply for state compensation:  
(1) unlawfully taking custodial care or confinement measures, or taking such measures in accordance with statutory conditions and procedures but exceeding the statutory time limit for custodial care or confinement, and subsequently deciding to dismiss the case;  
(2) deciding to dismiss the case after taking detention measures;  
(3) unlawfully confiscating, recovering, sealing, seizing, or freezing property, causing damage;  
(4) unlawfully exercising functions, causing bodily injury or death to the person under investigation, involved persons, or witnesses;  
(5) other acts infringing upon the lawful rights and interests of citizens, legal persons, or other organizations, causing damage.  
Where the victim dies, his or her successors and other family members who have a dependency relationship shall have the right to claim compensation; where the aggrieved legal person or other organization is terminated, its successor in interest shall have the right to claim compensation.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条　监察机关及其工作人员违法行使职权侵犯公民、法人和其他组织的合法权益造成损害的，该机关为赔偿义务机关。申请赔偿应当向赔偿义务机关提出，由该机关负责复审复核工作的部门受理。  
赔偿以支付赔偿金为主要方式。能够返还财产或者恢复原状的，予以返还财产或者恢复原状。

**Article 321** : Where a supervisory organ or its staff member unlawfully exercises powers and infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of citizens, legal persons, or other organizations, causing damage, that organ shall be the compensation obligor. An application for compensation shall be submitted to the compensation obligor and shall be accepted by the department of that organ responsible for re-examination and review.  
Compensation shall be mainly in the form of monetary payment. Where it is possible to return property or restore it to its original state, the property shall be returned or restored to its original state.

### 第九章　附则

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　本条例所称监察机关，包括各级监察委员会及其派驻或者派出监察机构、监察专员，以及再派出的监察机构、监察专员。

**Article 322** : For the purposes of these Regulations, the term "supervisory organ" includes the supervisory commissions at all levels, their dispatched or detached supervisory organs and supervisory commissioners, as well as supervisory organs and supervisory commissioners that are further dispatched or detached.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条　本条例所称严重职务违法，是指根据监察机关已经掌握的事实及证据，被调查人涉嫌的职务违法行为情节严重，可能被给予撤职以上政务处分。  
本条例所称重大职务犯罪、重大贪污贿赂等职务犯罪，是指具有下列情形之一的职务犯罪：  
（一）案情重大、复杂，涉及国家利益、重大公共利益或者犯罪行为致使公共财产、国家和人民利益遭受特别重大损失的；  
（二）被调查人可能被判处十年有期徒刑以上刑罚的；  
（三）案件在全国或者本省、自治区、直辖市范围内有较大影响的。

**Article 323** : For the purposes of these Regulations, the term "serious duty-related illegality" refers to a situation where, based on the facts and evidence already obtained by the supervisory organ, the duty-related illegal act suspected of being committed by the person under investigation is serious and may result in a disciplinary sanction for government affairs of removal from office or above.  
For the purposes of these Regulations, the term "major duty-related crime" or major crime such as serious corruption or bribery refers to a duty-related crime that has any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the case is major and complex, involves national interests or major public interests, or the criminal act causes particularly heavy losses to public property, the state, or the interests of the people;  
(2) the person under investigation may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of 10 years or more;  
(3) the case has a significant impact nationwide or within the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　本条例所称同种罪行和不同种罪行，应当以罪名区分，但属选择性罪名或者在法律、事实上密切关联的犯罪，应当认定为同种罪行。

**Article 324** : For the purposes of these Regulations, the terms "crimes of the same kind" and "crimes of different kinds" shall be distinguished by the charge, except that crimes which are selective charges or are closely related in law or fact shall be deemed crimes of the same kind.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　本条例所称近亲属，是指夫、妻、父、母、子、女、同胞兄弟姊妹。

**Article 325** : For the purposes of these Regulations, the term "close relative" refers to a spouse, parent, child, or sibling of the same parents.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　本条例所称以上、以下、以内，包括本级、本数。

**Article 326** : For the purposes of these Regulations, the terms "or above," "or below," and "within" shall include the indicated level or number itself.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　期间以时、日、月、年计算，期间开始的时和日不算在期间以内。本条例另有规定的除外。  
按照年、月计算期间的，到期月的对应日为期间的最后一日；没有对应日的，月末日为期间的最后一日。  
期间的最后一日是节假日的，以节假日结束的次日为期间的最后一日。但被调查人被采取责令候查、管护、留置或者禁闭措施的期间应当至期满之日为止，不得因节假日而延长。

**Article 327** : A period shall be calculated in hours, days, months, or years. The hour and day from which the period begins shall not be counted within the period, unless otherwise provided in these Regulations.  
Where a period is calculated in years or months, the corresponding day of the due month shall be the last day of the period; if there is no corresponding day, the last day of the month shall be the last day of the period.  
Where the last day of a period falls on a holiday, the day following the end of the holiday shall be the last day of the period. However, a period during which the person under investigation is subjected to measures of ordered awaiting investigation, custodial care, detention, or confinement shall run until the expiry date, and shall not be extended due to holidays.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　本条例由国家监察委员会负责解释。

**Article 328** : These Regulations shall be interpreted by the National Commission of Supervision.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　本条例自发布之日起施行。

**Article 329** : These Regulations shall come into force as of the date of promulgation.
